   2040           break;
   2041         }
   2042         current = current.name_next;
   2043       }
   2044     }
   2045   },
   2046   lookupNode(parent, name) {
   2047     var errCode = FS.mayLookup(parent);
   2048     if (errCode) {
   2049       throw new FS.ErrnoError(errCode);
   2050     }
   2051     var hash = FS.hashName(parent.id, name);
   2052     for (var node = FS.nameTable[hash]; node; node = node.name_next) {
   2053       var nodeName = node.name;
   2054       if (node.parent.id === parent.id && nodeName === name) {
   2055         return node;
   2056       }
   2057     }
   2058     // if we failed to find it in the cache, call into the VFS
   2059     return FS.lookup(parent, name);
   2060   },
   2061   createNode(parent, name, mode, rdev) {
   2062     var node = new FS.FSNode(parent, name, mode, rdev);
   2063     FS.hashAddNode(node);
   2064     return node;
   2065   },
   2066   destroyNode(node) {
   2067     FS.hashRemoveNode(node);
   2068   },
   2069   isRoot(node) {
   2070     return node === node.parent;
   2071   },
   2072   isMountpoint(node) {
   2073     return !!node.mounted;
   2074   },
   2075   isFile(mode) {
   2076     return (mode & 61440) === 32768;
   2077   },
   2078   isDir(mode) {
   2079     return (mode & 61440) === 16384;
   2080   },
   2081   isLink(mode) {
   2082     return (mode & 61440) === 40960;
   2083   },
   2084   isChrdev(mode) {
   2085     return (mode & 61440) === 8192;
   2086   },
   2087   isBlkdev(mode) {
   2088     return (mode & 61440) === 24576;
   2089   },
   2090   isFIFO(mode) {
   2091     return (mode & 61440) === 4096;
   2092   },
   2093   isSocket(mode) {
   2094     return (mode & 49152) === 49152;
   2095   },
   2096   flagsToPermissionString(flag) {
   2097     var perms = [ "r", "w", "rw" ][flag & 3];
   2098     if ((flag & 512)) {
   2099       perms += "w";
   2100     }
   2101     return perms;
   2102   },
   2103   nodePermissions(node, perms) {
   2104     if (FS.ignorePermissions) {
   2105       return 0;
   2106     }
   2107     // return 0 if any user, group or owner bits are set.
   2108     if (perms.includes("r") && !(node.mode & 292)) {
   2109       return 2;
   2110     }
   2111     if (perms.includes("w") && !(node.mode & 146)) {
   2112       return 2;
   2113     }
   2114     if (perms.includes("x") && !(node.mode & 73)) {
   2115       return 2;
   2116     }
   2117     return 0;
   2118   },
   2119   mayLookup(dir) {
   2120     if (!FS.isDir(dir.mode)) return 54;
   2121     var errCode = FS.nodePermissions(dir, "x");
   2122     if (errCode) return errCode;
   2123     if (!dir.node_ops.lookup) return 2;
   2124     return 0;
   2125   },
   2126   mayCreate(dir, name) {
   2127     if (!FS.isDir(dir.mode)) {
   2128       return 54;
   2129     }
   2130     try {
   2131       var node = FS.lookupNode(dir, name);
   2132       return 20;
   2133     } catch (e) {}
   2134     return FS.nodePermissions(dir, "wx");
   2135   },
   2136   mayDelete(dir, name, isdir) {
   2137     var node;
   2138     try {
   2139       node = FS.lookupNode(dir, name);
   2140     } catch (e) {
   2141       return e.errno;
   2142     }
   2143     var errCode = FS.nodePermissions(dir, "wx");
   2144     if (errCode) {
   2145       return errCode;
   2146     }
   2147     if (isdir) {
   2148       if (!FS.isDir(node.mode)) {
   2149         return 54;
   2150       }
   2151       if (FS.isRoot(node) || FS.getPath(node) === FS.cwd()) {
   2152         return 10;
   2153       }
   2154     } else if (FS.isDir(node.mode)) {
   2155       return 31;
   2156     }
   2157     return 0;
   2158   },
   2159   mayOpen(node, flags) {
   2160     if (!node) {
   2161       return 44;
   2162     }
   2163     if (FS.isLink(node.mode)) {
   2164       return 32;
   2165     }
   2166     var mode = FS.flagsToPermissionString(flags);
   2167     if (FS.isDir(node.mode)) {
   2168       // opening for write
   2169       // TODO: check for O_SEARCH? (== search for dir only)
   2170       if (mode !== "r" || (flags & (512 | 64))) {
   2171         return 31;
   2172       }
   2173     }
   2174     return FS.nodePermissions(node, mode);
   2175   },
   2176   checkOpExists(op, err) {
   2177     if (!op) {
   2178       throw new FS.ErrnoError(err);
   2179     }
   2180     return op;
   2181   },
   2182   MAX_OPEN_FDS: 4096,
   2183   nextfd() {
   2184     for (var fd = 0; fd <= FS.MAX_OPEN_FDS; fd++) {
   2185       if (!FS.streams[fd]) {
   2186         return fd;
   2187       }
   2188     }
   2189     throw new FS.ErrnoError(33);
   2190   },
   2191   getStreamChecked(fd) {
   2192     var stream = FS.getStream(fd);
   2193     if (!stream) {
   2194       throw new FS.ErrnoError(8);
   2195     }
   2196     return stream;
   2197   },
   2198   getStream: fd => FS.streams[fd],
   2199   createStream(stream, fd = -1) {
   2200     // clone it, so we can return an instance of FSStream
   2201     stream = Object.assign(new FS.FSStream, stream);
   2202     if (fd == -1) {
   2203       fd = FS.nextfd();
   2204     }
   2205     stream.fd = fd;
   2206     FS.streams[fd] = stream;
   2207     return stream;
   2208   },
   2209   closeStream(fd) {
   2210     FS.streams[fd] = null;
   2211   },
   2212   dupStream(origStream, fd = -1) {
   2213     var stream = FS.createStream(origStream, fd);
   2214     stream.stream_ops?.dup?.(stream);
   2215     return stream;
   2216   },
   2217   doSetAttr(stream, node, attr) {
   2218     var setattr = stream?.stream_ops.setattr;
   2219     var arg = setattr ? stream : node;
   2220     setattr ??= node.node_ops.setattr;
   2221     FS.checkOpExists(setattr, 63);
   2222     setattr(arg, attr);
   2223   },
   2224   chrdev_stream_ops: {
   2225     open(stream) {
   2226       var device = FS.getDevice(stream.node.rdev);
   2227       // override node's stream ops with the device's
   2228       stream.stream_ops = device.stream_ops;
   2229       // forward the open call
   2230       stream.stream_ops.open?.(stream);
   2231     },
   2232     llseek() {
   2233       throw new FS.ErrnoError(70);
   2234     }
   2235   },
   2236   major: dev => ((dev) >> 8),
   2237   minor: dev => ((dev) & 255),
   2238   makedev: (ma, mi) => ((ma) << 8 | (mi)),
   2239   registerDevice(dev, ops) {
   2240     FS.devices[dev] = {
   2241       stream_ops: ops
   2242     };
   2243   },
   2244   getDevice: dev => FS.devices[dev],
   2245   getMounts(mount) {
   2246     var mounts = [];
   2247     var check = [ mount ];
   2248     while (check.length) {
   2249       var m = check.pop();
   2250       mounts.push(m);
   2251       check.push(...m.mounts);
   2252     }
   2253     return mounts;
   2254   },
   2255   syncfs(populate, callback) {
   2256     if (typeof populate == "function") {
   2257       callback = populate;
   2258       populate = false;
   2259     }
   2260     FS.syncFSRequests++;
   2261     if (FS.syncFSRequests > 1) {
   2262       err(`warning: ${FS.syncFSRequests} FS.syncfs operations in flight at once, probably just doing extra work`);
   2263     }
   2264     var mounts = FS.getMounts(FS.root.mount);
   2265     var completed = 0;
   2266     function doCallback(errCode) {
   2267       FS.syncFSRequests--;
   2268       return callback(errCode);
   2269     }
   2270     function done(errCode) {
   2271       if (errCode) {
   2272         if (!done.errored) {
   2273           done.errored = true;
   2274           return doCallback(errCode);
   2275         }
   2276         return;
   2277       }
   2278       if (++completed >= mounts.length) {
   2279         doCallback(null);
   2280       }
   2281     }
   2282     // sync all mounts
   2283     for (var mount of mounts) {
   2284       if (mount.type.syncfs) {
   2285         mount.type.syncfs(mount, populate, done);
   2286       } else {
   2287         done(null);
   2288       }
   2289     }
   2290   },
   2291   mount(type, opts, mountpoint) {
   2292     var root = mountpoint === "/";
   2293     var pseudo = !mountpoint;
   2294     var node;
   2295     if (root && FS.root) {
   2296       throw new FS.ErrnoError(10);
   2297     } else if (!root && !pseudo) {
   2298       var lookup = FS.lookupPath(mountpoint, {
   2299         follow_mount: false
   2300       });
   2301       mountpoint = lookup.path;
   2302       // use the absolute path
   2303       node = lookup.node;
   2304       if (FS.isMountpoint(node)) {
   2305         throw new FS.ErrnoError(10);
   2306       }
   2307       if (!FS.isDir(node.mode)) {
   2308         throw new FS.ErrnoError(54);
   2309       }
   2310     }
   2311     var mount = {
   2312       type,
   2313       opts,
   2314       mountpoint,
   2315       mounts: []
   2316     };
   2317     // create a root node for the fs
   2318     var mountRoot = type.mount(mount);
   2319     mountRoot.mount = mount;
   2320     mount.root = mountRoot;
   2321     if (root) {
   2322       FS.root = mountRoot;
   2323     } else if (node) {
   2324       // set as a mountpoint
   2325       node.mounted = mount;
   2326       // add the new mount to the current mount's children
   2327       if (node.mount) {
   2328         node.mount.mounts.push(mount);
   2329       }
   2330     }
   2331     return mountRoot;
   2332   },
   2333   unmount(mountpoint) {
   2334     var lookup = FS.lookupPath(mountpoint, {
   2335       follow_mount: false
   2336     });
   2337     if (!FS.isMountpoint(lookup.node)) {
   2338       throw new FS.ErrnoError(28);
   2339     }
   2340     // destroy the nodes for this mount, and all its child mounts
   2341     var node = lookup.node;
   2342     var mount = node.mounted;
   2343     var mounts = FS.getMounts(mount);
   2344     for (var [hash, current] of Object.entries(FS.nameTable)) {
   2345       while (current) {
   2346         var next = current.name_next;
   2347         if (mounts.includes(current.mount)) {
   2348           FS.destroyNode(current);
   2349         }
   2350         current = next;
   2351       }
   2352     }
   2353     // no longer a mountpoint
   2354     node.mounted = null;
   2355     // remove this mount from the child mounts
   2356     var idx = node.mount.mounts.indexOf(mount);
   2357     node.mount.mounts.splice(idx, 1);
   2358   },
   2359   lookup(parent, name) {
   2360     return parent.node_ops.lookup(parent, name);
   2361   },
   2362   mknod(path, mode, dev) {
   2363     var lookup = FS.lookupPath(path, {
   2364       parent: true
   2365     });
   2366     var parent = lookup.node;
   2367     var name = PATH.basename(path);
   2368     if (!name) {
   2369       throw new FS.ErrnoError(28);
   2370     }
   2371     if (name === "." || name === "..") {
   2372       throw new FS.ErrnoError(20);
   2373     }
   2374     var errCode = FS.mayCreate(parent, name);
   2375     if (errCode) {
   2376       throw new FS.ErrnoError(errCode);
   2377     }
   2378     if (!parent.node_ops.mknod) {
   2379       throw new FS.ErrnoError(63);
   2380     }
   2381     return parent.node_ops.mknod(parent, name, mode, dev);
   2382   },
   2383   statfs(path) {
   2384     return FS.statfsNode(FS.lookupPath(path, {
   2385       follow: true
   2386     }).node);
   2387   },
   2388   statfsStream(stream) {
   2389     // We keep a separate statfsStream function because noderawfs overrides
   2390     // it. In noderawfs, stream.node is sometimes null. Instead, we need to
   2391     // look at stream.path.
   2392     return FS.statfsNode(stream.node);
   2393   },
   2394   statfsNode(node) {
   2395     // NOTE: None of the defaults here are true. We're just returning safe and
   2396     //       sane values. Currently nodefs and rawfs replace these defaults,
   2397     //       other file systems leave them alone.
   2398     var rtn = {
   2399       bsize: 4096,
   2400       frsize: 4096,
   2401       blocks: 1e6,
   2402       bfree: 5e5,
   2403       bavail: 5e5,
   2404       files: FS.nextInode,
   2405       ffree: FS.nextInode - 1,
   2406       fsid: 42,
   2407       flags: 2,
   2408       namelen: 255
   2409     };
   2410     if (node.node_ops.statfs) {
   2411       Object.assign(rtn, node.node_ops.statfs(node.mount.opts.root));
   2412     }
   2413     return rtn;
   2414   },
   2415   create(path, mode = 438) {
   2416     mode &= 4095;
   2417     mode |= 32768;
   2418     return FS.mknod(path, mode, 0);
   2419   },
   2420   mkdir(path, mode = 511) {
   2421     mode &= 511 | 512;
   2422     mode |= 16384;
   2423     return FS.mknod(path, mode, 0);
   2424   },
   2425   mkdirTree(path, mode) {
   2426     var dirs = path.split("/");
   2427     var d = "";
   2428     for (var dir of dirs) {
   2429       if (!dir) continue;
   2430       if (d || PATH.isAbs(path)) d += "/";
   2431       d += dir;
   2432       try {
   2433         FS.mkdir(d, mode);
   2434       } catch (e) {
   2435         if (e.errno != 20) throw e;
   2436       }
   2437     }
   2438   },
   2439   mkdev(path, mode, dev) {
   2440     if (typeof dev == "undefined") {
   2441       dev = mode;
   2442       mode = 438;
   2443     }
   2444     mode |= 8192;
   2445     return FS.mknod(path, mode, dev);
   2446   },
   2447   symlink(oldpath, newpath) {
   2448     if (!PATH_FS.resolve(oldpath)) {
   2449       throw new FS.ErrnoError(44);
   2450     }
   2451     var lookup = FS.lookupPath(newpath, {
   2452       parent: true
   2453     });
   2454     var parent = lookup.node;
   2455     if (!parent) {
   2456       throw new FS.ErrnoError(44);
   2457     }
   2458     var newname = PATH.basename(newpath);
   2459     var errCode = FS.mayCreate(parent, newname);
   2460     if (errCode) {
   2461       throw new FS.ErrnoError(errCode);
   2462     }
   2463     if (!parent.node_ops.symlink) {
   2464       throw new FS.ErrnoError(63);
   2465     }
   2466     return parent.node_ops.symlink(parent, newname, oldpath);
   2467   },
   2468   rename(old_path, new_path) {
   2469     var old_dirname = PATH.dirname(old_path);
   2470     var new_dirname = PATH.dirname(new_path);
   2471     var old_name = PATH.basename(old_path);
   2472     var new_name = PATH.basename(new_path);
   2473     // parents must exist
   2474     var lookup, old_dir, new_dir;
   2475     // let the errors from non existent directories percolate up
   2476     lookup = FS.lookupPath(old_path, {
   2477       parent: true
   2478     });
   2479     old_dir = lookup.node;
   2480     lookup = FS.lookupPath(new_path, {
   2481       parent: true
   2482     });
   2483     new_dir = lookup.node;
   2484     if (!old_dir || !new_dir) throw new FS.ErrnoError(44);
   2485     // need to be part of the same mount
   2486     if (old_dir.mount !== new_dir.mount) {
   2487       throw new FS.ErrnoError(75);
   2488     }
   2489     // source must exist
   2490     var old_node = FS.lookupNode(old_dir, old_name);
   2491     // old path should not be an ancestor of the new path
   2492     var relative = PATH_FS.relative(old_path, new_dirname);
   2493     if (relative.charAt(0) !== ".") {
   2494       throw new FS.ErrnoError(28);
   2495     }
   2496     // new path should not be an ancestor of the old path
   2497     relative = PATH_FS.relative(new_path, old_dirname);
   2498     if (relative.charAt(0) !== ".") {
   2499       throw new FS.ErrnoError(55);
   2500     }
   2501     // see if the new path already exists
   2502     var new_node;
   2503     try {
   2504       new_node = FS.lookupNode(new_dir, new_name);
   2505     } catch (e) {}
   2506     // early out if nothing needs to change
   2507     if (old_node === new_node) {
   2508       return;
   2509     }
   2510     // we'll need to delete the old entry
   2511     var isdir = FS.isDir(old_node.mode);
   2512     var errCode = FS.mayDelete(old_dir, old_name, isdir);
   2513     if (errCode) {
   2514       throw new FS.ErrnoError(errCode);
   2515     }
   2516     // need delete permissions if we'll be overwriting.
   2517     // need create permissions if new doesn't already exist.
   2518     errCode = new_node ? FS.mayDelete(new_dir, new_name, isdir) : FS.mayCreate(new_dir, new_name);
   2519     if (errCode) {
   2520       throw new FS.ErrnoError(errCode);
   2521     }
   2522     if (!old_dir.node_ops.rename) {
   2523       throw new FS.ErrnoError(63);
   2524     }
   2525     if (FS.isMountpoint(old_node) || (new_node && FS.isMountpoint(new_node))) {
   2526       throw new FS.ErrnoError(10);
   2527     }
   2528     // if we are going to change the parent, check write permissions
   2529     if (new_dir !== old_dir) {
   2530       errCode = FS.nodePermissions(old_dir, "w");
   2531       if (errCode) {
   2532         throw new FS.ErrnoError(errCode);
   2533       }
   2534     }
   2535     // remove the node from the lookup hash
   2536     FS.hashRemoveNode(old_node);
   2537     // do the underlying fs rename
   2538     try {
   2539       old_dir.node_ops.rename(old_node, new_dir, new_name);
   2540       // update old node (we do this here to avoid each backend
   2541       // needing to)
   2542       old_node.parent = new_dir;
   2543     } catch (e) {
   2544       throw e;
   2545     } finally {
   2546       // add the node back to the hash (in case node_ops.rename
   2547       // changed its name)
   2548       FS.hashAddNode(old_node);
   2549     }
   2550   },
   2551   rmdir(path) {
   2552     var lookup = FS.lookupPath(path, {
   2553       parent: true
   2554     });
   2555     var parent = lookup.node;
   2556     var name = PATH.basename(path);
   2557     var node = FS.lookupNode(parent, name);
   2558     var errCode = FS.mayDelete(parent, name, true);
   2559     if (errCode) {
   2560       throw new FS.ErrnoError(errCode);
   2561     }
   2562     if (!parent.node_ops.rmdir) {
   2563       throw new FS.ErrnoError(63);
   2564     }
   2565     if (FS.isMountpoint(node)) {
   2566       throw new FS.ErrnoError(10);
   2567     }
   2568     parent.node_ops.rmdir(parent, name);
   2569     FS.destroyNode(node);
   2570   },
   2571   readdir(path) {
   2572     var lookup = FS.lookupPath(path, {
   2573       follow: true
   2574     });
   2575     var node = lookup.node;
   2576     var readdir = FS.checkOpExists(node.node_ops.readdir, 54);
   2577     return readdir(node);
   2578   },
   2579   unlink(path) {
   2580     var lookup = FS.lookupPath(path, {
   2581       parent: true
   2582     });
   2583     var parent = lookup.node;
   2584     if (!parent) {
   2585       throw new FS.ErrnoError(44);
   2586     }
   2587     var name = PATH.basename(path);
   2588     var node = FS.lookupNode(parent, name);
   2589     var errCode = FS.mayDelete(parent, name, false);
   2590     if (errCode) {
   2591       // According to POSIX, we should map EISDIR to EPERM, but
   2592       // we instead do what Linux does (and we must, as we use
   2593       // the musl linux libc).
   2594       throw new FS.ErrnoError(errCode);
   2595     }
   2596     if (!parent.node_ops.unlink) {
   2597       throw new FS.ErrnoError(63);
   2598     }
   2599     if (FS.isMountpoint(node)) {
   2600       throw new FS.ErrnoError(10);
   2601     }
   2602     parent.node_ops.unlink(parent, name);
   2603     FS.destroyNode(node);
   2604   },
   2605   readlink(path) {
   2606     var lookup = FS.lookupPath(path);
   2607     var link = lookup.node;
   2608     if (!link) {
   2609       throw new FS.ErrnoError(44);
   2610     }
   2611     if (!link.node_ops.readlink) {
   2612       throw new FS.ErrnoError(28);
   2613     }
   2614     return link.node_ops.readlink(link);
   2615   },
   2616   stat(path, dontFollow) {
   2617     var lookup = FS.lookupPath(path, {
   2618       follow: !dontFollow
   2619     });
   2620     var node = lookup.node;
   2621     var getattr = FS.checkOpExists(node.node_ops.getattr, 63);
   2622     return getattr(node);
   2623   },
   2624   fstat(fd) {
   2625     var stream = FS.getStreamChecked(fd);
   2626     var node = stream.node;
   2627     var getattr = stream.stream_ops.getattr;
   2628     var arg = getattr ? stream : node;
   2629     getattr ??= node.node_ops.getattr;
   2630     FS.checkOpExists(getattr, 63);
   2631     return getattr(arg);
   2632   },
   2633   lstat(path) {
   2634     return FS.stat(path, true);
   2635   },
   2636   doChmod(stream, node, mode, dontFollow) {
   2637     FS.doSetAttr(stream, node, {
   2638       mode: (mode & 4095) | (node.mode & ~4095),
   2639       ctime: Date.now(),
   2640       dontFollow
   2641     });
   2642   },
   2643   chmod(path, mode, dontFollow) {
   2644     var node;
   2645     if (typeof path == "string") {
   2646       var lookup = FS.lookupPath(path, {
   2647         follow: !dontFollow
   2648       });
   2649       node = lookup.node;
   2650     } else {
   2651       node = path;
   2652     }
   2653     FS.doChmod(null, node, mode, dontFollow);
   2654   },
   2655   lchmod(path, mode) {
   2656     FS.chmod(path, mode, true);
   2657   },
   2658   fchmod(fd, mode) {
   2659     var stream = FS.getStreamChecked(fd);
   2660     FS.doChmod(stream, stream.node, mode, false);
   2661   },
   2662   doChown(stream, node, dontFollow) {
   2663     FS.doSetAttr(stream, node, {
   2664       timestamp: Date.now(),
   2665       dontFollow
   2666     });
   2667   },
   2668   chown(path, uid, gid, dontFollow) {
   2669     var node;
   2670     if (typeof path == "string") {
   2671       var lookup = FS.lookupPath(path, {
   2672         follow: !dontFollow
   2673       });
   2674       node = lookup.node;
   2675     } else {
   2676       node = path;
   2677     }
   2678     FS.doChown(null, node, dontFollow);
   2679   },
   2680   lchown(path, uid, gid) {
   2681     FS.chown(path, uid, gid, true);
   2682   },
   2683   fchown(fd, uid, gid) {
   2684     var stream = FS.getStreamChecked(fd);
   2685     FS.doChown(stream, stream.node, false);
   2686   },
   2687   doTruncate(stream, node, len) {
   2688     if (FS.isDir(node.mode)) {
   2689       throw new FS.ErrnoError(31);
   2690     }
   2691     if (!FS.isFile(node.mode)) {
   2692       throw new FS.ErrnoError(28);
   2693     }
   2694     var errCode = FS.nodePermissions(node, "w");
   2695     if (errCode) {
   2696       throw new FS.ErrnoError(errCode);
   2697     }
   2698     FS.doSetAttr(stream, node, {
   2699       size: len,
   2700       timestamp: Date.now()
   2701     });
   2702   },
   2703   truncate(path, len) {
   2704     if (len < 0) {
   2705       throw new FS.ErrnoError(28);
   2706     }
   2707     var node;
   2708     if (typeof path == "string") {
   2709       var lookup = FS.lookupPath(path, {
   2710         follow: true
   2711       });
   2712       node = lookup.node;
   2713     } else {
   2714       node = path;
   2715     }
   2716     FS.doTruncate(null, node, len);
   2717   },
   2718   ftruncate(fd, len) {
   2719     var stream = FS.getStreamChecked(fd);
   2720     if (len < 0 || (stream.flags & 2097155) === 0) {
   2721       throw new FS.ErrnoError(28);
   2722     }
   2723     FS.doTruncate(stream, stream.node, len);
   2724   },
   2725   utime(path, atime, mtime) {
   2726     var lookup = FS.lookupPath(path, {
   2727       follow: true
   2728     });
   2729     var node = lookup.node;
   2730     var setattr = FS.checkOpExists(node.node_ops.setattr, 63);
   2731     setattr(node, {
   2732       atime,
   2733       mtime
   2734     });
   2735   },
   2736   open(path, flags, mode = 438) {
   2737     if (path === "") {
   2738       throw new FS.ErrnoError(44);
   2739     }
   2740     flags = FS_modeStringToFlags(flags);
   2741     if ((flags & 64)) {
   2742       mode = (mode & 4095) | 32768;
   2743     } else {
   2744       mode = 0;
   2745     }
   2746     var node;
   2747     var isDirPath;
   2748     if (typeof path == "object") {
   2749       node = path;
   2750     } else {
   2751       isDirPath = path.endsWith("/");
   2752       // noent_okay makes it so that if the final component of the path
   2753       // doesn't exist, lookupPath returns `node: undefined`. `path` will be
   2754       // updated to point to the target of all symlinks.
   2755       var lookup = FS.lookupPath(path, {
   2756         follow: !(flags & 131072),
   2757         noent_okay: true
   2758       });
   2759       node = lookup.node;
   2760       path = lookup.path;
   2761     }
   2762     // perhaps we need to create the node
   2763     var created = false;
   2764     if ((flags & 64)) {
   2765       if (node) {
   2766         // if O_CREAT and O_EXCL are set, error out if the node already exists
   2767         if ((flags & 128)) {
   2768           throw new FS.ErrnoError(20);
   2769         }
   2770       } else if (isDirPath) {
   2771         throw new FS.ErrnoError(31);
   2772       } else {
   2773         // node doesn't exist, try to create it
   2774         // Ignore the permission bits here to ensure we can `open` this new
   2775         // file below. We use chmod below to apply the permissions once the
   2776         // file is open.
   2777         node = FS.mknod(path, mode | 511, 0);
   2778         created = true;
   2779       }
   2780     }
   2781     if (!node) {
   2782       throw new FS.ErrnoError(44);
   2783     }
   2784     // can't truncate a device
   2785     if (FS.isChrdev(node.mode)) {
   2786       flags &= ~512;
   2787     }
   2788     // if asked only for a directory, then this must be one
   2789     if ((flags & 65536) && !FS.isDir(node.mode)) {
   2790       throw new FS.ErrnoError(54);
   2791     }
   2792     // check permissions, if this is not a file we just created now (it is ok to
   2793     // create and write to a file with read-only permissions; it is read-only
   2794     // for later use)
   2795     if (!created) {
   2796       var errCode = FS.mayOpen(node, flags);
   2797       if (errCode) {
   2798         throw new FS.ErrnoError(errCode);
   2799       }
   2800     }
   2801     // do truncation if necessary
   2802     if ((flags & 512) && !created) {
   2803       FS.truncate(node, 0);
   2804     }
   2805     // we've already handled these, don't pass down to the underlying vfs
   2806     flags &= ~(128 | 512 | 131072);
   2807     // register the stream with the filesystem
   2808     var stream = FS.createStream({
   2809       node,
   2810       path: FS.getPath(node),
   2811       // we want the absolute path to the node
   2812       flags,
   2813       seekable: true,
   2814       position: 0,
   2815       stream_ops: node.stream_ops,
   2816       // used by the file family libc calls (fopen, fwrite, ferror, etc.)
   2817       ungotten: [],
   2818       error: false
   2819     });
   2820     // call the new stream's open function
   2821     if (stream.stream_ops.open) {
   2822       stream.stream_ops.open(stream);
   2823     }
   2824     if (created) {
   2825       FS.chmod(node, mode & 511);
   2826     }
   2827     return stream;
   2828   },
   2829   close(stream) {
   2830     if (FS.isClosed(stream)) {
   2831       throw new FS.ErrnoError(8);
   2832     }
   2833     if (stream.getdents) stream.getdents = null;
   2834     // free readdir state
   2835     try {
   2836       if (stream.stream_ops.close) {
   2837         stream.stream_ops.close(stream);
   2838       }
   2839     } catch (e) {
   2840       throw e;
   2841     } finally {
   2842       FS.closeStream(stream.fd);
   2843     }
   2844     stream.fd = null;
   2845   },
   2846   isClosed(stream) {
   2847     return stream.fd === null;
   2848   },
   2849   llseek(stream, offset, whence) {
   2850     if (FS.isClosed(stream)) {
   2851       throw new FS.ErrnoError(8);
   2852     }
   2853     if (!stream.seekable || !stream.stream_ops.llseek) {
   2854       throw new FS.ErrnoError(70);
   2855     }
   2856     if (whence != 0 && whence != 1 && whence != 2) {
   2857       throw new FS.ErrnoError(28);
   2858     }
   2859     stream.position = stream.stream_ops.llseek(stream, offset, whence);
   2860     stream.ungotten = [];
   2861     return stream.position;
   2862   },
   2863   read(stream, buffer, offset, length, position) {
   2864     if (length < 0 || position < 0) {
   2865       throw new FS.ErrnoError(28);
   2866     }
   2867     if (FS.isClosed(stream)) {
   2868       throw new FS.ErrnoError(8);
   2869     }
   2870     if ((stream.flags & 2097155) === 1) {
   2871       throw new FS.ErrnoError(8);
   2872     }
   2873     if (FS.isDir(stream.node.mode)) {
   2874       throw new FS.ErrnoError(31);
   2875     }
   2876     if (!stream.stream_ops.read) {
   2877       throw new FS.ErrnoError(28);
   2878     }
   2879     var seeking = typeof position != "undefined";
   2880     if (!seeking) {
   2881       position = stream.position;
   2882     } else if (!stream.seekable) {
   2883       throw new FS.ErrnoError(70);
   2884     }
   2885     var bytesRead = stream.stream_ops.read(stream, buffer, offset, length, position);
   2886     if (!seeking) stream.position += bytesRead;
   2887     return bytesRead;
   2888   },
   2889   write(stream, buffer, offset, length, position, canOwn) {
   2890     if (length < 0 || position < 0) {
   2891       throw new FS.ErrnoError(28);
   2892     }
   2893     if (FS.isClosed(stream)) {
   2894       throw new FS.ErrnoError(8);
   2895     }
   2896     if ((stream.flags & 2097155) === 0) {
   2897       throw new FS.ErrnoError(8);
   2898     }
   2899     if (FS.isDir(stream.node.mode)) {
   2900       throw new FS.ErrnoError(31);
   2901     }
   2902     if (!stream.stream_ops.write) {
   2903       throw new FS.ErrnoError(28);
   2904     }
   2905     if (stream.seekable && stream.flags & 1024) {
   2906       // seek to the end before writing in append mode
   2907       FS.llseek(stream, 0, 2);
   2908     }
   2909     var seeking = typeof position != "undefined";
   2910     if (!seeking) {
   2911       position = stream.position;
   2912     } else if (!stream.seekable) {
   2913       throw new FS.ErrnoError(70);
   2914     }
   2915     var bytesWritten = stream.stream_ops.write(stream, buffer, offset, length, position, canOwn);
   2916     if (!seeking) stream.position += bytesWritten;
   2917     return bytesWritten;
   2918   },
   2919   mmap(stream, length, position, prot, flags) {
   2920     // User requests writing to file (prot & PROT_WRITE != 0).
   2921     // Checking if we have permissions to write to the file unless
   2922     // MAP_PRIVATE flag is set. According to POSIX spec it is possible
   2923     // to write to file opened in read-only mode with MAP_PRIVATE flag,
   2924     // as all modifications will be visible only in the memory of
   2925     // the current process.
   2926     if ((prot & 2) !== 0 && (flags & 2) === 0 && (stream.flags & 2097155) !== 2) {
   2927       throw new FS.ErrnoError(2);
   2928     }
   2929     if ((stream.flags & 2097155) === 1) {
   2930       throw new FS.ErrnoError(2);
   2931     }
   2932     if (!stream.stream_ops.mmap) {
   2933       throw new FS.ErrnoError(43);
   2934     }
   2935     if (!length) {
   2936       throw new FS.ErrnoError(28);
   2937     }
   2938     return stream.stream_ops.mmap(stream, length, position, prot, flags);
   2939   },
   2940   msync(stream, buffer, offset, length, mmapFlags) {
   2941     if (!stream.stream_ops.msync) {
   2942       return 0;
   2943     }
   2944     return stream.stream_ops.msync(stream, buffer, offset, length, mmapFlags);
   2945   },
   2946   ioctl(stream, cmd, arg) {
   2947     if (!stream.stream_ops.ioctl) {
   2948       throw new FS.ErrnoError(59);
   2949     }
   2950     return stream.stream_ops.ioctl(stream, cmd, arg);
   2951   },
   2952   readFile(path, opts = {}) {
   2953     opts.flags = opts.flags || 0;
   2954     opts.encoding = opts.encoding || "binary";
   2955     if (opts.encoding !== "utf8" && opts.encoding !== "binary") {
   2956       abort(`Invalid encoding type "${opts.encoding}"`);
   2957     }
   2958     var stream = FS.open(path, opts.flags);
   2959     var stat = FS.stat(path);
   2960     var length = stat.size;
   2961     var buf = new Uint8Array(length);
   2962     FS.read(stream, buf, 0, length, 0);
   2963     if (opts.encoding === "utf8") {
   2964       buf = UTF8ArrayToString(buf);
   2965     }
   2966     FS.close(stream);
   2967     return buf;
   2968   },
   2969   writeFile(path, data, opts = {}) {
   2970     opts.flags = opts.flags || 577;
   2971     var stream = FS.open(path, opts.flags, opts.mode);
   2972     data = FS_fileDataToTypedArray(data);
   2973     FS.write(stream, data, 0, data.byteLength, undefined, opts.canOwn);
   2974     FS.close(stream);
   2975   },
   2976   cwd: () => FS.currentPath,
   2977   chdir(path) {
   2978     var lookup = FS.lookupPath(path, {
   2979       follow: true
   2980     });
   2981     if (lookup.node === null) {
   2982       throw new FS.ErrnoError(44);
   2983     }
   2984     if (!FS.isDir(lookup.node.mode)) {
   2985       throw new FS.ErrnoError(54);
   2986     }
   2987     var errCode = FS.nodePermissions(lookup.node, "x");
   2988     if (errCode) {
   2989       throw new FS.ErrnoError(errCode);
   2990     }
   2991     FS.currentPath = lookup.path;
   2992   },
   2993   createDefaultDirectories() {
   2994     FS.mkdir("/tmp");
   2995     FS.mkdir("/home");
   2996     FS.mkdir("/home/web_user");
   2997   },
   2998   createDefaultDevices() {
   2999     // create /dev
   3000     FS.mkdir("/dev");
   3001     // setup /dev/null
   3002     FS.registerDevice(FS.makedev(1, 3), {
   3003       read: () => 0,
   3004       write: (stream, buffer, offset, length, pos) => length,
   3005       llseek: () => 0
   3006     });
   3007     FS.mkdev("/dev/null", FS.makedev(1, 3));
   3008     // setup /dev/tty and /dev/tty1
   3009     // stderr needs to print output using err() rather than out()
   3010     // so we register a second tty just for it.
   3011     TTY.register(FS.makedev(5, 0), TTY.default_tty_ops);
   3012     TTY.register(FS.makedev(6, 0), TTY.default_tty1_ops);
   3013     FS.mkdev("/dev/tty", FS.makedev(5, 0));
   3014     FS.mkdev("/dev/tty1", FS.makedev(6, 0));
   3015     // setup /dev/[u]random
   3016     // use a buffer to avoid overhead of individual crypto calls per byte
   3017     var randomBuffer = new Uint8Array(1024), randomLeft = 0;
   3018     var randomByte = () => {
   3019       if (randomLeft === 0) {
   3020         randomFill(randomBuffer);
   3021         randomLeft = randomBuffer.byteLength;
   3022       }
   3023       return randomBuffer[--randomLeft];
   3024     };
   3025     FS.createDevice("/dev", "random", randomByte);
   3026     FS.createDevice("/dev", "urandom", randomByte);
   3027     // we're not going to emulate the actual shm device,
   3028     // just create the tmp dirs that reside in it commonly
   3029     FS.mkdir("/dev/shm");
   3030     FS.mkdir("/dev/shm/tmp");
   3031   },
   3032   createSpecialDirectories() {
   3033     // create /proc/self/fd which allows /proc/self/fd/6 => readlink gives the
   3034     // name of the stream for fd 6 (see test_unistd_ttyname)
   3035     FS.mkdir("/proc");
   3036     var proc_self = FS.mkdir("/proc/self");
   3037     FS.mkdir("/proc/self/fd");
   3038     FS.mount({
   3039       mount() {
   3040         var node = FS.createNode(proc_self, "fd", 16895, 73);
   3041         node.stream_ops = {
   3042           llseek: MEMFS.stream_ops.llseek
   3043         };
   3044         node.node_ops = {
   3045           lookup(parent, name) {
   3046             var fd = +name;
   3047             var stream = FS.getStreamChecked(fd);
   3048             var ret = {
   3049               parent: null,
   3050               mount: {
   3051                 mountpoint: "fake"
   3052               },
   3053               node_ops: {
   3054                 readlink: () => stream.path
   3055               },
   3056               id: fd + 1
   3057             };
   3058             ret.parent = ret;
   3059             // make it look like a simple root node
   3060             return ret;
   3061           },
   3062           readdir() {
   3063             return Array.from(FS.streams.entries()).filter(([k, v]) => v).map(([k, v]) => k.toString());
   3064           }
   3065         };
   3066         return node;
   3067       }
   3068     }, {}, "/proc/self/fd");
   3069   },
   3070   createStandardStreams(input, output, error) {
   3071     // TODO deprecate the old functionality of a single
   3072     // input / output callback and that utilizes FS.createDevice
   3073     // and instead require a unique set of stream ops
   3074     // by default, we symlink the standard streams to the
   3075     // default tty devices. however, if the standard streams
   3076     // have been overwritten we create a unique device for
   3077     // them instead.
   3078     if (input) {
   3079       FS.createDevice("/dev", "stdin", input);
   3080     } else {
   3081       FS.symlink("/dev/tty", "/dev/stdin");
   3082     }
   3083     if (output) {
   3084       FS.createDevice("/dev", "stdout", null, output);
   3085     } else {
   3086       FS.symlink("/dev/tty", "/dev/stdout");
   3087     }
   3088     if (error) {
   3089       FS.createDevice("/dev", "stderr", null, error);
   3090     } else {
   3091       FS.symlink("/dev/tty1", "/dev/stderr");
   3092     }
   3093     // open default streams for the stdin, stdout and stderr devices
   3094     var stdin = FS.open("/dev/stdin", 0);
   3095     var stdout = FS.open("/dev/stdout", 1);
   3096     var stderr = FS.open("/dev/stderr", 1);
   3097   },
   3098   staticInit() {
   3099     FS.nameTable = new Array(4096);
   3100     FS.mount(MEMFS, {}, "/");
   3101     FS.createDefaultDirectories();
   3102     FS.createDefaultDevices();
   3103     FS.createSpecialDirectories();
   3104     FS.filesystems = {
   3105       "MEMFS": MEMFS
   3106     };
   3107   },
   3108   init(input, output, error) {
   3109     FS.initialized = true;
   3110     // Allow Module.stdin etc. to provide defaults, if none explicitly passed to us here
   3111     input ??= Module["stdin"];
   3112     output ??= Module["stdout"];
   3113     error ??= Module["stderr"];
   3114     FS.createStandardStreams(input, output, error);
   3115   },
   3116   quit() {
   3117     FS.initialized = false;
   3118     // force-flush all streams, so we get musl std streams printed out
   3119     // close all of our streams
   3120     for (var stream of FS.streams) {
   3121       if (stream) {
   3122         FS.close(stream);
   3123       }
   3124     }
   3125   },
   3126   findObject(path, dontResolveLastLink) {
   3127     var ret = FS.analyzePath(path, dontResolveLastLink);
   3128     if (!ret.exists) {
   3129       return null;
   3130     }
   3131     return ret.object;
   3132   },
   3133   analyzePath(path, dontResolveLastLink) {
   3134     // operate from within the context of the symlink's target
   3135     try {
   3136       var lookup = FS.lookupPath(path, {
   3137         follow: !dontResolveLastLink
   3138       });
   3139       path = lookup.path;
   3140     } catch (e) {}
   3141     var ret = {
   3142       isRoot: false,
   3143       exists: false,
   3144       error: 0,
   3145       name: null,
   3146       path: null,
   3147       object: null,
   3148       parentExists: false,
   3149       parentPath: null,
   3150       parentObject: null
   3151     };
   3152     try {
   3153       var lookup = FS.lookupPath(path, {
   3154         parent: true
   3155       });
   3156       ret.parentExists = true;
   3157       ret.parentPath = lookup.path;
   3158       ret.parentObject = lookup.node;
   3159       ret.name = PATH.basename(path);
   3160       lookup = FS.lookupPath(path, {
   3161         follow: !dontResolveLastLink
   3162       });
   3163       ret.exists = true;
   3164       ret.path = lookup.path;
   3165       ret.object = lookup.node;
   3166       ret.name = lookup.node.name;
   3167       ret.isRoot = lookup.path === "/";
   3168     } catch (e) {
   3169       ret.error = e.errno;
   3170     }
   3171     return ret;
   3172   },
   3173   createPath(parent, path, canRead, canWrite) {
   3174     parent = typeof parent == "string" ? parent : FS.getPath(parent);
   3175     var parts = path.split("/").reverse();
   3176     while (parts.length) {
   3177       var part = parts.pop();
   3178       if (!part) continue;
   3179       var current = PATH.join2(parent, part);
   3180       try {
   3181         FS.mkdir(current);
   3182       } catch (e) {
   3183         if (e.errno != 20) throw e;
   3184       }
   3185       parent = current;
   3186     }
   3187     return current;
   3188   },
   3189   createFile(parent, name, properties, canRead, canWrite) {
   3190     var path = PATH.join2(typeof parent == "string" ? parent : FS.getPath(parent), name);
   3191     var mode = FS_getMode(canRead, canWrite);
   3192     return FS.create(path, mode);
   3193   },
   3194   createDataFile(parent, name, data, canRead, canWrite, canOwn) {
   3195     var path = name;
   3196     if (parent) {
   3197       parent = typeof parent == "string" ? parent : FS.getPath(parent);
   3198       path = name ? PATH.join2(parent, name) : parent;
   3199     }
   3200     var mode = FS_getMode(canRead, canWrite);
   3201     var node = FS.create(path, mode);
   3202     if (data) {
   3203       data = FS_fileDataToTypedArray(data);
   3204       // make sure we can write to the file
   3205       FS.chmod(node, mode | 146);
   3206       var stream = FS.open(node, 577);
   3207       FS.write(stream, data, 0, data.length, 0, canOwn);
   3208       FS.close(stream);
   3209       FS.chmod(node, mode);
   3210     }
   3211   },
   3212   createDevice(parent, name, input, output) {
   3213     var path = PATH.join2(typeof parent == "string" ? parent : FS.getPath(parent), name);
   3214     var mode = FS_getMode(!!input, !!output);
   3215     FS.createDevice.major ??= 64;
   3216     var dev = FS.makedev(FS.createDevice.major++, 0);
   3217     // Create a fake device that a set of stream ops to emulate
   3218     // the old behavior.
   3219     FS.registerDevice(dev, {
   3220       open(stream) {
   3221         stream.seekable = false;
   3222       },
   3223       close(stream) {
   3224         // flush any pending line data
   3225         if (output?.buffer?.length) {
   3226           output(10);
   3227         }
   3228       },
   3229       read(stream, buffer, offset, length, pos) {
   3230         var bytesRead = 0;
   3231         for (var i = 0; i < length; i++) {
   3232           var result;
   3233           try {
   3234             result = input();
   3235           } catch (e) {
   3236             throw new FS.ErrnoError(29);
   3237           }
   3238           if (result === undefined && bytesRead === 0) {
   3239             throw new FS.ErrnoError(6);
   3240           }
   3241           if (result === null || result === undefined) break;
   3242           bytesRead++;
   3243           buffer[offset + i] = result;
   3244         }
   3245         if (bytesRead) {
   3246           stream.node.atime = Date.now();
   3247         }
   3248         return bytesRead;
   3249       },
   3250       write(stream, buffer, offset, length, pos) {
   3251         for (var i = 0; i < length; i++) {
   3252           try {
   3253             output(buffer[offset + i]);
   3254           } catch (e) {
   3255             throw new FS.ErrnoError(29);
   3256           }
   3257         }
   3258         if (length) {
   3259           stream.node.mtime = stream.node.ctime = Date.now();
   3260         }
   3261         return i;
   3262       }
   3263     });
   3264     return FS.mkdev(path, mode, dev);
   3265   },
   3266   forceLoadFile(obj) {
   3267     if (obj.isDevice || obj.isFolder || obj.link || obj.contents) return true;
   3268     if (globalThis.XMLHttpRequest) {
   3269       abort("Lazy loading should have been performed (contents set) in createLazyFile, but it was not. Lazy loading only works in web workers. Use --embed-file or --preload-file in emcc on the main thread.");
   3270     } else {
   3271       // Command-line.
   3272       try {
   3273         obj.contents = readBinary(obj.url);
   3274       } catch (e) {
   3275         throw new FS.ErrnoError(29);
   3276       }
   3277     }
   3278   },
   3279   createLazyFile(parent, name, url, canRead, canWrite) {
   3280     // Lazy chunked Uint8Array (implements get and length from Uint8Array).
   3281     // Actual getting is abstracted away for eventual reuse.
   3282     class LazyUint8Array {
   3283       lengthKnown=false;
   3284       chunks=[];
   3285       // Loaded chunks. Index is the chunk number
   3286       get(idx) {
   3287         if (idx > this.length - 1 || idx < 0) {
   3288           return undefined;
   3289         }
   3290         var chunkOffset = idx % this.chunkSize;
   3291         var chunkNum = (idx / this.chunkSize) | 0;
   3292         return this.getter(chunkNum)[chunkOffset];
   3293       }
   3294       setDataGetter(getter) {
   3295         this.getter = getter;
   3296       }
   3297       cacheLength() {
   3298         // Find length
   3299         var xhr = new XMLHttpRequest;
   3300         xhr.open("HEAD", url, false);
   3301         xhr.send(null);
   3302         if (!(xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 300 || xhr.status === 304)) abort("Couldn't load " + url + ". Status: " + xhr.status);
   3303         var datalength = Number(xhr.getResponseHeader("Content-length"));
   3304         var header;
   3305         var hasByteServing = (header = xhr.getResponseHeader("Accept-Ranges")) && header === "bytes";
   3306         var usesGzip = (header = xhr.getResponseHeader("Content-Encoding")) && header === "gzip";
   3307         var chunkSize = 1024 * 1024;
   3308         // Chunk size in bytes
   3309         if (!hasByteServing) chunkSize = datalength;
   3310         // Function to get a range from the remote URL.
   3311         var doXHR = (from, to) => {
   3312           if (from > to) abort("invalid range (" + from + ", " + to + ") or no bytes requested!");
   3313           if (to > datalength - 1) abort("only " + datalength + " bytes available! programmer error!");
   3314           // TODO: Use mozResponseArrayBuffer, responseStream, etc. if available.
   3315           var xhr = new XMLHttpRequest;
   3316           xhr.open("GET", url, false);
   3317           if (datalength !== chunkSize) xhr.setRequestHeader("Range", "bytes=" + from + "-" + to);
   3318           // Some hints to the browser that we want binary data.
   3319           xhr.responseType = "arraybuffer";
   3320           if (xhr.overrideMimeType) {
   3321             xhr.overrideMimeType("text/plain; charset=x-user-defined");
   3322           }
   3323           xhr.send(null);
   3324           if (!(xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 300 || xhr.status === 304)) abort("Couldn't load " + url + ". Status: " + xhr.status);
   3325           if (xhr.response !== undefined) {
   3326             return new Uint8Array(/** @type{Array<number>} */ (xhr.response || []));
   3327           }
   3328           return intArrayFromString(xhr.responseText || "", true);
   3329         };
   3330         var lazyArray = this;
   3331         lazyArray.setDataGetter(chunkNum => {
   3332           var start = chunkNum * chunkSize;
   3333           var end = (chunkNum + 1) * chunkSize - 1;
   3334           // including this byte
   3335           end = Math.min(end, datalength - 1);
   3336           // if datalength-1 is selected, this is the last block
   3337           if (typeof lazyArray.chunks[chunkNum] == "undefined") {
   3338             lazyArray.chunks[chunkNum] = doXHR(start, end);
   3339           }
   3340           if (typeof lazyArray.chunks[chunkNum] == "undefined") abort("doXHR failed!");
   3341           return lazyArray.chunks[chunkNum];
   3342         });
   3343         if (usesGzip || !datalength) {
   3344           // if the server uses gzip or doesn't supply the length, we have to download the whole file to get the (uncompressed) length
   3345           chunkSize = datalength = 1;
   3346           // this will force getter(0)/doXHR do download the whole file
   3347           datalength = this.getter(0).length;
   3348           chunkSize = datalength;
   3349           out("LazyFiles on gzip forces download of the whole file when length is accessed");
   3350         }
   3351         this._length = datalength;
   3352         this._chunkSize = chunkSize;
   3353         this.lengthKnown = true;
   3354       }
   3355       get length() {
   3356         if (!this.lengthKnown) {
   3357           this.cacheLength();
   3358         }
   3359         return this._length;
   3360       }
   3361       get chunkSize() {
   3362         if (!this.lengthKnown) {
   3363           this.cacheLength();
   3364         }
   3365         return this._chunkSize;
   3366       }
   3367     }
   3368     if (globalThis.XMLHttpRequest) {
   3369       if (!ENVIRONMENT_IS_WORKER) abort("Cannot do synchronous binary XHRs outside webworkers in modern browsers. Use --embed-file or --preload-file in emcc");
   3370       var lazyArray = new LazyUint8Array;
   3371       var properties = {
   3372         isDevice: false,
   3373         contents: lazyArray
   3374       };
   3375     } else {
   3376       var properties = {
   3377         isDevice: false,
   3378         url
   3379       };
   3380     }
   3381     var node = FS.createFile(parent, name, properties, canRead, canWrite);
   3382     // This is a total hack, but I want to get this lazy file code out of the
   3383     // core of MEMFS. If we want to keep this lazy file concept I feel it should
   3384     // be its own thin LAZYFS proxying calls to MEMFS.
   3385     if (properties.contents) {
   3386       node.contents = properties.contents;
   3387     } else if (properties.url) {
   3388       node.contents = null;
   3389       node.url = properties.url;
   3390     }
   3391     // Add a function that defers querying the file size until it is asked the first time.
   3392     Object.defineProperties(node, {
   3393       usedBytes: {
   3394         get: function() {
   3395           return this.contents.length;
   3396         }
   3397       }
   3398     });
   3399     // override each stream op with one that tries to force load the lazy file first
   3400     var stream_ops = {};
   3401     for (const [key, fn] of Object.entries(node.stream_ops)) {
   3402       stream_ops[key] = (...args) => {
   3403         FS.forceLoadFile(node);
   3404         return fn(...args);
   3405       };
   3406     }
   3407     function writeChunks(stream, buffer, offset, length, position) {
   3408       var contents = stream.node.contents;
   3409       if (position >= contents.length) return 0;
   3410       var size = Math.min(contents.length - position, length);
   3411       if (contents.slice) {
   3412         // normal array
   3413         for (var i = 0; i < size; i++) {
   3414           buffer[offset + i] = contents[position + i];
   3415         }
   3416       } else {
   3417         for (var i = 0; i < size; i++) {
   3418           // LazyUint8Array from sync binary XHR
   3419           buffer[offset + i] = contents.get(position + i);
   3420         }
   3421       }
   3422       return size;
   3423     }
   3424     // use a custom read function
   3425     stream_ops.read = (stream, buffer, offset, length, position) => {
   3426       FS.forceLoadFile(node);
   3427       return writeChunks(stream, buffer, offset, length, position);
   3428     };
   3429     // use a custom mmap function
   3430     stream_ops.mmap = (stream, length, position, prot, flags) => {
   3431       FS.forceLoadFile(node);
   3432       var ptr = mmapAlloc(length);
   3433       if (!ptr) {
   3434         throw new FS.ErrnoError(48);
   3435       }
   3436       writeChunks(stream, HEAP8, ptr, length, position);
   3437       return {
   3438         ptr,
   3439         allocated: true
   3440       };
   3441     };
   3442     node.stream_ops = stream_ops;
   3443     return node;
   3444   }
   3445 };
   3446 
   3447 /**
   3448    * Given a pointer 'ptr' to a null-terminated UTF8-encoded string in the
   3449    * emscripten HEAP, returns a copy of that string as a Javascript String object.
   3450    *
   3451    * @param {number} ptr
   3452    * @param {number=} maxBytesToRead - An optional length that specifies the
   3453    *   maximum number of bytes to read. You can omit this parameter to scan the
   3454    *   string until the first 0 byte. If maxBytesToRead is passed, and the string
   3455    *   at [ptr, ptr+maxBytesToReadr[ contains a null byte in the middle, then the
   3456    *   string will cut short at that byte index.
   3457    * @param {boolean=} ignoreNul - If true, the function will not stop on a NUL character.
   3458    * @return {string}
   3459    */ var UTF8ToString = (ptr, maxBytesToRead, ignoreNul) => {
   3460   if (!ptr) return "";
   3461   var end = findStringEnd(HEAPU8, ptr, maxBytesToRead, ignoreNul);
   3462   return UTF8Decoder.decode(HEAPU8.subarray(ptr, end));
   3463 };
   3464 
   3465 var SYSCALLS = {
   3466   calculateAt(dirfd, path, allowEmpty) {
   3467     if (PATH.isAbs(path)) {
   3468       return path;
   3469     }
   3470     // relative path
   3471     var dir;
   3472     if (dirfd === -100) {
   3473       dir = FS.cwd();
   3474     } else {
   3475       var dirstream = SYSCALLS.getStreamFromFD(dirfd);
   3476       dir = dirstream.path;
   3477     }
   3478     if (path.length == 0) {
   3479       if (!allowEmpty) {
   3480         throw new FS.ErrnoError(44);
   3481       }
   3482       return dir;
   3483     }
   3484     return dir + "/" + path;
   3485   },
   3486   writeStat(buf, stat) {
   3487     HEAPU32[((buf) >> 2)] = stat.dev;
   3488     HEAPU32[(((buf) + (4)) >> 2)] = stat.mode;
   3489     HEAPU32[(((buf) + (8)) >> 2)] = stat.nlink;
   3490     HEAPU32[(((buf) + (12)) >> 2)] = stat.uid;
   3491     HEAPU32[(((buf) + (16)) >> 2)] = stat.gid;
   3492     HEAPU32[(((buf) + (20)) >> 2)] = stat.rdev;
   3493     (tempI64 = [ stat.size >>> 0, (tempDouble = stat.size, (+(Math.abs(tempDouble))) >= 1 ? (tempDouble > 0 ? (+(Math.floor((tempDouble) / 4294967296))) >>> 0 : (~~((+(Math.ceil((tempDouble - +(((~~(tempDouble))) >>> 0)) / 4294967296))))) >>> 0) : 0) ], 
   3494     HEAP32[(((buf) + (24)) >> 2)] = tempI64[0], HEAP32[(((buf) + (28)) >> 2)] = tempI64[1]);
   3495     HEAP32[(((buf) + (32)) >> 2)] = 4096;
   3496     HEAP32[(((buf) + (36)) >> 2)] = stat.blocks;
   3497     var atime = stat.atime.getTime();
   3498     var mtime = stat.mtime.getTime();
   3499     var ctime = stat.ctime.getTime();
   3500     (tempI64 = [ Math.floor(atime / 1e3) >>> 0, (tempDouble = Math.floor(atime / 1e3), 
   3501     (+(Math.abs(tempDouble))) >= 1 ? (tempDouble > 0 ? (+(Math.floor((tempDouble) / 4294967296))) >>> 0 : (~~((+(Math.ceil((tempDouble - +(((~~(tempDouble))) >>> 0)) / 4294967296))))) >>> 0) : 0) ], 
   3502     HEAP32[(((buf) + (40)) >> 2)] = tempI64[0], HEAP32[(((buf) + (44)) >> 2)] = tempI64[1]);
   3503     HEAPU32[(((buf) + (48)) >> 2)] = (atime % 1e3) * 1e3 * 1e3;
   3504     (tempI64 = [ Math.floor(mtime / 1e3) >>> 0, (tempDouble = Math.floor(mtime / 1e3), 
   3505     (+(Math.abs(tempDouble))) >= 1 ? (tempDouble > 0 ? (+(Math.floor((tempDouble) / 4294967296))) >>> 0 : (~~((+(Math.ceil((tempDouble - +(((~~(tempDouble))) >>> 0)) / 4294967296))))) >>> 0) : 0) ], 
   3506     HEAP32[(((buf) + (56)) >> 2)] = tempI64[0], HEAP32[(((buf) + (60)) >> 2)] = tempI64[1]);
   3507     HEAPU32[(((buf) + (64)) >> 2)] = (mtime % 1e3) * 1e3 * 1e3;
   3508     (tempI64 = [ Math.floor(ctime / 1e3) >>> 0, (tempDouble = Math.floor(ctime / 1e3), 
   3509     (+(Math.abs(tempDouble))) >= 1 ? (tempDouble > 0 ? (+(Math.floor((tempDouble) / 4294967296))) >>> 0 : (~~((+(Math.ceil((tempDouble - +(((~~(tempDouble))) >>> 0)) / 4294967296))))) >>> 0) : 0) ], 
   3510     HEAP32[(((buf) + (72)) >> 2)] = tempI64[0], HEAP32[(((buf) + (76)) >> 2)] = tempI64[1]);
   3511     HEAPU32[(((buf) + (80)) >> 2)] = (ctime % 1e3) * 1e3 * 1e3;
   3512     (tempI64 = [ stat.ino >>> 0, (tempDouble = stat.ino, (+(Math.abs(tempDouble))) >= 1 ? (tempDouble > 0 ? (+(Math.floor((tempDouble) / 4294967296))) >>> 0 : (~~((+(Math.ceil((tempDouble - +(((~~(tempDouble))) >>> 0)) / 4294967296))))) >>> 0) : 0) ], 
   3513     HEAP32[(((buf) + (88)) >> 2)] = tempI64[0], HEAP32[(((buf) + (92)) >> 2)] = tempI64[1]);
   3514     return 0;
   3515   },
   3516   writeStatFs(buf, stats) {
   3517     HEAPU32[(((buf) + (4)) >> 2)] = stats.bsize;
   3518     HEAPU32[(((buf) + (60)) >> 2)] = stats.bsize;
   3519     (tempI64 = [ stats.blocks >>> 0, (tempDouble = stats.blocks, (+(Math.abs(tempDouble))) >= 1 ? (tempDouble > 0 ? (+(Math.floor((tempDouble) / 4294967296))) >>> 0 : (~~((+(Math.ceil((tempDouble - +(((~~(tempDouble))) >>> 0)) / 4294967296))))) >>> 0) : 0) ], 
   3520     HEAP32[(((buf) + (8)) >> 2)] = tempI64[0], HEAP32[(((buf) + (12)) >> 2)] = tempI64[1]);
   3521     (tempI64 = [ stats.bfree >>> 0, (tempDouble = stats.bfree, (+(Math.abs(tempDouble))) >= 1 ? (tempDouble > 0 ? (+(Math.floor((tempDouble) / 4294967296))) >>> 0 : (~~((+(Math.ceil((tempDouble - +(((~~(tempDouble))) >>> 0)) / 4294967296))))) >>> 0) : 0) ], 
   3522     HEAP32[(((buf) + (16)) >> 2)] = tempI64[0], HEAP32[(((buf) + (20)) >> 2)] = tempI64[1]);
   3523     (tempI64 = [ stats.bavail >>> 0, (tempDouble = stats.bavail, (+(Math.abs(tempDouble))) >= 1 ? (tempDouble > 0 ? (+(Math.floor((tempDouble) / 4294967296))) >>> 0 : (~~((+(Math.ceil((tempDouble - +(((~~(tempDouble))) >>> 0)) / 4294967296))))) >>> 0) : 0) ], 
   3524     HEAP32[(((buf) + (24)) >> 2)] = tempI64[0], HEAP32[(((buf) + (28)) >> 2)] = tempI64[1]);
   3525     (tempI64 = [ stats.files >>> 0, (tempDouble = stats.files, (+(Math.abs(tempDouble))) >= 1 ? (tempDouble > 0 ? (+(Math.floor((tempDouble) / 4294967296))) >>> 0 : (~~((+(Math.ceil((tempDouble - +(((~~(tempDouble))) >>> 0)) / 4294967296))))) >>> 0) : 0) ], 
   3526     HEAP32[(((buf) + (32)) >> 2)] = tempI64[0], HEAP32[(((buf) + (36)) >> 2)] = tempI64[1]);
   3527     (tempI64 = [ stats.ffree >>> 0, (tempDouble = stats.ffree, (+(Math.abs(tempDouble))) >= 1 ? (tempDouble > 0 ? (+(Math.floor((tempDouble) / 4294967296))) >>> 0 : (~~((+(Math.ceil((tempDouble - +(((~~(tempDouble))) >>> 0)) / 4294967296))))) >>> 0) : 0) ], 
   3528     HEAP32[(((buf) + (40)) >> 2)] = tempI64[0], HEAP32[(((buf) + (44)) >> 2)] = tempI64[1]);
   3529     HEAPU32[(((buf) + (48)) >> 2)] = stats.fsid;
   3530     HEAPU32[(((buf) + (64)) >> 2)] = stats.flags;
   3531     // ST_NOSUID
   3532     HEAPU32[(((buf) + (56)) >> 2)] = stats.namelen;
   3533   },
   3534   doMsync(addr, stream, len, flags, offset) {
   3535     if (!FS.isFile(stream.node.mode)) {
   3536       throw new FS.ErrnoError(43);
   3537     }
   3538     if (flags & 2) {
   3539       // MAP_PRIVATE calls need not to be synced back to underlying fs
   3540       return 0;
   3541     }
   3542     var buffer = HEAPU8.slice(addr, addr + len);
   3543     FS.msync(stream, buffer, offset, len, flags);
   3544   },
   3545   getStreamFromFD(fd) {
   3546     var stream = FS.getStreamChecked(fd);
   3547     return stream;
   3548   },
   3549   varargs: undefined,
   3550   getStr(ptr) {
   3551     var ret = UTF8ToString(ptr);
   3552     return ret;
   3553   }
   3554 };

   3556 function ___syscall_dup(fd) {
   3557   try {
   3558     var old = SYSCALLS.getStreamFromFD(fd);
   3559     return FS.dupStream(old).fd;
   3560   } catch (e) {
   3561     if (typeof FS == "undefined" || !(e.name === "ErrnoError")) throw e;
   3562     return -e.errno;
   3563   }
   3564 }

   3566 function ___syscall_faccessat(dirfd, path, amode, flags) {
   3567   try {
   3568     path = SYSCALLS.getStr(path);
   3569     path = SYSCALLS.calculateAt(dirfd, path);
   3570     if (amode & ~7) {
   3571       // need a valid mode
   3572       return -28;
   3573     }
   3574     var lookup = FS.lookupPath(path, {
   3575       follow: true
   3576     });
   3577     var node = lookup.node;
   3578     if (!node) {
   3579       return -44;
   3580     }
   3581     var perms = "";
   3582     if (amode & 4) perms += "r";
   3583     if (amode & 2) perms += "w";
   3584     if (amode & 1) perms += "x";
   3585     if (perms && FS.nodePermissions(node, perms)) {
   3586       return -2;
   3587     }
   3588     return 0;
   3589   } catch (e) {
   3590     if (typeof FS == "undefined" || !(e.name === "ErrnoError")) throw e;
   3591     return -e.errno;
   3592   }
   3593 }
   3594 
   3595 var syscallGetVarargI = () => {
   3596   // the `+` prepended here is necessary to convince the JSCompiler that varargs is indeed a number.
   3597   var ret = HEAP32[((+SYSCALLS.varargs) >> 2)];
   3598   SYSCALLS.varargs += 4;
   3599   return ret;
   3600 };
   3601 
   3602 var syscallGetVarargP = syscallGetVarargI;
   3603 
   3604 function ___syscall_fcntl64(fd, cmd, varargs) {
   3605   SYSCALLS.varargs = varargs;
   3606   try {
   3607     var stream = SYSCALLS.getStreamFromFD(fd);
   3608     switch (cmd) {
   3609      case 0:
   3610       {
   3611         var arg = syscallGetVarargI();
   3612         if (arg < 0) {
   3613           return -28;
   3614         }
   3615         while (FS.streams[arg]) {
   3616           arg++;
   3617         }
   3618         var newStream;
   3619         newStream = FS.dupStream(stream, arg);
   3620         return newStream.fd;
   3621       }
   3622 
   3623      case 1:
   3624      case 2:
   3625       return 0;
   3626 
   3627      // FD_CLOEXEC makes no sense for a single process.
   3628       case 3:
   3629       return stream.flags;
   3630 
   3631      case 4:
   3632       {
   3633         var arg = syscallGetVarargI();
   3634         stream.flags |= arg;
   3635         return 0;
   3636       }
   3637 
   3638      case 12:
   3639       {
   3640         var arg = syscallGetVarargP();
   3641         var offset = 0;
   3642         // We're always unlocked.
   3643         HEAP16[(((arg) + (offset)) >> 1)] = 2;
   3644         return 0;
   3645       }
   3646 
   3647      case 13:
   3648      case 14:
   3649       // Pretend that the locking is successful. These are process-level locks,
   3650       // and Emscripten programs are a single process. If we supported linking a
   3651       // filesystem between programs, we'd need to do more here.
   3652       // See https://github.com/emscripten-core/emscripten/issues/23697
   3653       return 0;
   3654     }
   3655     return -28;
   3656   } catch (e) {
   3657     if (typeof FS == "undefined" || !(e.name === "ErrnoError")) throw e;
   3658     return -e.errno;
   3659   }
   3660 }
   3661 
   3662 function ___syscall_fstat64(fd, buf) {

   3666     if (typeof FS == "undefined" || !(e.name === "ErrnoError")) throw e;
   3667     return -e.errno;
   3668   }
   3669 }

   3671 var convertI32PairToI53Checked = (lo, hi) => ((hi + 2097152) >>> 0 < 4194305 - !!lo) ? (lo >>> 0) + hi * 4294967296 : NaN;
   3672 
   3673 function ___syscall_ftruncate64(fd, length_low, length_high) {
   3674   var length = convertI32PairToI53Checked(length_low, length_high);
   3675   try {
   3676     if (isNaN(length)) return -61;
   3677     FS.ftruncate(fd, length);
   3678     return 0;
   3679   } catch (e) {
   3680     if (typeof FS == "undefined" || !(e.name === "ErrnoError")) throw e;
   3681     return -e.errno;
   3682   }

   3685 function ___syscall_ioctl(fd, op, varargs) {
   3686   SYSCALLS.varargs = varargs;
   3687   try {
   3688     var stream = SYSCALLS.getStreamFromFD(fd);
   3689     switch (op) {
   3690      case 21509:
   3691       {
   3692         if (!stream.tty) return -59;
   3693         return 0;
   3694       }
   3695 
   3696      case 21505:
   3697       {
   3698         if (!stream.tty) return -59;
   3699         if (stream.tty.ops.ioctl_tcgets) {
   3700           var termios = stream.tty.ops.ioctl_tcgets(stream);
   3701           var argp = syscallGetVarargP();
   3702           HEAP32[((argp) >> 2)] = termios.c_iflag || 0;
   3703           HEAP32[(((argp) + (4)) >> 2)] = termios.c_oflag || 0;
   3704           HEAP32[(((argp) + (8)) >> 2)] = termios.c_cflag || 0;
   3705           HEAP32[(((argp) + (12)) >> 2)] = termios.c_lflag || 0;
   3706           for (var i = 0; i < 32; i++) {
   3707             HEAP8[(argp + i) + (17)] = termios.c_cc[i] || 0;
   3708           }
   3709           return 0;
   3710         }
   3711         return 0;
   3712       }
   3713 
   3714      case 21510:
   3715      case 21511:
   3716      case 21512:
   3717       {
   3718         if (!stream.tty) return -59;
   3719         return 0;
   3720       }
   3721 
   3722      case 21506:
   3723      case 21507:
   3724      case 21508:
   3725       {
   3726         if (!stream.tty) return -59;
   3727         if (stream.tty.ops.ioctl_tcsets) {
   3728           var argp = syscallGetVarargP();
   3729           var c_iflag = HEAP32[((argp) >> 2)];
   3730           var c_oflag = HEAP32[(((argp) + (4)) >> 2)];
   3731           var c_cflag = HEAP32[(((argp) + (8)) >> 2)];
   3732           var c_lflag = HEAP32[(((argp) + (12)) >> 2)];
   3733           var c_cc = [];
   3734           for (var i = 0; i < 32; i++) {
   3735             c_cc.push(HEAP8[(argp + i) + (17)]);
   3736           }
   3737           return stream.tty.ops.ioctl_tcsets(stream.tty, op, {
   3738             c_iflag,
   3739             c_oflag,
   3740             c_cflag,
   3741             c_lflag,
   3742             c_cc
   3743           });
   3744         }
   3745         return 0;
   3746       }
   3747 
   3748      case 21519:
   3749       {
   3750         if (!stream.tty) return -59;
   3751         var argp = syscallGetVarargP();
   3752         HEAP32[((argp) >> 2)] = 0;
   3753         return 0;
   3754       }
   3755 
   3756      case 21520:
   3757       {
   3758         if (!stream.tty) return -59;
   3759         return -28;
   3760       }
   3761 
   3762      case 21537:
   3763      case 21531:
   3764       {
   3765         var argp = syscallGetVarargP();
   3766         return FS.ioctl(stream, op, argp);
   3767       }
   3768 
   3769      case 21523:
   3770       {
   3771         // TODO: in theory we should write to the winsize struct that gets
   3772         // passed in, but for now musl doesn't read anything on it
   3773         if (!stream.tty) return -59;
   3774         if (stream.tty.ops.ioctl_tiocgwinsz) {
   3775           var winsize = stream.tty.ops.ioctl_tiocgwinsz(stream.tty);
   3776           var argp = syscallGetVarargP();
   3777           HEAP16[((argp) >> 1)] = winsize[0];
   3778           HEAP16[(((argp) + (2)) >> 1)] = winsize[1];
   3779         }
   3780         return 0;
   3781       }
   3782 
   3783      case 21524:
   3784       {
   3785         // TODO: technically, this ioctl call should change the window size.
   3786         // but, since emscripten doesn't have any concept of a terminal window
   3787         // yet, we'll just silently throw it away as we do TIOCGWINSZ
   3788         if (!stream.tty) return -59;
   3789         return 0;
   3790       }
   3791 
   3792      case 21515:
   3793       {
   3794         if (!stream.tty) return -59;
   3795         return 0;
   3796       }
   3797 
   3798      default:
   3799       return -28;
   3800     }
   3801   } catch (e) {
   3802     if (typeof FS == "undefined" || !(e.name === "ErrnoError")) throw e;
   3803     return -e.errno;
   3804   }
   3805 }

   3817 function ___syscall_newfstatat(dirfd, path, buf, flags) {
   3818   try {
   3819     path = SYSCALLS.getStr(path);
   3820     var nofollow = flags & 256;
   3821     var allowEmpty = flags & 4096;
   3822     flags = flags & (~6400);
   3823     path = SYSCALLS.calculateAt(dirfd, path, allowEmpty);
   3824     return SYSCALLS.writeStat(buf, nofollow ? FS.lstat(path) : FS.stat(path));
   3825   } catch (e) {
   3826     if (typeof FS == "undefined" || !(e.name === "ErrnoError")) throw e;
   3827     return -e.errno;
   3828   }

   3858 var AsciiToString = ptr => {
   3859   var str = "";
   3860   while (1) {
   3861     var ch = HEAPU8[ptr++];
   3862     if (!ch) return str;
   3863     str += String.fromCharCode(ch);
   3864   }
   3865 };
   3866 
   3867 var awaitingDependencies = {};
   3868 
   3869 var registeredTypes = {};
   3870 
   3871 var typeDependencies = {};
   3872 
   3873 var BindingError = class BindingError extends Error {
   3874   constructor(message) {
   3875     super(message);
   3876     this.name = "BindingError";
   3877   }
   3878 };
   3879 
   3880 var throwBindingError = message => {
   3881   throw new BindingError(message);
   3882 };
   3883 
   3884 /** @param {Object=} options */ function sharedRegisterType(rawType, registeredInstance, options = {}) {
   3885   var name = registeredInstance.name;
   3886   if (!rawType) {
   3887     throwBindingError(`type "${name}" must have a positive integer typeid pointer`);
   3888   }
   3889   if (registeredTypes.hasOwnProperty(rawType)) {
   3890     if (options.ignoreDuplicateRegistrations) {
   3891       return;
   3892     } else {
   3893       throwBindingError(`Cannot register type '${name}' twice`);
   3894     }
   3895   }
   3896   registeredTypes[rawType] = registeredInstance;
   3897   delete typeDependencies[rawType];
   3898   if (awaitingDependencies.hasOwnProperty(rawType)) {
   3899     var callbacks = awaitingDependencies[rawType];
   3900     delete awaitingDependencies[rawType];
   3901     callbacks.forEach(cb => cb());
   3902   }
   3903 }
   3904 
   3905 /** @param {Object=} options */ function registerType(rawType, registeredInstance, options = {}) {
   3906   return sharedRegisterType(rawType, registeredInstance, options);
   3907 }
   3908 
   3909 /** @suppress {globalThis} */ var __embind_register_bool = (rawType, name, trueValue, falseValue) => {
   3910   name = AsciiToString(name);
   3911   registerType(rawType, {
   3912     name,
   3913     fromWireType: function(wt) {
   3914       // ambiguous emscripten ABI: sometimes return values are
   3915       // true or false, and sometimes integers (0 or 1)
   3916       return !!wt;
   3917     },
   3918     toWireType: function(destructors, o) {
   3919       return o ? trueValue : falseValue;
   3920     },
   3921     readValueFromPointer: function(pointer) {
   3922       return this.fromWireType(HEAPU8[pointer]);
   3923     },
   3924     destructorFunction: null
   3925   });
   3926 };
   3927 
   3928 var emval_freelist = [];
   3929 
   3930 var emval_handles = [ 0, 1, , 1, null, 1, true, 1, false, 1 ];
   3931 
   3932 var __emval_decref = handle => {
   3933   if (handle > 9 && 0 === --emval_handles[handle + 1]) {
   3934     var value = emval_handles[handle];
   3935     emval_handles[handle] = undefined;
   3936     emval_freelist.push(handle);
   3937   }
   3938 };
   3939 
   3940 var Emval = {
   3941   toValue: handle => {
   3942     if (!handle) {
   3943       throwBindingError(`Cannot use deleted val. handle = ${handle}`);
   3944     }
   3945     return emval_handles[handle];
   3946   },
   3947   toHandle: value => {
   3948     switch (value) {
   3949      case undefined:
   3950       return 2;
   3951 
   3952      case null:
   3953       return 4;
   3954 
   3955      case true:
   3956       return 6;
   3957 
   3958      case false:
   3959       return 8;
   3960 
   3961      default:
   3962       {
   3963         const handle = emval_freelist.pop() || emval_handles.length;
   3964         emval_handles[handle] = value;
   3965         emval_handles[handle + 1] = 1;
   3966         return handle;
   3967       }
   3968     }
   3969   }
   3970 };
   3971 
   3972 /** @suppress {globalThis} */ function readPointer(pointer) {
   3973   return this.fromWireType(HEAPU32[((pointer) >> 2)]);
   3974 }
   3975 
   3976 var EmValType = {
   3977   name: "emscripten::val",
   3978   fromWireType: handle => {
   3979     var rv = Emval.toValue(handle);
   3980     __emval_decref(handle);
   3981     return rv;
   3982   },
   3983   toWireType: (destructors, value) => Emval.toHandle(value),
   3984   readValueFromPointer: readPointer,
   3985   destructorFunction: null
   3986 };
   3987 
   3988 var __embind_register_emval = rawType => registerType(rawType, EmValType);
   3989 
   3990 var floatReadValueFromPointer = (name, width) => {
   3991   switch (width) {
   3992    case 4:
   3993     return function(pointer) {
   3994       return this.fromWireType(HEAPF32[((pointer) >> 2)]);
   3995     };
   3996 
   3997    case 8:
   3998     return function(pointer) {
   3999       return this.fromWireType(HEAPF64[((pointer) >> 3)]);
   4000     };
   4001 
   4002    default:
   4003     throw new TypeError(`invalid float width (${width}): ${name}`);
   4004   }
   4005 };
   4006 
   4007 var __embind_register_float = (rawType, name, size) => {
   4008   name = AsciiToString(name);
   4009   registerType(rawType, {
   4010     name,
   4011     fromWireType: value => value,
   4012     toWireType: (destructors, value) => value,
   4013     readValueFromPointer: floatReadValueFromPointer(name, size),
   4014     destructorFunction: null
   4015   });
   4016 };
   4017 
   4018 var integerReadValueFromPointer = (name, width, signed) => {
   4019   // integers are quite common, so generate very specialized functions
   4020   switch (width) {
   4021    case 1:
   4022     return signed ? pointer => HEAP8[pointer] : pointer => HEAPU8[pointer];
   4023 
   4024    case 2:
   4025     return signed ? pointer => HEAP16[((pointer) >> 1)] : pointer => HEAPU16[((pointer) >> 1)];
   4026 
   4027    case 4:
   4028     return signed ? pointer => HEAP32[((pointer) >> 2)] : pointer => HEAPU32[((pointer) >> 2)];
   4029 
   4030    default:
   4031     throw new TypeError(`invalid integer width (${width}): ${name}`);
   4032   }
   4033 };
   4034 
   4035 /** @suppress {globalThis} */ var __embind_register_integer = (primitiveType, name, size, minRange, maxRange) => {
   4036   name = AsciiToString(name);
   4037   const isUnsignedType = minRange === 0;
   4038   let fromWireType = value => value;
   4039   if (isUnsignedType) {
   4040     var bitshift = 32 - 8 * size;
   4041     fromWireType = value => (value << bitshift) >>> bitshift;
   4042     maxRange = fromWireType(maxRange);
   4043   }
   4044   registerType(primitiveType, {
   4045     name,
   4046     fromWireType,
   4047     toWireType: (destructors, value) => value,
   4048     readValueFromPointer: integerReadValueFromPointer(name, size, minRange !== 0),
   4049     destructorFunction: null
   4050   });
   4051 };
   4052 
   4053 var __embind_register_memory_view = (rawType, dataTypeIndex, name) => {
   4054   var typeMapping = [ Int8Array, Uint8Array, Int16Array, Uint16Array, Int32Array, Uint32Array, Float32Array, Float64Array ];
   4055   var TA = typeMapping[dataTypeIndex];
   4056   function decodeMemoryView(handle) {
   4057     var size = HEAPU32[((handle) >> 2)];
   4058     var data = HEAPU32[(((handle) + (4)) >> 2)];
   4059     return new TA(HEAP8.buffer, data, size);
   4060   }
   4061   name = AsciiToString(name);
   4062   registerType(rawType, {
   4063     name,
   4064     fromWireType: decodeMemoryView,
   4065     readValueFromPointer: decodeMemoryView
   4066   }, {
   4067     ignoreDuplicateRegistrations: true
   4068   });
   4069 };
