   4071 var stringToUTF8 = (str, outPtr, maxBytesToWrite) => stringToUTF8Array(str, HEAPU8, outPtr, maxBytesToWrite);
   4072 
   4073 var __embind_register_std_string = (rawType, name) => {
   4074   name = AsciiToString(name);
   4075   var stdStringIsUTF8 = true;
   4076   registerType(rawType, {
   4077     name,
   4078     // For some method names we use string keys here since they are part of
   4079     // the public/external API and/or used by the runtime-generated code.
   4080     fromWireType(value) {
   4081       var length = HEAPU32[((value) >> 2)];
   4082       var payload = value + 4;
   4083       var str;
   4084       if (stdStringIsUTF8) {
   4085         str = UTF8ToString(payload, length, true);
   4086       } else {
   4087         str = "";
   4088         for (var i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
   4089           str += String.fromCharCode(HEAPU8[payload + i]);
   4090         }
   4091       }
   4092       _free(value);
   4093       return str;
   4094     },
   4095     toWireType(destructors, value) {
   4096       if (value instanceof ArrayBuffer) {
   4097         value = new Uint8Array(value);
   4098       }
   4099       var length;
   4100       var valueIsOfTypeString = (typeof value == "string");
   4101       // We accept `string` or array views with single byte elements
   4102       if (!(valueIsOfTypeString || (ArrayBuffer.isView(value) && value.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT == 1))) {
   4103         throwBindingError("Cannot pass non-string to std::string");
   4104       }
   4105       if (stdStringIsUTF8 && valueIsOfTypeString) {
   4106         length = lengthBytesUTF8(value);
   4107       } else {
   4108         length = value.length;
   4109       }
   4110       // assumes POINTER_SIZE alignment
   4111       var base = _malloc(4 + length + 1);
   4112       var ptr = base + 4;
   4113       HEAPU32[((base) >> 2)] = length;
   4114       if (valueIsOfTypeString) {
   4115         if (stdStringIsUTF8) {
   4116           stringToUTF8(value, ptr, length + 1);
   4117         } else {
   4118           for (var i = 0; i < length; ++i) {
   4119             var charCode = value.charCodeAt(i);
   4120             if (charCode > 255) {
   4121               _free(base);
   4122               throwBindingError("String has UTF-16 code units that do not fit in 8 bits");
   4123             }
   4124             HEAPU8[ptr + i] = charCode;
   4125           }
   4126         }
   4127       } else {
   4128         HEAPU8.set(value, ptr);
   4129       }
   4130       if (destructors !== null) {
   4131         destructors.push(_free, base);
   4132       }
   4133       return base;
   4134     },
   4135     readValueFromPointer: readPointer,
   4136     destructorFunction(ptr) {
   4137       _free(ptr);
   4138     }
   4139   });
   4140 };
   4141 
   4142 var UTF16Decoder = new TextDecoder("utf-16le");
   4143 
   4144 var UTF16ToString = (ptr, maxBytesToRead, ignoreNul) => {
   4145   var idx = ((ptr) >> 1);
   4146   var endIdx = findStringEnd(HEAPU16, idx, maxBytesToRead / 2, ignoreNul);
   4147   return UTF16Decoder.decode(HEAPU16.subarray(idx, endIdx));
   4148 };
   4149 
   4150 var stringToUTF16 = (str, outPtr, maxBytesToWrite) => {
   4151   // Backwards compatibility: if max bytes is not specified, assume unsafe unbounded write is allowed.
   4152   maxBytesToWrite ??= 2147483647;
   4153   if (maxBytesToWrite < 2) return 0;
   4154   maxBytesToWrite -= 2;
   4155   // Null terminator.
   4156   var startPtr = outPtr;
   4157   var numCharsToWrite = (maxBytesToWrite < str.length * 2) ? (maxBytesToWrite / 2) : str.length;
   4158   for (var i = 0; i < numCharsToWrite; ++i) {
   4159     // charCodeAt returns a UTF-16 encoded code unit, so it can be directly written to the HEAP.
   4160     var codeUnit = str.charCodeAt(i);
   4161     // possibly a lead surrogate
   4162     HEAP16[((outPtr) >> 1)] = codeUnit;
   4163     outPtr += 2;
   4164   }
   4165   // Null-terminate the pointer to the HEAP.
   4166   HEAP16[((outPtr) >> 1)] = 0;
   4167   return outPtr - startPtr;
   4168 };
   4169 
   4170 var lengthBytesUTF16 = str => str.length * 2;
   4171 
   4172 var UTF32ToString = (ptr, maxBytesToRead, ignoreNul) => {
   4173   var str = "";
   4174   var startIdx = ((ptr) >> 2);
   4175   // If maxBytesToRead is not passed explicitly, it will be undefined, and this
   4176   // will always evaluate to true. This saves on code size.
   4177   for (var i = 0; !(i >= maxBytesToRead / 4); i++) {
   4178     var utf32 = HEAPU32[startIdx + i];
   4179     if (!utf32 && !ignoreNul) break;
   4180     str += String.fromCodePoint(utf32);
   4181   }
   4182   return str;
   4183 };
   4184 
   4185 var stringToUTF32 = (str, outPtr, maxBytesToWrite) => {
   4186   // Backwards compatibility: if max bytes is not specified, assume unsafe unbounded write is allowed.
   4187   maxBytesToWrite ??= 2147483647;
   4188   if (maxBytesToWrite < 4) return 0;
   4189   var startPtr = outPtr;
   4190   var endPtr = startPtr + maxBytesToWrite - 4;
   4191   for (var i = 0; i < str.length; ++i) {
   4192     var codePoint = str.codePointAt(i);
   4193     // Gotcha: if codePoint is over 0xFFFF, it is represented as a surrogate pair in UTF-16.
   4194     // We need to manually skip over the second code unit for correct iteration.
   4195     if (codePoint > 65535) {
   4196       i++;
   4197     }
   4198     HEAP32[((outPtr) >> 2)] = codePoint;
   4199     outPtr += 4;
   4200     if (outPtr + 4 > endPtr) break;
   4201   }
   4202   // Null-terminate the pointer to the HEAP.
   4203   HEAP32[((outPtr) >> 2)] = 0;
   4204   return outPtr - startPtr;
   4205 };
   4206 
   4207 var lengthBytesUTF32 = str => {
   4208   var len = 0;
   4209   for (var i = 0; i < str.length; ++i) {
   4210     var codePoint = str.codePointAt(i);
   4211     // Gotcha: if codePoint is over 0xFFFF, it is represented as a surrogate pair in UTF-16.
   4212     // We need to manually skip over the second code unit for correct iteration.
   4213     if (codePoint > 65535) {
   4214       i++;
   4215     }
   4216     len += 4;
   4217   }
   4218   return len;
   4219 };
   4220 
   4221 var __embind_register_std_wstring = (rawType, charSize, name) => {
   4222   name = AsciiToString(name);
   4223   var decodeString, encodeString, lengthBytesUTF;
   4224   if (charSize === 2) {
   4225     decodeString = UTF16ToString;
   4226     encodeString = stringToUTF16;
   4227     lengthBytesUTF = lengthBytesUTF16;
   4228   } else {
   4229     decodeString = UTF32ToString;
   4230     encodeString = stringToUTF32;
   4231     lengthBytesUTF = lengthBytesUTF32;
   4232   }
   4233   registerType(rawType, {
   4234     name,
   4235     fromWireType: value => {
   4236       // Code mostly taken from _embind_register_std_string fromWireType
   4237       var length = HEAPU32[((value) >> 2)];
   4238       var str = decodeString(value + 4, length * charSize, true);
   4239       _free(value);
   4240       return str;
   4241     },
   4242     toWireType: (destructors, value) => {
   4243       if (!(typeof value == "string")) {
   4244         throwBindingError(`Cannot pass non-string to C++ string type ${name}`);
   4245       }
   4246       // assumes POINTER_SIZE alignment
   4247       var length = lengthBytesUTF(value);
   4248       var ptr = _malloc(4 + length + charSize);
   4249       HEAPU32[((ptr) >> 2)] = length / charSize;
   4250       encodeString(value, ptr + 4, length + charSize);
   4251       if (destructors !== null) {
   4252         destructors.push(_free, ptr);
   4253       }
   4254       return ptr;
   4255     },
   4256     readValueFromPointer: readPointer,
   4257     destructorFunction(ptr) {
   4258       _free(ptr);
   4259     }
   4260   });
   4261 };

   4263 var __embind_register_void = (rawType, name) => {

   4269     fromWireType: () => undefined,
   4270     // TODO: assert if anything else is given?
   4271     toWireType: (destructors, o) => undefined
   4272   });
   4273 };

   4310 var emval_returnValue = (toReturnWire, destructorsRef, handle) => {
   4311   var destructors = [];
   4312   var result = toReturnWire(destructors, handle);
   4313   if (destructors.length) {
   4314     // void, primitives and any other types w/o destructors don't need to allocate a handle
   4315     HEAPU32[((destructorsRef) >> 2)] = Emval.toHandle(destructors);
   4316   }
   4317   return result;
   4318 };

   4330 var __emval_create_invoker = (argCount, argTypesPtr, kind) => {
   4331   var GenericWireTypeSize = 8;
   4332   var [retType, ...argTypes] = emval_lookupTypes(argCount, argTypesPtr);
   4333   var toReturnWire = retType.toWireType.bind(retType);
   4334   var argFromPtr = argTypes.map(type => type.readValueFromPointer.bind(type));
   4335   argCount--;
   4336   // remove the extracted return type
   4337   var argN = new Array(argCount);
   4338   var invokerFunction = (handle, methodName, destructorsRef, args) => {
   4339     var offset = 0;
   4340     for (var i = 0; i < argCount; ++i) {
   4341       argN[i] = argFromPtr[i](args + offset);
   4342       offset += GenericWireTypeSize;
   4343     }
   4344     var rv;
   4345     switch (kind) {
   4346      case 0:
   4347       rv = Emval.toValue(handle).apply(null, argN);
   4348       break;
   4349 
   4350      case 2:
   4351       rv = Reflect.construct(Emval.toValue(handle), argN);
   4352       break;
   4353 
   4354      case 3:
   4355       // no-op, just return the argument
   4356       rv = argN[0];
   4357       break;
   4358 
   4359      case 1:
   4360       rv = Emval.toValue(handle)[getStringOrSymbol(methodName)](...argN);
   4361       break;
   4362     }
   4363     return emval_returnValue(toReturnWire, destructorsRef, rv);
   4364   };
   4365   var functionName = `methodCaller<(${argTypes.map(t => t.name)}) => ${retType.name}>`;
   4366   return emval_addMethodCaller(createNamedFunction(functionName, invokerFunction));
   4367 };

   4399 var runDestructors = destructors => {

   4401     var ptr = destructors.pop();
   4402     var del = destructors.pop();

   4404   }
   4405 };

   4425 function __gmtime_js(time_low, time_high, tmPtr) {
   4426   var time = convertI32PairToI53Checked(time_low, time_high);
   4427   var date = new Date(time * 1e3);
   4428   HEAP32[((tmPtr) >> 2)] = date.getUTCSeconds();
   4429   HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (4)) >> 2)] = date.getUTCMinutes();
   4430   HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (8)) >> 2)] = date.getUTCHours();
   4431   HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (12)) >> 2)] = date.getUTCDate();
   4432   HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (16)) >> 2)] = date.getUTCMonth();
   4433   HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (20)) >> 2)] = date.getUTCFullYear() - 1900;
   4434   HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (24)) >> 2)] = date.getUTCDay();
   4435   var start = Date.UTC(date.getUTCFullYear(), 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0);
   4436   var yday = ((date.getTime() - start) / (1e3 * 60 * 60 * 24)) | 0;
   4437   HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (28)) >> 2)] = yday;
   4438 }
   4439 
   4440 var isLeapYear = year => year % 4 === 0 && (year % 100 !== 0 || year % 400 === 0);
   4441 
   4442 var MONTH_DAYS_LEAP_CUMULATIVE = [ 0, 31, 60, 91, 121, 152, 182, 213, 244, 274, 305, 335 ];
   4443 
   4444 var MONTH_DAYS_REGULAR_CUMULATIVE = [ 0, 31, 59, 90, 120, 151, 181, 212, 243, 273, 304, 334 ];
   4445 
   4446 var ydayFromDate = date => {
   4447   var leap = isLeapYear(date.getFullYear());
   4448   var monthDaysCumulative = (leap ? MONTH_DAYS_LEAP_CUMULATIVE : MONTH_DAYS_REGULAR_CUMULATIVE);
   4449   var yday = monthDaysCumulative[date.getMonth()] + date.getDate() - 1;
   4450   // -1 since it's days since Jan 1
   4451   return yday;
   4452 };
   4453 
   4454 function __localtime_js(time_low, time_high, tmPtr) {
   4455   var time = convertI32PairToI53Checked(time_low, time_high);
   4456   var date = new Date(time * 1e3);
   4457   HEAP32[((tmPtr) >> 2)] = date.getSeconds();
   4458   HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (4)) >> 2)] = date.getMinutes();
   4459   HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (8)) >> 2)] = date.getHours();
   4460   HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (12)) >> 2)] = date.getDate();
   4461   HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (16)) >> 2)] = date.getMonth();
   4462   HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (20)) >> 2)] = date.getFullYear() - 1900;
   4463   HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (24)) >> 2)] = date.getDay();
   4464   var yday = ydayFromDate(date) | 0;
   4465   HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (28)) >> 2)] = yday;
   4466   HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (36)) >> 2)] = -(date.getTimezoneOffset() * 60);
   4467   // Attention: DST is in December in South, and some regions don't have DST at all.
   4468   var start = new Date(date.getFullYear(), 0, 1);
   4469   var summerOffset = new Date(date.getFullYear(), 6, 1).getTimezoneOffset();
   4470   var winterOffset = start.getTimezoneOffset();
   4471   var dst = (summerOffset != winterOffset && date.getTimezoneOffset() == Math.min(winterOffset, summerOffset)) | 0;
   4472   HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (32)) >> 2)] = dst;
   4473 }

   4475 var setTempRet0 = val => __emscripten_tempret_set(val);
   4476 
   4477 var __mktime_js = function(tmPtr) {
   4478   var ret = (() => {
   4479     var date = new Date(HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (20)) >> 2)] + 1900, HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (16)) >> 2)], HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (12)) >> 2)], HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (8)) >> 2)], HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (4)) >> 2)], HEAP32[((tmPtr) >> 2)], 0);
   4480     // There's an ambiguous hour when the time goes back; the tm_isdst field is
   4481     // used to disambiguate it.  Date() basically guesses, so we fix it up if it
   4482     // guessed wrong, or fill in tm_isdst with the guess if it's -1.
   4483     var dst = HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (32)) >> 2)];
   4484     var guessedOffset = date.getTimezoneOffset();
   4485     var start = new Date(date.getFullYear(), 0, 1);
   4486     var summerOffset = new Date(date.getFullYear(), 6, 1).getTimezoneOffset();
   4487     var winterOffset = start.getTimezoneOffset();
   4488     var dstOffset = Math.min(winterOffset, summerOffset);
   4489     // DST is in December in South
   4490     if (dst < 0) {
   4491       // Attention: some regions don't have DST at all.
   4492       HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (32)) >> 2)] = Number(summerOffset != winterOffset && dstOffset == guessedOffset);
   4493     } else if ((dst > 0) != (dstOffset == guessedOffset)) {
   4494       var nonDstOffset = Math.max(winterOffset, summerOffset);
   4495       var trueOffset = dst > 0 ? dstOffset : nonDstOffset;
   4496       // Don't try setMinutes(date.getMinutes() + ...) -- it's messed up.
   4497       date.setTime(date.getTime() + (trueOffset - guessedOffset) * 6e4);
   4498     }
   4499     HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (24)) >> 2)] = date.getDay();
   4500     var yday = ydayFromDate(date) | 0;
   4501     HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (28)) >> 2)] = yday;
   4502     // To match expected behavior, update fields from date
   4503     HEAP32[((tmPtr) >> 2)] = date.getSeconds();
   4504     HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (4)) >> 2)] = date.getMinutes();
   4505     HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (8)) >> 2)] = date.getHours();
   4506     HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (12)) >> 2)] = date.getDate();
   4507     HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (16)) >> 2)] = date.getMonth();
   4508     HEAP32[(((tmPtr) + (20)) >> 2)] = date.getYear();
   4509     var timeMs = date.getTime();
   4510     if (isNaN(timeMs)) {
   4511       return -1;
   4512     }
   4513     // Return time in microseconds
   4514     return timeMs / 1e3;
   4515   })();
   4516   return (setTempRet0((tempDouble = ret, (+(Math.abs(tempDouble))) >= 1 ? (tempDouble > 0 ? (+(Math.floor((tempDouble) / 4294967296))) >>> 0 : (~~((+(Math.ceil((tempDouble - +(((~~(tempDouble))) >>> 0)) / 4294967296))))) >>> 0) : 0)), 
   4517   ret >>> 0);
   4518 };
   4519 
   4520 function __mmap_js(len, prot, flags, fd, offset_low, offset_high, allocated, addr) {
   4521   var offset = convertI32PairToI53Checked(offset_low, offset_high);
   4522   try {
   4523     var stream = SYSCALLS.getStreamFromFD(fd);
   4524     var res = FS.mmap(stream, len, offset, prot, flags);
   4525     var ptr = res.ptr;
   4526     HEAP32[((allocated) >> 2)] = res.allocated;
   4527     HEAPU32[((addr) >> 2)] = ptr;
   4528     return 0;
   4529   } catch (e) {
   4530     if (typeof FS == "undefined" || !(e.name === "ErrnoError")) throw e;
   4531     return -e.errno;
   4532   }
   4533 }
   4534 
   4535 function __munmap_js(addr, len, prot, flags, fd, offset_low, offset_high) {
   4536   var offset = convertI32PairToI53Checked(offset_low, offset_high);
   4537   try {
   4538     var stream = SYSCALLS.getStreamFromFD(fd);
   4539     if (prot & 2) {
   4540       SYSCALLS.doMsync(addr, stream, len, flags, offset);
   4541     }
   4542   } catch (e) {
   4543     if (typeof FS == "undefined" || !(e.name === "ErrnoError")) throw e;
   4544     return -e.errno;
   4545   }
   4546 }
   4547 
   4548 var __tzset_js = (timezone, daylight, std_name, dst_name) => {
   4549   // TODO: Use (malleable) environment variables instead of system settings.
   4550   var currentYear = (new Date).getFullYear();
   4551   var winter = new Date(currentYear, 0, 1);
   4552   var summer = new Date(currentYear, 6, 1);
   4553   var winterOffset = winter.getTimezoneOffset();
   4554   var summerOffset = summer.getTimezoneOffset();
   4555   // Local standard timezone offset. Local standard time is not adjusted for
   4556   // daylight savings.  This code uses the fact that getTimezoneOffset returns
   4557   // a greater value during Standard Time versus Daylight Saving Time (DST).
   4558   // Thus it determines the expected output during Standard Time, and it
   4559   // compares whether the output of the given date the same (Standard) or less
   4560   // (DST).
   4561   var stdTimezoneOffset = Math.max(winterOffset, summerOffset);
   4562   // timezone is specified as seconds west of UTC ("The external variable
   4563   // `timezone` shall be set to the difference, in seconds, between
   4564   // Coordinated Universal Time (UTC) and local standard time."), the same
   4565   // as returned by stdTimezoneOffset.
   4566   // See http://pubs.opengroup.org/onlinepubs/009695399/functions/tzset.html
   4567   HEAPU32[((timezone) >> 2)] = stdTimezoneOffset * 60;
   4568   HEAP32[((daylight) >> 2)] = Number(winterOffset != summerOffset);
   4569   var extractZone = timezoneOffset => {
   4570     // Why inverse sign?
   4571     // Read here https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Date/getTimezoneOffset
   4572     var sign = timezoneOffset >= 0 ? "-" : "+";
   4573     var absOffset = Math.abs(timezoneOffset);
   4574     var hours = String(Math.floor(absOffset / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
   4575     var minutes = String(absOffset % 60).padStart(2, "0");
   4576     return `UTC${sign}${hours}${minutes}`;
   4577   };
   4578   var winterName = extractZone(winterOffset);
   4579   var summerName = extractZone(summerOffset);
   4580   if (summerOffset < winterOffset) {
   4581     // Northern hemisphere
   4582     stringToUTF8(winterName, std_name, 17);
   4583     stringToUTF8(summerName, dst_name, 17);
   4584   } else {
   4585     stringToUTF8(winterName, dst_name, 17);
   4586     stringToUTF8(summerName, std_name, 17);
   4587   }
   4588 };
   4589 
   4590 var _emscripten_get_now = () => performance.now();
   4591 
   4592 var _emscripten_date_now = () => Date.now();
   4593 
   4594 var nowIsMonotonic = 1;
   4595 
   4596 var checkWasiClock = clock_id => clock_id >= 0 && clock_id <= 3;
   4597 
   4598 function _clock_time_get(clk_id, ignored_precision_low, ignored_precision_high, ptime) {
   4599   var ignored_precision = convertI32PairToI53Checked(ignored_precision_low, ignored_precision_high);
   4600   if (!checkWasiClock(clk_id)) {
   4601     return 28;
   4602   }
   4603   var now;
   4604   // all wasi clocks but realtime are monotonic
   4605   if (clk_id === 0) {
   4606     now = _emscripten_date_now();
   4607   } else if (nowIsMonotonic) {
   4608     now = _emscripten_get_now();
   4609   } else {
   4610     return 52;
   4611   }
   4612   // "now" is in ms, and wasi times are in ns.
   4613   var nsec = Math.round(now * 1e3 * 1e3);
   4614   (tempI64 = [ nsec >>> 0, (tempDouble = nsec, (+(Math.abs(tempDouble))) >= 1 ? (tempDouble > 0 ? (+(Math.floor((tempDouble) / 4294967296))) >>> 0 : (~~((+(Math.ceil((tempDouble - +(((~~(tempDouble))) >>> 0)) / 4294967296))))) >>> 0) : 0) ], 
   4615   HEAP32[((ptime) >> 2)] = tempI64[0], HEAP32[(((ptime) + (4)) >> 2)] = tempI64[1]);
   4616   return 0;
   4617 }
   4618 
   4619 var readEmAsmArgsArray = [];
   4620 
   4621 var readEmAsmArgs = (sigPtr, buf) => {
   4622   readEmAsmArgsArray.length = 0;
   4623   var ch;
   4624   // Most arguments are i32s, so shift the buffer pointer so it is a plain
   4625   // index into HEAP32.
   4626   while (ch = HEAPU8[sigPtr++]) {
   4627     // Floats are always passed as doubles, so all types except for 'i'
   4628     // are 8 bytes and require alignment.
   4629     var wide = (ch != 105);
   4630     wide &= (ch != 112);
   4631     buf += wide && (buf % 8) ? 4 : 0;
   4632     readEmAsmArgsArray.push(// Special case for pointers under wasm64 or CAN_ADDRESS_2GB mode.
   4633     ch == 112 ? HEAPU32[((buf) >> 2)] : ch == 105 ? HEAP32[((buf) >> 2)] : HEAPF64[((buf) >> 3)]);
   4634     buf += wide ? 8 : 4;
   4635   }
   4636   return readEmAsmArgsArray;
   4637 };
   4638 
   4639 var runEmAsmFunction = (code, sigPtr, argbuf) => {
   4640   var args = readEmAsmArgs(sigPtr, argbuf);
   4641   return ASM_CONSTS[code](...args);
   4642 };

   4644 var _emscripten_asm_const_int = (code, sigPtr, argbuf) => runEmAsmFunction(code, sigPtr, argbuf);
   4645 
   4646 var _emscripten_asm_const_ptr = (code, sigPtr, argbuf) => runEmAsmFunction(code, sigPtr, argbuf);

   4648 var _emscripten_errn = (str, len) => err(UTF8ToString(str, len));

   4650 var getHeapMax = () => // Stay one Wasm page short of 4GB: while e.g. Chrome is able to allocate
   4651 // full 4GB Wasm memories, the size will wrap back to 0 bytes in Wasm side
   4652 // for any code that deals with heap sizes, which would require special
   4653 // casing all heap size related code to treat 0 specially.
   4654 2147483648;
   4655 
   4656 var _emscripten_get_heap_max = () => getHeapMax();
   4657 
   4658 var _emscripten_has_asyncify = () => 0;
   4659 
   4660 var _emscripten_outn = (str, len) => out(UTF8ToString(str, len));
   4661 
   4662 var UNWIND_CACHE = {};
   4663 
   4664 var stringToNewUTF8 = str => {
   4665   var size = lengthBytesUTF8(str) + 1;
   4666   var ret = _malloc(size);
   4667   if (ret) stringToUTF8(str, ret, size);
   4668   return ret;
   4669 };
   4670 
   4671 /** @returns {number} */ var convertFrameToPC = frame => {
   4672   var match;
   4673   if (match = /\bwasm-function\[\d+\]:(0x[0-9a-f]+)/.exec(frame)) {
   4674     // Wasm engines give the binary offset directly, so we use that as return address
   4675     return +match[1];
   4676   } else if (match = /:(\d+):\d+(?:\)|$)/.exec(frame)) {
   4677     // If we are in js, we can use the js line number as the "return address".
   4678     // This should work for wasm2js.  We tag the high bit to distinguish this
   4679     // from wasm addresses.
   4680     return 2147483648 | +match[1];
   4681   }
   4682   // return 0 if we can't find any
   4683   return 0;
   4684 };
   4685 
   4686 var saveInUnwindCache = callstack => {
   4687   for (var line of callstack) {
   4688     var pc = convertFrameToPC(line);
   4689     if (pc) {
   4690       UNWIND_CACHE[pc] = line;
   4691     }
   4692   }
   4693 };
   4694 
   4695 var jsStackTrace = () => (new Error).stack.toString();
   4696 
   4697 var _emscripten_stack_snapshot = () => {
   4698   var callstack = jsStackTrace().split("\n");
   4699   if (callstack[0] == "Error") {
   4700     callstack.shift();
   4701   }
   4702   saveInUnwindCache(callstack);
   4703   // Caches the stack snapshot so that emscripten_stack_unwind_buffer() can
   4704   // unwind from this spot.
   4705   UNWIND_CACHE.last_addr = convertFrameToPC(callstack[3]);
   4706   UNWIND_CACHE.last_stack = callstack;
   4707   return UNWIND_CACHE.last_addr;
   4708 };
   4709 
   4710 var _emscripten_pc_get_function = pc => {
   4711   var frame = UNWIND_CACHE[pc];
   4712   if (!frame) return 0;
   4713   var name;
   4714   var match;
   4715   // First try to match foo.wasm.sym files explcitly. e.g.
   4716   //   at test_return_address.wasm.main (wasm://wasm/test_return_address.wasm-0012cc2a:wasm-function[26]:0x9f3
   4717   // Then match JS symbols which don't include that module name:
   4718   //   at invokeEntryPoint (.../test_return_address.js:1500:42)
   4719   // Finally match firefox format:
   4720   //   Object._main@http://server.com:4324:12'
   4721   if (match = /^\s+at .*\.wasm\.(.*) \(.*\)$/.exec(frame)) {
   4722     name = match[1];
   4723   } else if (match = /^\s+at (.*) \(.*\)$/.exec(frame)) {
   4724     name = match[1];
   4725   } else if (match = /^(.+?)@/.exec(frame)) {
   4726     name = match[1];
   4727   } else {
   4728     return 0;
   4729   }
   4730   _free(_emscripten_pc_get_function.ret ?? 0);
   4731   _emscripten_pc_get_function.ret = stringToNewUTF8(name);
   4732   return _emscripten_pc_get_function.ret;
   4733 };
   4734 
   4735 var growMemory = size => {
   4736   var oldHeapSize = wasmMemory.buffer.byteLength;
   4737   var pages = ((size - oldHeapSize + 65535) / 65536) | 0;
   4738   try {
   4739     // round size grow request up to wasm page size (fixed 64KB per spec)
   4740     wasmMemory.grow(pages);
   4741     // .grow() takes a delta compared to the previous size
   4742     updateMemoryViews();
   4743     return 1;
   4744   } catch (e) {}
   4745 };
   4746 
   4747 var _emscripten_resize_heap = requestedSize => {
   4748   var oldSize = HEAPU8.length;
   4749   // With CAN_ADDRESS_2GB or MEMORY64, pointers are already unsigned.
   4750   requestedSize >>>= 0;
   4751   // With multithreaded builds, races can happen (another thread might increase the size
   4752   // in between), so return a failure, and let the caller retry.
   4753   // Memory resize rules:
   4754   // 1.  Always increase heap size to at least the requested size, rounded up
   4755   //     to next page multiple.
   4756   // 2a. If MEMORY_GROWTH_LINEAR_STEP == -1, excessively resize the heap
   4757   //     geometrically: increase the heap size according to
   4758   //     MEMORY_GROWTH_GEOMETRIC_STEP factor (default +20%), At most
   4759   //     overreserve by MEMORY_GROWTH_GEOMETRIC_CAP bytes (default 96MB).
   4760   // 2b. If MEMORY_GROWTH_LINEAR_STEP != -1, excessively resize the heap
   4761   //     linearly: increase the heap size by at least
   4762   //     MEMORY_GROWTH_LINEAR_STEP bytes.
   4763   // 3.  Max size for the heap is capped at 2048MB-WASM_PAGE_SIZE, or by
   4764   //     MAXIMUM_MEMORY, or by ASAN limit, depending on which is smallest
   4765   // 4.  If we were unable to allocate as much memory, it may be due to
   4766   //     over-eager decision to excessively reserve due to (3) above.
   4767   //     Hence if an allocation fails, cut down on the amount of excess
   4768   //     growth, in an attempt to succeed to perform a smaller allocation.
   4769   // A limit is set for how much we can grow. We should not exceed that
   4770   // (the wasm binary specifies it, so if we tried, we'd fail anyhow).
   4771   var maxHeapSize = getHeapMax();
   4772   if (requestedSize > maxHeapSize) {
   4773     return false;
   4774   }
   4775   // Loop through potential heap size increases. If we attempt a too eager
   4776   // reservation that fails, cut down on the attempted size and reserve a
   4777   // smaller bump instead. (max 3 times, chosen somewhat arbitrarily)
   4778   for (var cutDown = 1; cutDown <= 4; cutDown *= 2) {
   4779     var overGrownHeapSize = oldSize * (1 + .2 / cutDown);
   4780     // ensure geometric growth
   4781     // but limit overreserving (default to capping at +96MB overgrowth at most)
   4782     overGrownHeapSize = Math.min(overGrownHeapSize, requestedSize + 100663296);
   4783     var newSize = Math.min(maxHeapSize, alignMemory(Math.max(requestedSize, overGrownHeapSize), 65536));
   4784     var replacement = growMemory(newSize);
   4785     if (replacement) {
   4786       return true;
   4787     }
   4788   }
   4789   return false;
   4790 };
   4791 
   4792 var _emscripten_stack_unwind_buffer = (addr, buffer, count) => {
   4793   var stack;
   4794   if (UNWIND_CACHE.last_addr == addr) {
   4795     stack = UNWIND_CACHE.last_stack;
   4796   } else {
   4797     stack = jsStackTrace().split("\n");
   4798     if (stack[0] == "Error") {
   4799       stack.shift();
   4800     }
   4801     saveInUnwindCache(stack);
   4802   }
   4803   var offset = 3;
   4804   while (stack[offset] && convertFrameToPC(stack[offset]) != addr) {
   4805     ++offset;
   4806   }
   4807   for (var i = 0; i < count && stack[i + offset]; ++i) {
   4808     HEAP32[(((buffer) + (i * 4)) >> 2)] = convertFrameToPC(stack[i + offset]);
   4809   }
   4810   return i;
   4811 };

   4815 var webgl_enable_ANGLE_instanced_arrays = ctx => {
   4816   // Extension available in WebGL 1 from Firefox 26 and Google Chrome 30 onwards. Core feature in WebGL 2.
   4817   var ext = ctx.getExtension("ANGLE_instanced_arrays");
   4818   // Because this extension is a core function in WebGL 2, assign the extension entry points in place of
   4819   // where the core functions will reside in WebGL 2. This way the calling code can call these without
   4820   // having to dynamically branch depending if running against WebGL 1 or WebGL 2.

   4823     ctx["drawArraysInstanced"] = (mode, first, count, primcount) => ext["drawArraysInstancedANGLE"](mode, first, count, primcount);
   4824     ctx["drawElementsInstanced"] = (mode, count, type, indices, primcount) => ext["drawElementsInstancedANGLE"](mode, count, type, indices, primcount);
   4825     return 1;
   4826   }
   4827 };

   4864 var getEmscriptenSupportedExtensions = ctx => {

   4880 var GL = {
   4881   counter: 1,
   4882   buffers: [],
   4883   mappedBuffers: {},
   4884   programs: [],
   4885   framebuffers: [],
   4886   renderbuffers: [],
   4887   textures: [],
   4888   shaders: [],
   4889   vaos: [],
   4890   contexts: [],
   4891   offscreenCanvases: {},
   4892   queries: [],
   4893   samplers: [],
   4894   transformFeedbacks: [],
   4895   syncs: [],
   4896   byteSizeByTypeRoot: 5120,
   4897   byteSizeByType: [ 1, 1, 2, 2, 4, 4, 4, 2, 3, 4, 8 ],
   4898   stringCache: {},
   4899   stringiCache: {},
   4900   unpackAlignment: 4,
   4901   unpackRowLength: 0,
   4902   recordError: errorCode => {
   4903     if (!GL.lastError) {
   4904       GL.lastError = errorCode;
   4905     }
   4906   },
   4907   getNewId: table => {
   4908     var ret = GL.counter++;
   4909     for (var i = table.length; i < ret; i++) {
   4910       table[i] = null;
   4911     }
   4912     // Skip over any non-null elements that might have been created by
   4913     // glBindBuffer.
   4914     while (table[ret]) {
   4915       ret = GL.counter++;
   4916     }
   4917     return ret;
   4918   },
   4919   genObject: (n, buffers, createFunction, objectTable) => {
   4920     for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   4921       var buffer = GLctx[createFunction]();
   4922       var id = buffer && GL.getNewId(objectTable);
   4923       if (buffer) {
   4924         buffer.name = id;
   4925         objectTable[id] = buffer;
   4926       } else {
   4927         GL.recordError(1282);
   4928       }
   4929       HEAP32[(((buffers) + (i * 4)) >> 2)] = id;
   4930     }
   4931   },
   4932   MAX_TEMP_BUFFER_SIZE: 2097152,
   4933   numTempVertexBuffersPerSize: 64,
   4934   log2ceilLookup: i => 32 - Math.clz32(i === 0 ? 0 : i - 1),
   4935   generateTempBuffers: (quads, context) => {
   4936     var largestIndex = GL.log2ceilLookup(GL.MAX_TEMP_BUFFER_SIZE);
   4937     context.tempVertexBufferCounters1 = [];
   4938     context.tempVertexBufferCounters2 = [];
   4939     context.tempVertexBufferCounters1.length = context.tempVertexBufferCounters2.length = largestIndex + 1;
   4940     context.tempVertexBuffers1 = [];
   4941     context.tempVertexBuffers2 = [];
   4942     context.tempVertexBuffers1.length = context.tempVertexBuffers2.length = largestIndex + 1;
   4943     context.tempIndexBuffers = [];
   4944     context.tempIndexBuffers.length = largestIndex + 1;
   4945     for (var i = 0; i <= largestIndex; ++i) {
   4946       context.tempIndexBuffers[i] = null;
   4947       // Created on-demand
   4948       context.tempVertexBufferCounters1[i] = context.tempVertexBufferCounters2[i] = 0;
   4949       var ringbufferLength = GL.numTempVertexBuffersPerSize;
   4950       context.tempVertexBuffers1[i] = [];
   4951       context.tempVertexBuffers2[i] = [];
   4952       var ringbuffer1 = context.tempVertexBuffers1[i];
   4953       var ringbuffer2 = context.tempVertexBuffers2[i];
   4954       ringbuffer1.length = ringbuffer2.length = ringbufferLength;
   4955       for (var j = 0; j < ringbufferLength; ++j) {
   4956         ringbuffer1[j] = ringbuffer2[j] = null;
   4957       }
   4958     }
   4959     if (quads) {
   4960       // GL_QUAD indexes can be precalculated
   4961       context.tempQuadIndexBuffer = GLctx.createBuffer();
   4962       context.GLctx.bindBuffer(34963, context.tempQuadIndexBuffer);
   4963       var numIndexes = GL.MAX_TEMP_BUFFER_SIZE >> 1;
   4964       var quadIndexes = new Uint16Array(numIndexes);
   4965       var i = 0, v = 0;
   4966       while (1) {
   4967         quadIndexes[i++] = v;
   4968         if (i >= numIndexes) break;
   4969         quadIndexes[i++] = v + 1;
   4970         if (i >= numIndexes) break;
   4971         quadIndexes[i++] = v + 2;
   4972         if (i >= numIndexes) break;
   4973         quadIndexes[i++] = v;
   4974         if (i >= numIndexes) break;
   4975         quadIndexes[i++] = v + 2;
   4976         if (i >= numIndexes) break;
   4977         quadIndexes[i++] = v + 3;
   4978         if (i >= numIndexes) break;
   4979         v += 4;
   4980       }
   4981       context.GLctx.bufferData(34963, quadIndexes, 35044);
   4982       context.GLctx.bindBuffer(34963, null);
   4983     }
   4984   },
   4985   getTempVertexBuffer: sizeBytes => {
   4986     var idx = GL.log2ceilLookup(sizeBytes);
   4987     var ringbuffer = GL.currentContext.tempVertexBuffers1[idx];
   4988     var nextFreeBufferIndex = GL.currentContext.tempVertexBufferCounters1[idx];
   4989     GL.currentContext.tempVertexBufferCounters1[idx] = (GL.currentContext.tempVertexBufferCounters1[idx] + 1) & (GL.numTempVertexBuffersPerSize - 1);
   4990     var vbo = ringbuffer[nextFreeBufferIndex];
   4991     if (vbo) {
   4992       return vbo;
   4993     }
   4994     var prevVBO = GLctx.getParameter(34964);
   4995     ringbuffer[nextFreeBufferIndex] = GLctx.createBuffer();
   4996     GLctx.bindBuffer(34962, ringbuffer[nextFreeBufferIndex]);
   4997     GLctx.bufferData(34962, 1 << idx, 35048);
   4998     GLctx.bindBuffer(34962, prevVBO);
   4999     return ringbuffer[nextFreeBufferIndex];
   5000   },
   5001   getTempIndexBuffer: sizeBytes => {
   5002     var idx = GL.log2ceilLookup(sizeBytes);
   5003     var ibo = GL.currentContext.tempIndexBuffers[idx];
   5004     if (ibo) {
   5005       return ibo;
   5006     }
   5007     var prevIBO = GLctx.getParameter(34965);
   5008     GL.currentContext.tempIndexBuffers[idx] = GLctx.createBuffer();
   5009     GLctx.bindBuffer(34963, GL.currentContext.tempIndexBuffers[idx]);
   5010     GLctx.bufferData(34963, 1 << idx, 35048);
   5011     GLctx.bindBuffer(34963, prevIBO);
   5012     return GL.currentContext.tempIndexBuffers[idx];
   5013   },
   5014   newRenderingFrameStarted: () => {
   5015     if (!GL.currentContext) {
   5016       return;
   5017     }
   5018     var vb = GL.currentContext.tempVertexBuffers1;
   5019     GL.currentContext.tempVertexBuffers1 = GL.currentContext.tempVertexBuffers2;
   5020     GL.currentContext.tempVertexBuffers2 = vb;
   5021     vb = GL.currentContext.tempVertexBufferCounters1;
   5022     GL.currentContext.tempVertexBufferCounters1 = GL.currentContext.tempVertexBufferCounters2;
   5023     GL.currentContext.tempVertexBufferCounters2 = vb;
   5024     var largestIndex = GL.log2ceilLookup(GL.MAX_TEMP_BUFFER_SIZE);
   5025     for (var i = 0; i <= largestIndex; ++i) {
   5026       GL.currentContext.tempVertexBufferCounters1[i] = 0;
   5027     }
   5028   },
   5029   getSource: (shader, count, string, length) => {
   5030     var source = "";
   5031     for (var i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
   5032       var len = length ? HEAPU32[(((length) + (i * 4)) >> 2)] : undefined;
   5033       source += UTF8ToString(HEAPU32[(((string) + (i * 4)) >> 2)], len);
   5034     }
   5035     return source;
   5036   },
   5037   calcBufLength: (size, type, stride, count) => {
   5038     if (stride > 0) {
   5039       return count * stride;
   5040     }
   5041     var typeSize = GL.byteSizeByType[type - GL.byteSizeByTypeRoot];
   5042     return size * typeSize * count;
   5043   },
   5044   usedTempBuffers: [],
   5045   preDrawHandleClientVertexAttribBindings: count => {
   5046     GL.resetBufferBinding = false;
   5047     // TODO: initial pass to detect ranges we need to upload, might not need
   5048     // an upload per attrib
   5049     for (var i = 0; i < GL.currentContext.maxVertexAttribs; ++i) {
   5050       var cb = GL.currentContext.clientBuffers[i];
   5051       if (!cb.clientside || !cb.enabled) continue;
   5052       GL.resetBufferBinding = true;
   5053       var size = GL.calcBufLength(cb.size, cb.type, cb.stride, count);
   5054       var buf = GL.getTempVertexBuffer(size);
   5055       GLctx.bindBuffer(34962, buf);
   5056       GLctx.bufferSubData(34962, 0, HEAPU8.subarray(cb.ptr, cb.ptr + size));
   5057       cb.vertexAttribPointerAdaptor.call(GLctx, i, cb.size, cb.type, cb.normalized, cb.stride, 0);
   5058     }
   5059   },
   5060   postDrawHandleClientVertexAttribBindings: () => {
   5061     if (GL.resetBufferBinding) {
   5062       GLctx.bindBuffer(34962, GL.buffers[GLctx.currentArrayBufferBinding]);
   5063     }
   5064   },
   5065   createContext: (/** @type {HTMLCanvasElement} */ canvas, webGLContextAttributes) => {
   5066     // BUG: Workaround Safari WebGL issue: After successfully acquiring WebGL
   5067     // context on a canvas, calling .getContext() will always return that
   5068     // context independent of which 'webgl' or 'webgl2'
   5069     // context version was passed. See:
   5070     //   https://webkit.org/b/222758
   5071     // and:
   5072     //   https://github.com/emscripten-core/emscripten/issues/13295.
   5073     // TODO: Once the bug is fixed and shipped in Safari, adjust the Safari
   5074     // version field in above check.
   5075     if (!canvas.getContextSafariWebGL2Fixed) {
   5076       canvas.getContextSafariWebGL2Fixed = canvas.getContext;
   5077       /** @type {function(this:HTMLCanvasElement, string, (Object|null)=): (Object|null)} */ function fixedGetContext(ver, attrs) {
   5078         var gl = canvas.getContextSafariWebGL2Fixed(ver, attrs);
   5079         return ((ver == "webgl") == (gl instanceof WebGLRenderingContext)) ? gl : null;
   5080       }
   5081       canvas.getContext = fixedGetContext;
   5082     }
   5083     var ctx = (webGLContextAttributes.majorVersion > 1) ? canvas.getContext("webgl2", webGLContextAttributes) : canvas.getContext("webgl", webGLContextAttributes);
   5084     if (!ctx) return 0;
   5085     var handle = GL.registerContext(ctx, webGLContextAttributes);
   5086     return handle;
   5087   },
   5088   registerContext: (ctx, webGLContextAttributes) => {
   5089     // without pthreads a context is just an integer ID
   5090     var handle = GL.getNewId(GL.contexts);
   5091     var context = {
   5092       handle,
   5093       attributes: webGLContextAttributes,
   5094       version: webGLContextAttributes.majorVersion,
   5095       GLctx: ctx
   5096     };
   5097     // Store the created context object so that we can access the context
   5098     // given a canvas without having to pass the parameters again.
   5099     if (ctx.canvas) ctx.canvas.GLctxObject = context;
   5100     GL.contexts[handle] = context;
   5101     if (typeof webGLContextAttributes.enableExtensionsByDefault == "undefined" || webGLContextAttributes.enableExtensionsByDefault) {
   5102       GL.initExtensions(context);
   5103     }
   5104     context.maxVertexAttribs = context.GLctx.getParameter(34921);
   5105     context.clientBuffers = [];
   5106     for (var i = 0; i < context.maxVertexAttribs; i++) {
   5107       context.clientBuffers[i] = {
   5108         enabled: false,
   5109         clientside: false,
   5110         size: 0,
   5111         type: 0,
   5112         normalized: 0,
   5113         stride: 0,
   5114         ptr: 0,
   5115         vertexAttribPointerAdaptor: null
   5116       };
   5117     }
   5118     GL.generateTempBuffers(false, context);
   5119     return handle;
   5120   },
   5121   makeContextCurrent: contextHandle => {
   5122     // Active Emscripten GL layer context object.
   5123     GL.currentContext = GL.contexts[contextHandle];
   5124     // Active WebGL context object.
   5125     Module["ctx"] = GLctx = GL.currentContext?.GLctx;
   5126     return !(contextHandle && !GLctx);
   5127   },
   5128   getContext: contextHandle => GL.contexts[contextHandle],
   5129   deleteContext: contextHandle => {
   5130     if (GL.currentContext === GL.contexts[contextHandle]) {
   5131       GL.currentContext = null;
   5132     }
   5133     if (typeof JSEvents == "object") {
   5134       // Release all JS event handlers on the DOM element that the GL context is
   5135       // associated with since the context is now deleted.
   5136       JSEvents.removeAllHandlersOnTarget(GL.contexts[contextHandle].GLctx.canvas);
   5137     }
   5138     // Make sure the canvas object no longer refers to the context object so
   5139     // there are no GC surprises.
   5140     if (GL.contexts[contextHandle]?.GLctx.canvas) {
   5141       GL.contexts[contextHandle].GLctx.canvas.GLctxObject = undefined;
   5142     }
   5143     GL.contexts[contextHandle] = null;
   5144   },
   5145   initExtensions: context => {
   5146     // If this function is called without a specific context object, init the
   5147     // extensions of the currently active context.
   5148     context ||= GL.currentContext;
   5149     if (context.initExtensionsDone) return;
   5150     context.initExtensionsDone = true;
   5151     var GLctx = context.GLctx;
   5152     // Detect the presence of a few extensions manually, since the GL interop
   5153     // layer itself will need to know if they exist.
   5154     // Extensions that are available in both WebGL 1 and WebGL 2
   5155     webgl_enable_WEBGL_multi_draw(GLctx);
   5156     webgl_enable_EXT_polygon_offset_clamp(GLctx);
   5157     webgl_enable_EXT_clip_control(GLctx);
   5158     webgl_enable_WEBGL_polygon_mode(GLctx);
   5159     // Extensions that are only available in WebGL 1 (the calls will be no-ops
   5160     // if called on a WebGL 2 context active)
   5161     webgl_enable_ANGLE_instanced_arrays(GLctx);
   5162     webgl_enable_OES_vertex_array_object(GLctx);
   5163     webgl_enable_WEBGL_draw_buffers(GLctx);
   5164     // Extensions that are available from WebGL >= 2 (no-op if called on a WebGL 1 context active)
   5165     webgl_enable_WEBGL_draw_instanced_base_vertex_base_instance(GLctx);
   5166     webgl_enable_WEBGL_multi_draw_instanced_base_vertex_base_instance(GLctx);
   5167     // On WebGL 2, EXT_disjoint_timer_query is replaced with an alternative
   5168     // that's based on core APIs, and exposes only the queryCounterEXT()
   5169     // entrypoint.
   5170     if (context.version >= 2) {
   5171       GLctx.disjointTimerQueryExt = GLctx.getExtension("EXT_disjoint_timer_query_webgl2");
   5172     }
   5173     // However, Firefox exposes the WebGL 1 version on WebGL 2 as well and
   5174     // thus we look for the WebGL 1 version again if the WebGL 2 version
   5175     // isn't present. https://bugzil.la/1328882
   5176     if (context.version < 2 || !GLctx.disjointTimerQueryExt) {
   5177       GLctx.disjointTimerQueryExt = GLctx.getExtension("EXT_disjoint_timer_query");
   5178     }
   5179     for (var ext of getEmscriptenSupportedExtensions(GLctx)) {
   5180       // WEBGL_lose_context, WEBGL_debug_renderer_info and WEBGL_debug_shaders
   5181       // are not enabled by default.
   5182       if (!ext.includes("lose_context") && !ext.includes("debug")) {
   5183         // Call .getExtension() to enable that extension permanently.
   5184         GLctx.getExtension(ext);
   5185       }
   5186     }
   5187   }
   5188 };
   5189 
   5190 var webglPowerPreferences = [ "default", "low-power", "high-performance" ];
   5191 
   5192 /** @type {Object} */ var specialHTMLTargets = [ 0, globalThis.document ?? 0, globalThis.window ?? 0 ];
   5193 
   5194 var findEventTarget = target => {
   5195   // The sensible "default" target varies between events, but use window as the default
   5196   // since DOM events mostly can default to that. Specific callback registrations
   5197   // override their own defaults.
   5198   if (!target) return window;
   5199   if (typeof target == "number") target = specialHTMLTargets[target] || UTF8ToString(target);
   5200   if (target === "#window") return window; else if (target === "#document") return document; else if (target === "#screen") return screen; else if (target === "#canvas") return Module["canvas"]; else if (typeof target == "string") return globalThis.document?.getElementById(target);
   5201   return target;
   5202 };
   5203 
   5204 var findCanvasEventTarget = target => {
   5205   if (typeof target == "number") target = UTF8ToString(target);
   5206   if (!target || target === "#canvas") {
   5207     if (typeof GL != "undefined" && GL.offscreenCanvases["canvas"]) return GL.offscreenCanvases["canvas"];
   5208     // TODO: Remove this line, target '#canvas' should refer only to Module['canvas'], not to GL.offscreenCanvases['canvas'] - but need stricter tests to be able to remove this line.
   5209     return Module["canvas"];
   5210   }
   5211   if (typeof GL != "undefined" && GL.offscreenCanvases[target]) return GL.offscreenCanvases[target];
   5212   return findEventTarget(target);
   5213 };
   5214 
   5215 var _emscripten_webgl_do_create_context = (target, attributes) => {
   5216   var attr32 = ((attributes) >> 2);
   5217   var powerPreference = HEAP32[attr32 + (8 >> 2)];
   5218   var contextAttributes = {
   5219     "alpha": !!HEAP8[attributes + 0],
   5220     "depth": !!HEAP8[attributes + 1],
   5221     "stencil": !!HEAP8[attributes + 2],
   5222     "antialias": !!HEAP8[attributes + 3],
   5223     "premultipliedAlpha": !!HEAP8[attributes + 4],
   5224     "preserveDrawingBuffer": !!HEAP8[attributes + 5],
   5225     "powerPreference": webglPowerPreferences[powerPreference],
   5226     "failIfMajorPerformanceCaveat": !!HEAP8[attributes + 12],
   5227     // The following are not predefined WebGL context attributes in the WebGL specification, so the property names can be minified by Closure.
   5228     majorVersion: HEAP32[attr32 + (16 >> 2)],
   5229     minorVersion: HEAP32[attr32 + (20 >> 2)],
   5230     enableExtensionsByDefault: HEAP8[attributes + 24],
   5231     explicitSwapControl: HEAP8[attributes + 25],
   5232     proxyContextToMainThread: HEAP32[attr32 + (28 >> 2)],
   5233     renderViaOffscreenBackBuffer: HEAP8[attributes + 32]
   5234   };
   5235   var canvas = findCanvasEventTarget(target);
   5236   if (!canvas) {
   5237     return 0;
   5238   }
   5239   if (contextAttributes.explicitSwapControl) {
   5240     return 0;
   5241   }
   5242   var contextHandle = GL.createContext(canvas, contextAttributes);
   5243   return contextHandle;
   5244 };
   5245 
   5246 var _emscripten_webgl_create_context = _emscripten_webgl_do_create_context;
   5247 
   5248 var _emscripten_webgl_destroy_context = contextHandle => {
   5249   if (GL.currentContext == contextHandle) GL.currentContext = 0;
   5250   GL.deleteContext(contextHandle);
   5251 };
   5252 
   5253 var _emscripten_webgl_get_context_attributes = (c, a) => {
   5254   if (!a) return -5;
   5255   c = GL.contexts[c];
   5256   if (!c) return -3;
   5257   var t = c.GLctx?.getContextAttributes();
   5258   if (!t) return -3;
   5259   HEAP8[a] = t.alpha;
   5260   HEAP8[(a) + (1)] = t.depth;
   5261   HEAP8[(a) + (2)] = t.stencil;
   5262   HEAP8[(a) + (3)] = t.antialias;
   5263   HEAP8[(a) + (4)] = t.premultipliedAlpha;
   5264   HEAP8[(a) + (5)] = t.preserveDrawingBuffer;
   5265   var power = t["powerPreference"] && webglPowerPreferences.indexOf(t["powerPreference"]);
   5266   HEAP32[(((a) + (8)) >> 2)] = power;
   5267   HEAP8[(a) + (12)] = t.failIfMajorPerformanceCaveat;
   5268   HEAP32[(((a) + (16)) >> 2)] = c.version;
   5269   HEAP32[(((a) + (20)) >> 2)] = 0;
   5270   HEAP8[(a) + (24)] = c.attributes.enableExtensionsByDefault;
   5271   return 0;
   5272 };
   5273 
   5274 var _emscripten_webgl_do_get_current_context = () => GL.currentContext ? GL.currentContext.handle : 0;
   5275 
   5276 var _emscripten_webgl_get_current_context = _emscripten_webgl_do_get_current_context;
   5277 
   5278 var _emscripten_webgl_make_context_current = contextHandle => {
   5279   var success = GL.makeContextCurrent(contextHandle);
   5280   return success ? 0 : -5;
   5281 };

   5283 var stackAlloc = sz => __emscripten_stack_alloc(sz);
   5284 
   5285 var stringToUTF8OnStack = str => {
   5286   var size = lengthBytesUTF8(str) + 1;
   5287   var ret = stackAlloc(size);
   5288   stringToUTF8(str, ret, size);
   5289   return ret;
   5290 };

   5292 var writeI53ToI64 = (ptr, num) => {
   5293   HEAPU32[((ptr) >> 2)] = num;
   5294   var lower = HEAPU32[((ptr) >> 2)];
   5295   HEAPU32[(((ptr) + (4)) >> 2)] = (num - lower) / 4294967296;
   5296 };
   5297 
   5298 var readI53FromI64 = ptr => HEAPU32[((ptr) >> 2)] + HEAP32[(((ptr) + (4)) >> 2)] * 4294967296;

   5300 var wasmTableMirror = [];
   5301 
   5302 var getWasmTableEntry = funcPtr => {
   5303   var func = wasmTableMirror[funcPtr];
   5304   if (!func) {
   5305     /** @suppress {checkTypes} */ wasmTableMirror[funcPtr] = func = wasmTable.get(funcPtr);
   5306   }
   5307   return func;
   5308 };

   5440   errorCallback: (callback, type, message, userdata) => {
   5441     var sp = stackSave();
   5442     var messagePtr = stringToUTF8OnStack(message);
   5443     getWasmTableEntry(callback)(type, messagePtr, userdata);
   5444     stackRestore(sp);
   5445   },
   5446   iterateExtensions: (root, handlers) => {
   5447     for (var ptr = HEAPU32[((root) >> 2)]; ptr; ptr = HEAPU32[((ptr) >> 2)]) {
   5448       var sType = HEAP32[(((ptr) + (4)) >> 2)];
   5449       // This will crash if there's no handler indicating either a bogus
   5450       // sType, or one we haven't implemented yet.
   5451       var handler = handlers[sType](ptr);
   5452     }
   5453   },
   5454   setStringView: (ptr, data, length) => {
   5455     HEAPU32[((ptr) >> 2)] = data;
   5456     HEAPU32[(((ptr) + (4)) >> 2)] = length;
   5457   },
   5458   makeStringFromStringView: stringViewPtr => {
   5459     var ptr = HEAPU32[((stringViewPtr) >> 2)];
   5460     var length = HEAPU32[(((stringViewPtr) + (4)) >> 2)];
   5461     // UTF8ToString stops at the first null terminator character in the
   5462     // string regardless of the length.
   5463     return UTF8ToString(ptr, length);
   5464   },
   5465   makeStringFromOptionalStringView: stringViewPtr => {
   5466     var ptr = HEAPU32[((stringViewPtr) >> 2)];
   5467     var length = HEAPU32[(((stringViewPtr) + (4)) >> 2)];
   5468     // If we don't have a valid string pointer, just return undefined when
   5469     // optional.
   5470     if (!ptr) {
   5471       if (length === 0) {
   5472         return "";
   5473       }
   5474       return undefined;
   5475     }
   5476     // UTF8ToString stops at the first null terminator character in the
   5477     // string regardless of the length.
   5478     return UTF8ToString(ptr, length);
   5479   },
   5480   makeColor: ptr => ({
   5481     "r": HEAPF64[((ptr) >> 3)],

   5492     "x": HEAPU32[((ptr) >> 2)],
   5493     "y": HEAPU32[(((ptr) + (4)) >> 2)],
   5494     "z": HEAPU32[(((ptr) + (8)) >> 2)]
   5495   }),
   5496   makeTexelCopyTextureInfo: ptr => ({
   5497     "texture": WebGPU.getJsObject(HEAPU32[((ptr) >> 2)]),
   5498     "mipLevel": HEAPU32[(((ptr) + (4)) >> 2)],
   5499     "origin": WebGPU.makeOrigin3D(ptr + 8),
   5500     "aspect": WebGPU.TextureAspect[HEAP32[(((ptr) + (20)) >> 2)]]
   5501   }),
   5502   makeTexelCopyBufferLayout: ptr => {
   5503     var bytesPerRow = HEAPU32[(((ptr) + (8)) >> 2)];
   5504     var rowsPerImage = HEAPU32[(((ptr) + (12)) >> 2)];
   5505     return {
   5506       "offset": readI53FromI64(ptr),
   5507       "bytesPerRow": bytesPerRow === 4294967295 ? undefined : bytesPerRow,
   5508       "rowsPerImage": rowsPerImage === 4294967295 ? undefined : rowsPerImage
   5509     };
   5510   },
   5511   makeTexelCopyBufferInfo: ptr => {
   5512     var layoutPtr = ptr + 0;
   5513     var bufferCopyView = WebGPU.makeTexelCopyBufferLayout(layoutPtr);
   5514     bufferCopyView["buffer"] = WebGPU.getJsObject(HEAPU32[(((ptr) + (16)) >> 2)]);
   5515     return bufferCopyView;
   5516   },
   5517   makePassTimestampWrites: ptr => {
   5518     if (ptr === 0) return undefined;
   5519     return {
   5520       "querySet": WebGPU.getJsObject(HEAPU32[(((ptr) + (4)) >> 2)]),
   5521       "beginningOfPassWriteIndex": HEAPU32[(((ptr) + (8)) >> 2)],
   5522       "endOfPassWriteIndex": HEAPU32[(((ptr) + (12)) >> 2)]
   5523     };
   5524   },
   5525   makePipelineConstants: (constantCount, constantsPtr) => {
   5526     if (!constantCount) return;
   5527     var constants = {};
   5528     for (var i = 0; i < constantCount; ++i) {
   5529       var entryPtr = constantsPtr + 24 * i;
   5530       var key = WebGPU.makeStringFromStringView(entryPtr + 4);
   5531       constants[key] = HEAPF64[(((entryPtr) + (16)) >> 3)];
   5532     }
   5533     return constants;
   5534   },
   5535   makePipelineLayout: layoutPtr => {
   5536     if (!layoutPtr) return "auto";
   5537     return WebGPU.getJsObject(layoutPtr);
   5538   },
   5539   makeComputeState: ptr => {
   5540     if (!ptr) return undefined;
   5541     var desc = {
   5542       "module": WebGPU.getJsObject(HEAPU32[(((ptr) + (4)) >> 2)]),
   5543       "constants": WebGPU.makePipelineConstants(HEAPU32[(((ptr) + (16)) >> 2)], HEAPU32[(((ptr) + (20)) >> 2)]),
   5544       "entryPoint": WebGPU.makeStringFromOptionalStringView(ptr + 8)
   5545     };
   5546     return desc;
   5547   },
   5548   makeComputePipelineDesc: descriptor => {
   5549     var desc = {
   5550       "label": WebGPU.makeStringFromOptionalStringView(descriptor + 4),
   5551       "layout": WebGPU.makePipelineLayout(HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (12)) >> 2)]),
   5552       "compute": WebGPU.makeComputeState(descriptor + 16)
   5553     };
   5554     return desc;
   5555   },
   5556   makeRenderPipelineDesc: descriptor => {
   5557     function makePrimitiveState(psPtr) {
   5558       if (!psPtr) return undefined;
   5559       return {
   5560         "topology": WebGPU.PrimitiveTopology[HEAP32[(((psPtr) + (4)) >> 2)]],
   5561         "stripIndexFormat": WebGPU.IndexFormat[HEAP32[(((psPtr) + (8)) >> 2)]],
   5562         "frontFace": WebGPU.FrontFace[HEAP32[(((psPtr) + (12)) >> 2)]],
   5563         "cullMode": WebGPU.CullMode[HEAP32[(((psPtr) + (16)) >> 2)]],
   5564         "unclippedDepth": !!(HEAPU32[(((psPtr) + (20)) >> 2)])
   5565       };
   5566     }
   5567     function makeBlendComponent(bdPtr) {
   5568       if (!bdPtr) return undefined;
   5569       return {
   5570         "operation": WebGPU.BlendOperation[HEAP32[((bdPtr) >> 2)]],
   5571         "srcFactor": WebGPU.BlendFactor[HEAP32[(((bdPtr) + (4)) >> 2)]],
   5572         "dstFactor": WebGPU.BlendFactor[HEAP32[(((bdPtr) + (8)) >> 2)]]
   5573       };
   5574     }
   5575     function makeBlendState(bsPtr) {
   5576       if (!bsPtr) return undefined;
   5577       return {
   5578         "alpha": makeBlendComponent(bsPtr + 12),
   5579         "color": makeBlendComponent(bsPtr + 0)
   5580       };
   5581     }
   5582     function makeColorState(csPtr) {
   5583       var format = WebGPU.TextureFormat[HEAP32[(((csPtr) + (4)) >> 2)]];
   5584       return format ? {
   5585         "format": format,
   5586         "blend": makeBlendState(HEAPU32[(((csPtr) + (8)) >> 2)]),
   5587         "writeMask": HEAPU32[(((csPtr) + (16)) >> 2)]
   5588       } : undefined;
   5589     }
   5590     function makeColorStates(count, csArrayPtr) {
   5591       var states = [];
   5592       for (var i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
   5593         states.push(makeColorState(csArrayPtr + 24 * i));
   5594       }
   5595       return states;
   5596     }
   5597     function makeStencilStateFace(ssfPtr) {
   5598       return {
   5599         "compare": WebGPU.CompareFunction[HEAP32[((ssfPtr) >> 2)]],
   5600         "failOp": WebGPU.StencilOperation[HEAP32[(((ssfPtr) + (4)) >> 2)]],
   5601         "depthFailOp": WebGPU.StencilOperation[HEAP32[(((ssfPtr) + (8)) >> 2)]],
   5602         "passOp": WebGPU.StencilOperation[HEAP32[(((ssfPtr) + (12)) >> 2)]]
   5603       };
   5604     }
   5605     function makeDepthStencilState(dssPtr) {
   5606       if (!dssPtr) return undefined;
   5607       return {
   5608         "format": WebGPU.TextureFormat[HEAP32[(((dssPtr) + (4)) >> 2)]],
   5609         "depthWriteEnabled": !!(HEAPU32[(((dssPtr) + (8)) >> 2)]),
   5610         "depthCompare": WebGPU.CompareFunction[HEAP32[(((dssPtr) + (12)) >> 2)]],
   5611         "stencilFront": makeStencilStateFace(dssPtr + 16),
   5612         "stencilBack": makeStencilStateFace(dssPtr + 32),
   5613         "stencilReadMask": HEAPU32[(((dssPtr) + (48)) >> 2)],
   5614         "stencilWriteMask": HEAPU32[(((dssPtr) + (52)) >> 2)],
   5615         "depthBias": HEAP32[(((dssPtr) + (56)) >> 2)],
   5616         "depthBiasSlopeScale": HEAPF32[(((dssPtr) + (60)) >> 2)],
   5617         "depthBiasClamp": HEAPF32[(((dssPtr) + (64)) >> 2)]
   5618       };
   5619     }
   5620     function makeVertexAttribute(vaPtr) {
   5621       return {
   5622         "format": WebGPU.VertexFormat[HEAP32[(((vaPtr) + (4)) >> 2)]],
   5623         "offset": readI53FromI64((vaPtr) + (8)),
   5624         "shaderLocation": HEAPU32[(((vaPtr) + (16)) >> 2)]
   5625       };
   5626     }
   5627     function makeVertexAttributes(count, vaArrayPtr) {
   5628       var vas = [];
   5629       for (var i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
   5630         vas.push(makeVertexAttribute(vaArrayPtr + i * 24));
   5631       }
   5632       return vas;
   5633     }
   5634     function makeVertexBuffer(vbPtr) {
   5635       if (!vbPtr) return undefined;
   5636       var stepMode = WebGPU.VertexStepMode[HEAP32[(((vbPtr) + (4)) >> 2)]];
   5637       var attributeCount = HEAPU32[(((vbPtr) + (16)) >> 2)];
   5638       if (!stepMode && !attributeCount) {
   5639         return null;
   5640       }
   5641       return {
   5642         "arrayStride": readI53FromI64((vbPtr) + (8)),
   5643         "stepMode": stepMode,
   5644         "attributes": makeVertexAttributes(attributeCount, HEAPU32[(((vbPtr) + (20)) >> 2)])
   5645       };
   5646     }
   5647     function makeVertexBuffers(count, vbArrayPtr) {
   5648       if (!count) return undefined;
   5649       var vbs = [];
   5650       for (var i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
   5651         vbs.push(makeVertexBuffer(vbArrayPtr + i * 24));
   5652       }
   5653       return vbs;
   5654     }
   5655     function makeVertexState(viPtr) {
   5656       if (!viPtr) return undefined;
   5657       var desc = {
   5658         "module": WebGPU.getJsObject(HEAPU32[(((viPtr) + (4)) >> 2)]),
   5659         "constants": WebGPU.makePipelineConstants(HEAPU32[(((viPtr) + (16)) >> 2)], HEAPU32[(((viPtr) + (20)) >> 2)]),
   5660         "buffers": makeVertexBuffers(HEAPU32[(((viPtr) + (24)) >> 2)], HEAPU32[(((viPtr) + (28)) >> 2)]),
   5661         "entryPoint": WebGPU.makeStringFromOptionalStringView(viPtr + 8)
   5662       };
   5663       return desc;
   5664     }
   5665     function makeMultisampleState(msPtr) {
   5666       if (!msPtr) return undefined;
   5667       return {
   5668         "count": HEAPU32[(((msPtr) + (4)) >> 2)],
   5669         "mask": HEAPU32[(((msPtr) + (8)) >> 2)],
   5670         "alphaToCoverageEnabled": !!(HEAPU32[(((msPtr) + (12)) >> 2)])
   5671       };
   5672     }
   5673     function makeFragmentState(fsPtr) {
   5674       if (!fsPtr) return undefined;
   5675       var desc = {
   5676         "module": WebGPU.getJsObject(HEAPU32[(((fsPtr) + (4)) >> 2)]),
   5677         "constants": WebGPU.makePipelineConstants(HEAPU32[(((fsPtr) + (16)) >> 2)], HEAPU32[(((fsPtr) + (20)) >> 2)]),
   5678         "targets": makeColorStates(HEAPU32[(((fsPtr) + (24)) >> 2)], HEAPU32[(((fsPtr) + (28)) >> 2)]),
   5679         "entryPoint": WebGPU.makeStringFromOptionalStringView(fsPtr + 8)
   5680       };
   5681       return desc;
   5682     }
   5683     var desc = {
   5684       "label": WebGPU.makeStringFromOptionalStringView(descriptor + 4),
   5685       "layout": WebGPU.makePipelineLayout(HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (12)) >> 2)]),
   5686       "vertex": makeVertexState(descriptor + 16),
   5687       "primitive": makePrimitiveState(descriptor + 48),
   5688       "depthStencil": makeDepthStencilState(HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (72)) >> 2)]),
   5689       "multisample": makeMultisampleState(descriptor + 76),
   5690       "fragment": makeFragmentState(HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (92)) >> 2)])
   5691     };
   5692     return desc;
   5693   },
   5694   fillLimitStruct: (limits, limitsOutPtr) => {
   5695     var nextInChainPtr = HEAPU32[((limitsOutPtr) >> 2)];
   5696     function setLimitValueU32(name, basePtr, limitOffset, fallbackValue = 0) {
   5697       var limitValue = limits[name] ?? fallbackValue;
   5698       HEAPU32[(((basePtr) + (limitOffset)) >> 2)] = limitValue;
   5699     }
   5700     function setLimitValueU64(name, basePtr, limitOffset, fallbackValue = 0) {
   5701       var limitValue = limits[name] ?? fallbackValue;
   5702       // Limits are integer-valued JS `Number`s, so they fit in 'i53'.
   5703       writeI53ToI64((basePtr) + (limitOffset), limitValue);
   5704     }
   5705     setLimitValueU32("maxTextureDimension1D", limitsOutPtr, 4);
   5706     setLimitValueU32("maxTextureDimension2D", limitsOutPtr, 8);
   5707     setLimitValueU32("maxTextureDimension3D", limitsOutPtr, 12);
   5708     setLimitValueU32("maxTextureArrayLayers", limitsOutPtr, 16);
   5709     setLimitValueU32("maxBindGroups", limitsOutPtr, 20);
   5710     setLimitValueU32("maxBindGroupsPlusVertexBuffers", limitsOutPtr, 24);
   5711     setLimitValueU32("maxBindingsPerBindGroup", limitsOutPtr, 28);
   5712     setLimitValueU32("maxDynamicUniformBuffersPerPipelineLayout", limitsOutPtr, 32);
   5713     setLimitValueU32("maxDynamicStorageBuffersPerPipelineLayout", limitsOutPtr, 36);
   5714     setLimitValueU32("maxSampledTexturesPerShaderStage", limitsOutPtr, 40);
   5715     setLimitValueU32("maxSamplersPerShaderStage", limitsOutPtr, 44);
   5716     setLimitValueU32("maxStorageBuffersPerShaderStage", limitsOutPtr, 48);
   5717     setLimitValueU32("maxStorageTexturesPerShaderStage", limitsOutPtr, 52);
   5718     setLimitValueU32("maxUniformBuffersPerShaderStage", limitsOutPtr, 56);
   5719     setLimitValueU32("minUniformBufferOffsetAlignment", limitsOutPtr, 80);
   5720     setLimitValueU32("minStorageBufferOffsetAlignment", limitsOutPtr, 84);
   5721     setLimitValueU64("maxUniformBufferBindingSize", limitsOutPtr, 64);
   5722     setLimitValueU64("maxStorageBufferBindingSize", limitsOutPtr, 72);
   5723     setLimitValueU32("maxVertexBuffers", limitsOutPtr, 88);
   5724     setLimitValueU64("maxBufferSize", limitsOutPtr, 96);
   5725     setLimitValueU32("maxVertexAttributes", limitsOutPtr, 104);
   5726     setLimitValueU32("maxVertexBufferArrayStride", limitsOutPtr, 108);
   5727     setLimitValueU32("maxInterStageShaderVariables", limitsOutPtr, 112);
   5728     setLimitValueU32("maxColorAttachments", limitsOutPtr, 116);
   5729     setLimitValueU32("maxColorAttachmentBytesPerSample", limitsOutPtr, 120);
   5730     setLimitValueU32("maxComputeWorkgroupStorageSize", limitsOutPtr, 124);
   5731     setLimitValueU32("maxComputeInvocationsPerWorkgroup", limitsOutPtr, 128);
   5732     setLimitValueU32("maxComputeWorkgroupSizeX", limitsOutPtr, 132);
   5733     setLimitValueU32("maxComputeWorkgroupSizeY", limitsOutPtr, 136);
   5734     setLimitValueU32("maxComputeWorkgroupSizeZ", limitsOutPtr, 140);
   5735     setLimitValueU32("maxComputeWorkgroupsPerDimension", limitsOutPtr, 144);
   5736     // Note this limit is new and won't be present in all browsers for a while. Fall back to 0.
   5737     setLimitValueU32("maxImmediateSize", limitsOutPtr, 148);
   5738     if (nextInChainPtr !== 0) {
   5739       var sType = HEAP32[(((nextInChainPtr) + (4)) >> 2)];
   5740       var compatibilityModeLimitsPtr = nextInChainPtr;
   5741       // Note these limits are new and won't be present in all browsers for a while. Fall back to exposing the PerShaderStage limit.
   5742       setLimitValueU32("maxStorageBuffersInVertexStage", compatibilityModeLimitsPtr, 8, limits.maxStorageBuffersPerShaderStage);
   5743       setLimitValueU32("maxStorageBuffersInFragmentStage", compatibilityModeLimitsPtr, 16, limits.maxStorageBuffersPerShaderStage);
   5744       setLimitValueU32("maxStorageTexturesInVertexStage", compatibilityModeLimitsPtr, 12, limits.maxStorageTexturesPerShaderStage);
   5745       setLimitValueU32("maxStorageTexturesInFragmentStage", compatibilityModeLimitsPtr, 20, limits.maxStorageTexturesPerShaderStage);
   5746     }
   5747   },
   5748   fillAdapterInfoStruct: (info, infoStruct) => {
   5749     // Populate subgroup limits.
   5750     HEAPU32[(((infoStruct) + (52)) >> 2)] = info.subgroupMinSize;
   5751     HEAPU32[(((infoStruct) + (56)) >> 2)] = info.subgroupMaxSize;
   5752     // Append all the strings together to condense into a single malloc.
   5753     var strs = info.vendor + info.architecture + info.device + info.description;
   5754     var strPtr = stringToNewUTF8(strs);
   5755     var vendorLen = lengthBytesUTF8(info.vendor);
   5756     WebGPU.setStringView(infoStruct + 4, strPtr, vendorLen);
   5757     strPtr += vendorLen;
   5758     var architectureLen = lengthBytesUTF8(info.architecture);
   5759     WebGPU.setStringView(infoStruct + 12, strPtr, architectureLen);
   5760     strPtr += architectureLen;
   5761     var deviceLen = lengthBytesUTF8(info.device);
   5762     WebGPU.setStringView(infoStruct + 20, strPtr, deviceLen);
   5763     strPtr += deviceLen;
   5764     var descriptionLen = lengthBytesUTF8(info.description);
   5765     WebGPU.setStringView(infoStruct + 28, strPtr, descriptionLen);
   5766     strPtr += descriptionLen;
   5767     HEAP32[(((infoStruct) + (36)) >> 2)] = 2;
   5768     var adapterType = info.isFallbackAdapter ? 3 : 4;
   5769     HEAP32[(((infoStruct) + (40)) >> 2)] = adapterType;
   5770     HEAPU32[(((infoStruct) + (44)) >> 2)] = 0;
   5771     HEAPU32[(((infoStruct) + (48)) >> 2)] = 0;
   5772   },

   5837 };

   5839 var _emscripten_webgpu_get_device = () => {
   5840   if (WebGPU.preinitializedDeviceId === undefined) {
   5841     WebGPU.preinitializedDeviceId = WebGPU.importJsDevice(Module["preinitializedWebGPUDevice"]);
   5842     // Some users depend on this keeping the device alive, so we add an
   5843     // additional reference when we first initialize it.
   5844     _wgpuDeviceAddRef(WebGPU.preinitializedDeviceId);
   5845   }
   5846   _wgpuDeviceAddRef(WebGPU.preinitializedDeviceId);
   5847   return WebGPU.preinitializedDeviceId;
   5848 };

   5850 var _emwgpuBufferDestroy = bufferPtr => {
   5851   var buffer = WebGPU.getJsObject(bufferPtr);
   5852   var onUnmap = WebGPU.Internals.bufferOnUnmaps[bufferPtr];
   5853   if (onUnmap) {
   5854     for (var i = 0; i < onUnmap.length; ++i) {
   5855       onUnmap[i]();
   5856     }
   5857     delete WebGPU.Internals.bufferOnUnmaps[bufferPtr];
   5858   }
   5859   buffer.destroy();
   5860 };
   5861 
   5862 var _emwgpuBufferGetMappedRange = (bufferPtr, offset, size) => {
   5863   var buffer = WebGPU.getJsObject(bufferPtr);
   5864   if (size == -1) size = undefined;
   5865   var mapped;
   5866   try {
   5867     mapped = buffer.getMappedRange(offset, size);
   5868   } catch (ex) {
   5869     return 0;
   5870   }
   5871   var data = _memalign(16, mapped.byteLength);
   5872   HEAPU8.fill(0, data, mapped.byteLength);
   5873   WebGPU.Internals.bufferOnUnmaps[bufferPtr].push(() => {
   5874     new Uint8Array(mapped).set(HEAPU8.subarray(data, data + mapped.byteLength));
   5875     _free(data);
   5876   });
   5877   return data;
   5878 };
   5879 
   5880 var _emwgpuBufferUnmap = bufferPtr => {
   5881   var buffer = WebGPU.getJsObject(bufferPtr);
   5882   var onUnmap = WebGPU.Internals.bufferOnUnmaps[bufferPtr];
   5883   if (!onUnmap) {
   5884     // Already unmapped
   5885     return;
   5886   }
   5887   for (var i = 0; i < onUnmap.length; ++i) {
   5888     onUnmap[i]();
   5889   }
   5890   delete WebGPU.Internals.bufferOnUnmaps[bufferPtr];
   5891   buffer.unmap();
   5892 };

   5894 var _emwgpuDelete = ptr => {
   5895   delete WebGPU.Internals.jsObjects[ptr];
   5896 };

   5898 var _emwgpuDeviceCreateBuffer = (devicePtr, descriptor, bufferPtr) => {
   5899   var mappedAtCreation = !!(HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (32)) >> 2)]);
   5900   var desc = {
   5901     "label": WebGPU.makeStringFromOptionalStringView(descriptor + 4),
   5902     "usage": HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (16)) >> 2)],
   5903     "size": readI53FromI64((descriptor) + (24)),
   5904     "mappedAtCreation": mappedAtCreation
   5905   };
   5906   var device = WebGPU.getJsObject(devicePtr);
   5907   var buffer;
   5908   try {
   5909     buffer = device.createBuffer(desc);
   5910   } catch (ex) {
   5911     // The only exception should be RangeError if mapping at creation ran out of memory.
   5912     return false;
   5913   }
   5914   WebGPU.Internals.jsObjectInsert(bufferPtr, buffer);
   5915   if (mappedAtCreation) {
   5916     WebGPU.Internals.bufferOnUnmaps[bufferPtr] = [];
   5917   }
   5918   return true;
   5919 };

   5921 var _emwgpuDeviceCreateComputePipelineAsync = function(devicePtr, futureId_low, futureId_high, descriptor, pipelinePtr) {
   5922   var futureId = convertI32PairToI53Checked(futureId_low, futureId_high);
   5923   var desc = WebGPU.makeComputePipelineDesc(descriptor);
   5924   var device = WebGPU.getJsObject(devicePtr);
   5925   // createComputePipelineAsync
   5926   WebGPU.Internals.futureInsert(futureId, device.createComputePipelineAsync(desc).then(pipeline => {
   5927     // createComputePipelineAsync fulfilled
   5928     callUserCallback(() => {
   5929       WebGPU.Internals.jsObjectInsert(pipelinePtr, pipeline);
   5930       _emwgpuOnCreateComputePipelineCompleted(futureId, 1, pipelinePtr, 0);
   5931     });
   5932   }, pipelineError => {
   5933     // createComputePipelineAsync rejected
   5934     callUserCallback(() => {
   5935       var sp = stackSave();
   5936       var messagePtr = stringToUTF8OnStack(pipelineError.message);
   5937       var status = pipelineError.reason === "validation" ? 3 : pipelineError.reason === "internal" ? 4 : 0;
   5938       _emwgpuOnCreateComputePipelineCompleted(futureId, status, pipelinePtr, messagePtr);
   5939       stackRestore(sp);
   5940     });
   5941   }));
   5942 };

   5996 var ENV = {};

   5998 var getExecutableName = () => thisProgram || "./this.program";
   5999 
   6000 var getEnvStrings = () => {
   6001   if (!getEnvStrings.strings) {
   6002     // Default values.
   6003     // Browser language detection #8751
   6004     var lang = (globalThis.navigator?.language ?? "C").replace("-", "_") + ".UTF-8";
   6005     var env = {
   6006       "USER": "web_user",
   6007       "LOGNAME": "web_user",
   6008       "PATH": "/",
   6009       "PWD": "/",
   6010       "HOME": "/home/web_user",
   6011       "LANG": lang,
   6012       "_": getExecutableName()
   6013     };
   6014     // Apply the user-provided values, if any.
   6015     for (var x in ENV) {
   6016       // x is a key in ENV; if ENV[x] is undefined, that means it was
   6017       // explicitly set to be so. We allow user code to do that to
   6018       // force variables with default values to remain unset.
   6019       if (ENV[x] === undefined) delete env[x]; else env[x] = ENV[x];
   6020     }
   6021     var strings = [];
   6022     for (var x in env) {
   6023       strings.push(`${x}=${env[x]}`);
   6024     }
   6025     getEnvStrings.strings = strings;
   6026   }
   6027   return getEnvStrings.strings;
   6028 };
   6029 
   6030 var _environ_get = (__environ, environ_buf) => {
   6031   var bufSize = 0;
   6032   var envp = 0;
   6033   for (var string of getEnvStrings()) {
   6034     var ptr = environ_buf + bufSize;
   6035     HEAPU32[(((__environ) + (envp)) >> 2)] = ptr;
   6036     bufSize += stringToUTF8(string, ptr, Infinity) + 1;
   6037     envp += 4;
   6038   }
   6039   return 0;
   6040 };
   6041 
   6042 var _environ_sizes_get = (penviron_count, penviron_buf_size) => {
   6043   var strings = getEnvStrings();
   6044   HEAPU32[((penviron_count) >> 2)] = strings.length;
   6045   var bufSize = 0;
   6046   for (var string of strings) {
   6047     bufSize += lengthBytesUTF8(string) + 1;
   6048   }
   6049   HEAPU32[((penviron_buf_size) >> 2)] = bufSize;
   6050   return 0;
   6051 };

   6053 function _fd_close(fd) {
   6054   try {
   6055     var stream = SYSCALLS.getStreamFromFD(fd);
   6056     FS.close(stream);
   6057     return 0;
   6058   } catch (e) {
   6059     if (typeof FS == "undefined" || !(e.name === "ErrnoError")) throw e;
   6060     return e.errno;
   6061   }
   6062 }
   6063 
   6064 /** @param {number=} offset */ var doReadv = (stream, iov, iovcnt, offset) => {
   6065   var ret = 0;
   6066   for (var i = 0; i < iovcnt; i++) {
   6067     var ptr = HEAPU32[((iov) >> 2)];
   6068     var len = HEAPU32[(((iov) + (4)) >> 2)];
   6069     iov += 8;
   6070     var curr = FS.read(stream, HEAP8, ptr, len, offset);
   6071     if (curr < 0) return -1;
   6072     ret += curr;
   6073     if (curr < len) break;
   6074     // nothing more to read
   6075     if (typeof offset != "undefined") {
   6076       offset += curr;
   6077     }
   6078   }
   6079   return ret;
   6080 };
   6081 
   6082 function _fd_read(fd, iov, iovcnt, pnum) {
   6083   try {
   6084     var stream = SYSCALLS.getStreamFromFD(fd);
   6085     var num = doReadv(stream, iov, iovcnt);
   6086     HEAPU32[((pnum) >> 2)] = num;
   6087     return 0;
   6088   } catch (e) {
   6089     if (typeof FS == "undefined" || !(e.name === "ErrnoError")) throw e;
   6090     return e.errno;
   6091   }
   6092 }
   6093 
   6094 function _fd_seek(fd, offset_low, offset_high, whence, newOffset) {
   6095   var offset = convertI32PairToI53Checked(offset_low, offset_high);
   6096   try {
   6097     if (isNaN(offset)) return 61;
   6098     var stream = SYSCALLS.getStreamFromFD(fd);
   6099     FS.llseek(stream, offset, whence);
   6100     (tempI64 = [ stream.position >>> 0, (tempDouble = stream.position, (+(Math.abs(tempDouble))) >= 1 ? (tempDouble > 0 ? (+(Math.floor((tempDouble) / 4294967296))) >>> 0 : (~~((+(Math.ceil((tempDouble - +(((~~(tempDouble))) >>> 0)) / 4294967296))))) >>> 0) : 0) ], 
   6101     HEAP32[((newOffset) >> 2)] = tempI64[0], HEAP32[(((newOffset) + (4)) >> 2)] = tempI64[1]);
   6102     if (stream.getdents && offset === 0 && whence === 0) stream.getdents = null;
   6103     // reset readdir state
   6104     return 0;
   6105   } catch (e) {
   6106     if (typeof FS == "undefined" || !(e.name === "ErrnoError")) throw e;
   6107     return e.errno;
   6108   }
   6109 }
   6110 
   6111 /** @param {number=} offset */ var doWritev = (stream, iov, iovcnt, offset) => {
   6112   var ret = 0;
   6113   for (var i = 0; i < iovcnt; i++) {
   6114     var ptr = HEAPU32[((iov) >> 2)];
   6115     var len = HEAPU32[(((iov) + (4)) >> 2)];
   6116     iov += 8;
   6117     var curr = FS.write(stream, HEAP8, ptr, len, offset);
   6118     if (curr < 0) return -1;
   6119     ret += curr;
   6120     if (curr < len) {
   6121       // No more space to write.
   6122       break;
   6123     }
   6124     if (typeof offset != "undefined") {
   6125       offset += curr;
   6126     }
   6127   }
   6128   return ret;
   6129 };
   6130 
   6131 function _fd_write(fd, iov, iovcnt, pnum) {
   6132   try {
   6133     var stream = SYSCALLS.getStreamFromFD(fd);
   6134     var num = doWritev(stream, iov, iovcnt);
   6135     HEAPU32[((pnum) >> 2)] = num;
   6136     return 0;
   6137   } catch (e) {
   6138     if (typeof FS == "undefined" || !(e.name === "ErrnoError")) throw e;
   6139     return e.errno;
   6140   }
   6141 }

   6159 var _emscripten_glBindBuffer = (target, buffer) => {
   6160   // Calling glBindBuffer with an unknown buffer will implicitly create a
   6161   // new one.  Here we bypass `GL.counter` and directly using the ID passed
   6162   // in.
   6163   if (buffer && !GL.buffers[buffer]) {
   6164     var b = GLctx.createBuffer();
   6165     b.name = buffer;
   6166     GL.buffers[buffer] = b;
   6167   }
   6168   if (target == 34962) {
   6169     GLctx.currentArrayBufferBinding = buffer;
   6170   } else if (target == 34963) {
   6171     GLctx.currentElementArrayBufferBinding = buffer;
   6172   }
   6173   if (target == 35051) {
   6174     // In WebGL 2 glReadPixels entry point, we need to use a different WebGL 2
   6175     // API function call when a buffer is bound to
   6176     // GL_PIXEL_PACK_BUFFER_BINDING point, so must keep track whether that
   6177     // binding point is non-null to know what is the proper API function to
   6178     // call.
   6179     GLctx.currentPixelPackBufferBinding = buffer;
   6180   } else if (target == 35052) {
   6181     // In WebGL 2 gl(Compressed)Tex(Sub)Image[23]D entry points, we need to
   6182     // use a different WebGL 2 API function call when a buffer is bound to
   6183     // GL_PIXEL_UNPACK_BUFFER_BINDING point, so must keep track whether that
   6184     // binding point is non-null to know what is the proper API function to
   6185     // call.
   6186     GLctx.currentPixelUnpackBufferBinding = buffer;
   6187   }
   6188   GLctx.bindBuffer(target, GL.buffers[buffer]);
   6189 };
   6190 
   6191 var _glBindBuffer = _emscripten_glBindBuffer;
   6192
