   6227 var _emscripten_glBufferData = (target, size, data, usage) => {
   6228   if (GL.currentContext.version >= 2) {
   6229     // If size is zero, WebGL would interpret uploading the whole input
   6230     // arraybuffer (starting from given offset), which would not make sense in
   6231     // WebAssembly, so avoid uploading if size is zero. However we must still
   6232     // call bufferData to establish a backing storage of zero bytes.
   6233     if (data && size) {
   6234       GLctx.bufferData(target, HEAPU8, usage, data, size);
   6235     } else {
   6236       GLctx.bufferData(target, size, usage);
   6237     }
   6238     return;
   6239   }
   6240   // N.b. here first form specifies a heap subarray, second form an integer
   6241   // size, so the ?: code here is polymorphic. It is advised to avoid
   6242   // randomly mixing both uses in calling code, to avoid any potential JS
   6243   // engine JIT issues.
   6244   GLctx.bufferData(target, data ? HEAPU8.subarray(data, data + size) : size, usage);
   6245 };

   6257 var convertI32PairToI53 = (lo, hi) => (lo >>> 0) + hi * 4294967296;

   6259 var _emscripten_glClientWaitSync = (sync, flags, timeout_low, timeout_high) => {
   6260   // WebGL2 vs GLES3 differences: in GLES3, the timeout parameter is a uint64, where 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFULL means GL_TIMEOUT_IGNORED.
   6261   // In JS, there's no 64-bit value types, so instead timeout is taken to be signed, and GL_TIMEOUT_IGNORED is given value -1.
   6262   // Inherently the value accepted in the timeout is lossy, and can't take in arbitrary u64 bit pattern (but most likely doesn't matter)
   6263   // See https://www.khronos.org/registry/webgl/specs/latest/2.0/#5.15
   6264   var timeout = convertI32PairToI53(timeout_low, timeout_high);
   6265   return GLctx.clientWaitSync(GL.syncs[sync], flags, timeout);
   6266 };

   6282 var _emscripten_glCreateProgram = () => {
   6283   var id = GL.getNewId(GL.programs);
   6284   var program = GLctx.createProgram();
   6285   // Store additional information needed for each shader program:
   6286   program.name = id;
   6287   // Lazy cache results of
   6288   // glGetProgramiv(GL_ACTIVE_UNIFORM_MAX_LENGTH/GL_ACTIVE_ATTRIBUTE_MAX_LENGTH/GL_ACTIVE_UNIFORM_BLOCK_MAX_NAME_LENGTH)
   6289   program.maxUniformLength = program.maxAttributeLength = program.maxUniformBlockNameLength = 0;
   6290   program.uniformIdCounter = 1;
   6291   GL.programs[id] = program;
   6292   return id;
   6293 };

   6305 var _emscripten_glDeleteBuffers = (n, buffers) => {
   6306   for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   6307     var id = HEAP32[(((buffers) + (i * 4)) >> 2)];
   6308     var buffer = GL.buffers[id];
   6309     // From spec: "glDeleteBuffers silently ignores 0's and names that do not
   6310     // correspond to existing buffer objects."
   6311     if (!buffer) continue;
   6312     GLctx.deleteBuffer(buffer);
   6313     buffer.name = 0;
   6314     GL.buffers[id] = null;
   6315     if (id == GLctx.currentArrayBufferBinding) GLctx.currentArrayBufferBinding = 0;
   6316     if (id == GLctx.currentElementArrayBufferBinding) GLctx.currentElementArrayBufferBinding = 0;
   6317     if (id == GLctx.currentPixelPackBufferBinding) GLctx.currentPixelPackBufferBinding = 0;
   6318     if (id == GLctx.currentPixelUnpackBufferBinding) GLctx.currentPixelUnpackBufferBinding = 0;
   6319   }
   6320 };
   6321 
   6322 var _glDeleteBuffers = _emscripten_glDeleteBuffers;
   6323 
   6324 var _emscripten_glDeleteFramebuffers = (n, framebuffers) => {
   6325   for (var i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   6326     var id = HEAP32[(((framebuffers) + (i * 4)) >> 2)];
   6327     var framebuffer = GL.framebuffers[id];
   6328     if (!framebuffer) continue;
   6329     // GL spec: "glDeleteFramebuffers silently ignores 0s and names that do not correspond to existing framebuffer objects".
   6330     GLctx.deleteFramebuffer(framebuffer);
   6331     framebuffer.name = 0;
   6332     GL.framebuffers[id] = null;
   6333   }
   6334 };

   6338 var _emscripten_glDeleteProgram = id => {

   6343     // object, unlike some other GL delete functions.

   6345     return;
   6346   }

   6348   program.name = 0;
   6349   GL.programs[id] = null;
   6350 };

   6534 
   6535 var emscriptenWebGLGet = (name_, p, type) => {
   6536   // Guard against user passing a null pointer.
   6537   // Note that GLES2 spec does not say anything about how passing a null
   6538   // pointer should be treated.  Testing on desktop core GL 3, the application
   6539   // crashes on glGetIntegerv to a null pointer, but better to report an error
   6540   // instead of doing anything random.
   6541   if (!p) {
   6542     GL.recordError(1281);
   6543     return;
   6544   }
   6545   var ret = undefined;
   6546   switch (name_) {
   6547    // Handle a few trivial GLES values
   6548     case 36346:
   6549     // GL_SHADER_COMPILER
   6550     ret = 1;
   6551     break;
   6552 
   6553    case 36344:
   6554     // GL_SHADER_BINARY_FORMATS
   6555     if (type != 0 && type != 1) {
   6556       GL.recordError(1280);
   6557     }
   6558     // Do not write anything to the out pointer, since no binary formats are
   6559     // supported.
   6560     return;
   6561 
   6562    case 34814:
   6563    // GL_NUM_PROGRAM_BINARY_FORMATS
   6564     case 36345:
   6565     // GL_NUM_SHADER_BINARY_FORMATS
   6566     ret = 0;
   6567     break;
   6568 
   6569    case 34466:
   6570     // GL_NUM_COMPRESSED_TEXTURE_FORMATS
   6571     // WebGL doesn't have GL_NUM_COMPRESSED_TEXTURE_FORMATS (it's obsolete
   6572     // since GL_COMPRESSED_TEXTURE_FORMATS returns a JS array that can be
   6573     // queried for length), so implement it ourselves to allow C++ GLES2
   6574     // code to get the length.
   6575     var formats = GLctx.getParameter(34467);
   6576     ret = formats ? formats.length : 0;
   6577     break;
   6578 
   6579    case 33309:
   6580     // GL_NUM_EXTENSIONS
   6581     if (GL.currentContext.version < 2) {
   6582       // Calling GLES3/WebGL2 function with a GLES2/WebGL1 context
   6583       GL.recordError(1282);
   6584       return;
   6585     }
   6586     ret = webglGetExtensions().length;
   6587     break;
   6588 
   6589    case 33307:
   6590    // GL_MAJOR_VERSION
   6591     case 33308:
   6592     // GL_MINOR_VERSION
   6593     if (GL.currentContext.version < 2) {
   6594       GL.recordError(1280);
   6595       // GL_INVALID_ENUM
   6596       return;
   6597     }
   6598     ret = name_ == 33307 ? 3 : 0;
   6599     // return version 3.0
   6600     break;
   6601   }
   6602   if (ret === undefined) {
   6603     var result = GLctx.getParameter(name_);
   6604     switch (typeof result) {
   6605      case "number":
   6606       ret = result;
   6607       break;
   6608 
   6609      case "boolean":
   6610       ret = result ? 1 : 0;
   6611       break;
   6612 
   6613      case "string":
   6614       GL.recordError(1280);
   6615       // GL_INVALID_ENUM
   6616       return;
   6617 
   6618      case "object":
   6619       if (result === null) {
   6620         // null is a valid result for some (e.g., which buffer is bound -
   6621         // perhaps nothing is bound), but otherwise can mean an invalid
   6622         // name_, which we need to report as an error
   6623         switch (name_) {
   6624          case 34964:
   6625          // ARRAY_BUFFER_BINDING
   6626           case 35725:
   6627          // CURRENT_PROGRAM
   6628           case 34965:
   6629          // ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER_BINDING
   6630           case 36006:
   6631          // FRAMEBUFFER_BINDING or DRAW_FRAMEBUFFER_BINDING
   6632           case 36007:
   6633          // RENDERBUFFER_BINDING
   6634           case 32873:
   6635          // TEXTURE_BINDING_2D
   6636           case 34229:
   6637          // WebGL 2 GL_VERTEX_ARRAY_BINDING, or WebGL 1 extension OES_vertex_array_object GL_VERTEX_ARRAY_BINDING_OES
   6638           case 36662:
   6639          // COPY_READ_BUFFER_BINDING or COPY_READ_BUFFER
   6640           case 36663:
   6641          // COPY_WRITE_BUFFER_BINDING or COPY_WRITE_BUFFER
   6642           case 35053:
   6643          // PIXEL_PACK_BUFFER_BINDING
   6644           case 35055:
   6645          // PIXEL_UNPACK_BUFFER_BINDING
   6646           case 36010:
   6647          // READ_FRAMEBUFFER_BINDING
   6648           case 35097:
   6649          // SAMPLER_BINDING
   6650           case 35869:
   6651          // TEXTURE_BINDING_2D_ARRAY
   6652           case 32874:
   6653          // TEXTURE_BINDING_3D
   6654           case 36389:
   6655          // TRANSFORM_FEEDBACK_BINDING
   6656           case 35983:
   6657          // TRANSFORM_FEEDBACK_BUFFER_BINDING
   6658           case 35368:
   6659          // UNIFORM_BUFFER_BINDING
   6660           case 34068:
   6661           {
   6662             // TEXTURE_BINDING_CUBE_MAP
   6663             ret = 0;
   6664             break;
   6665           }
   6666 
   6667          default:
   6668           {
   6669             GL.recordError(1280);
   6670             // GL_INVALID_ENUM
   6671             return;
   6672           }
   6673         }
   6674       } else if (result instanceof Float32Array || result instanceof Uint32Array || result instanceof Int32Array || result instanceof Array) {
   6675         for (var i = 0; i < result.length; ++i) {
   6676           switch (type) {
   6677            case 0:
   6678             HEAP32[(((p) + (i * 4)) >> 2)] = result[i];
   6679             break;
   6680 
   6681            case 2:
   6682             HEAPF32[(((p) + (i * 4)) >> 2)] = result[i];
   6683             break;
   6684 
   6685            case 4:
   6686             HEAP8[(p) + (i)] = result[i] ? 1 : 0;
   6687             break;
   6688           }
   6689         }
   6690         return;
   6691       } else {
   6692         try {
   6693           ret = result.name | 0;
   6694         } catch (e) {
   6695           GL.recordError(1280);
   6696           // GL_INVALID_ENUM
   6697           err(`GL_INVALID_ENUM in glGet${type}v: Unknown object returned from WebGL getParameter(${name_})! (error: ${e})`);
   6698           return;
   6699         }
   6700       }
   6701       break;
   6702 
   6703      default:
   6704       GL.recordError(1280);
   6705       // GL_INVALID_ENUM
   6706       err(`GL_INVALID_ENUM in glGet${type}v: Native code calling glGet${type}v(${name_}) and it returns ${result} of type ${typeof (result)}!`);
   6707       return;
   6708     }
   6709   }
   6710   switch (type) {
   6711    case 1:
   6712     writeI53ToI64(p, ret);
   6713     break;
   6714 
   6715    case 0:
   6716     HEAP32[((p) >> 2)] = ret;
   6717     break;
   6718 
   6719    case 2:
   6720     HEAPF32[((p) >> 2)] = ret;
   6721     break;
   6722 
   6723    case 4:
   6724     HEAP8[p] = ret ? 1 : 0;
   6725     break;
   6726   }
   6727 };
   6728 
   6729 var _emscripten_glGetFloatv = (name_, p) => emscriptenWebGLGet(name_, p, 2);
   6730 
   6731 var _glGetFloatv = _emscripten_glGetFloatv;
   6732 
   6733 var _emscripten_glGetIntegerv = (name_, p) => emscriptenWebGLGet(name_, p, 0);
   6734 
   6735 var _glGetIntegerv = _emscripten_glGetIntegerv;
   6736 
   6737 var _emscripten_glGetProgramiv = (program, pname, p) => {
   6738   if (!p) {
   6739     // GLES2 specification does not specify how to behave if p is a null
   6740     // pointer. Since calling this function does not make sense if p == null,
   6741     // issue a GL error to notify user about it.
   6742     GL.recordError(1281);
   6743     return;
   6744   }
   6745   if (program >= GL.counter) {
   6746     GL.recordError(1281);
   6747     return;
   6748   }
   6749   program = GL.programs[program];
   6750   if (pname == 35716) {
   6751     // GL_INFO_LOG_LENGTH
   6752     var log = GLctx.getProgramInfoLog(program);
   6753     if (log === null) log = "(unknown error)";
   6754     HEAP32[((p) >> 2)] = log.length + 1;
   6755   } else if (pname == 35719) {
   6756     if (!program.maxUniformLength) {
   6757       var numActiveUniforms = GLctx.getProgramParameter(program, 35718);
   6758       for (var i = 0; i < numActiveUniforms; ++i) {
   6759         program.maxUniformLength = Math.max(program.maxUniformLength, GLctx.getActiveUniform(program, i).name.length + 1);
   6760       }
   6761     }
   6762     HEAP32[((p) >> 2)] = program.maxUniformLength;
   6763   } else if (pname == 35722) {
   6764     if (!program.maxAttributeLength) {
   6765       var numActiveAttributes = GLctx.getProgramParameter(program, 35721);
   6766       for (var i = 0; i < numActiveAttributes; ++i) {
   6767         program.maxAttributeLength = Math.max(program.maxAttributeLength, GLctx.getActiveAttrib(program, i).name.length + 1);
   6768       }
   6769     }
   6770     HEAP32[((p) >> 2)] = program.maxAttributeLength;
   6771   } else if (pname == 35381) {
   6772     if (!program.maxUniformBlockNameLength) {
   6773       var numActiveUniformBlocks = GLctx.getProgramParameter(program, 35382);
   6774       for (var i = 0; i < numActiveUniformBlocks; ++i) {
   6775         program.maxUniformBlockNameLength = Math.max(program.maxUniformBlockNameLength, GLctx.getActiveUniformBlockName(program, i).length + 1);
   6776       }
   6777     }
   6778     HEAP32[((p) >> 2)] = program.maxUniformBlockNameLength;
   6779   } else {
   6780     HEAP32[((p) >> 2)] = GLctx.getProgramParameter(program, pname);
   6781   }
   6782 };
   6783 
   6784 var _glGetProgramiv = _emscripten_glGetProgramiv;
   6785 
   6786 var _emscripten_glGetShaderInfoLog = (shader, maxLength, length, infoLog) => {
   6787   var log = GLctx.getShaderInfoLog(GL.shaders[shader]);
   6788   if (log === null) log = "(unknown error)";
   6789   var numBytesWrittenExclNull = (maxLength > 0 && infoLog) ? stringToUTF8(log, infoLog, maxLength) : 0;
   6790   if (length) HEAP32[((length) >> 2)] = numBytesWrittenExclNull;
   6791 };
   6792 
   6793 var _glGetShaderInfoLog = _emscripten_glGetShaderInfoLog;
   6794 
   6795 var _emscripten_glGetShaderiv = (shader, pname, p) => {
   6796   if (!p) {
   6797     // GLES2 specification does not specify how to behave if p is a null
   6798     // pointer. Since calling this function does not make sense if p == null,
   6799     // issue a GL error to notify user about it.
   6800     GL.recordError(1281);
   6801     return;
   6802   }
   6803   if (pname == 35716) {
   6804     // GL_INFO_LOG_LENGTH
   6805     var log = GLctx.getShaderInfoLog(GL.shaders[shader]);
   6806     if (log === null) log = "(unknown error)";
   6807     // The GLES2 specification says that if the shader has an empty info log,
   6808     // a value of 0 is returned. Otherwise the log has a null char appended.
   6809     // (An empty string is falsey, so we can just check that instead of
   6810     // looking at log.length.)
   6811     var logLength = log ? log.length + 1 : 0;
   6812     HEAP32[((p) >> 2)] = logLength;
   6813   } else if (pname == 35720) {
   6814     // GL_SHADER_SOURCE_LENGTH
   6815     var source = GLctx.getShaderSource(GL.shaders[shader]);
   6816     // source may be a null, or the empty string, both of which are falsey
   6817     // values that we report a 0 length for.
   6818     var sourceLength = source ? source.length + 1 : 0;
   6819     HEAP32[((p) >> 2)] = sourceLength;
   6820   } else {
   6821     HEAP32[((p) >> 2)] = GLctx.getShaderParameter(GL.shaders[shader], pname);
   6822   }
   6823 };
   6824 
   6825 var _glGetShaderiv = _emscripten_glGetShaderiv;
   6826 
   6827 var _emscripten_glGetString = name_ => {
   6828   var ret = GL.stringCache[name_];
   6829   if (!ret) {
   6830     switch (name_) {
   6831      case 7939:
   6832       ret = stringToNewUTF8(webglGetExtensions().join(" "));
   6833       break;
   6834 
   6835      case 7936:
   6836      case 7937:
   6837      case 37445:
   6838      case 37446:
   6839       var s = GLctx.getParameter(name_);
   6840       if (!s) {
   6841         GL.recordError(1280);
   6842       }
   6843       ret = s ? stringToNewUTF8(s) : 0;
   6844       break;
   6845 
   6846      case 7938:
   6847       var webGLVersion = GLctx.getParameter(7938);
   6848       // return GLES version string corresponding to the version of the WebGL context
   6849       var glVersion = `OpenGL ES 2.0 (${webGLVersion})`;
   6850       if (GL.currentContext.version >= 2) glVersion = `OpenGL ES 3.0 (${webGLVersion})`;
   6851       ret = stringToNewUTF8(glVersion);
   6852       break;
   6853 
   6854      case 35724:
   6855       var glslVersion = GLctx.getParameter(35724);
   6856       // extract the version number 'N.M' from the string 'WebGL GLSL ES N.M ...'
   6857       var ver_re = /^WebGL GLSL ES ([0-9]\.[0-9][0-9]?)(?:$| .*)/;
   6858       var ver_num = glslVersion.match(ver_re);
   6859       if (ver_num !== null) {
   6860         if (ver_num[1].length == 3) ver_num[1] = ver_num[1] + "0";
   6861         // ensure minor version has 2 digits
   6862         glslVersion = `OpenGL ES GLSL ES ${ver_num[1]} (${glslVersion})`;
   6863       }
   6864       ret = stringToNewUTF8(glslVersion);
   6865       break;
   6866 
   6867      default:
   6868       GL.recordError(1280);
   6869     }
   6870     GL.stringCache[name_] = ret;
   6871   }
   6872   return ret;
   6873 };
   6874 
   6875 var _glGetString = _emscripten_glGetString;
   6876 
   6877 var _emscripten_glGetUniformBlockIndex = (program, uniformBlockName) => GLctx.getUniformBlockIndex(GL.programs[program], UTF8ToString(uniformBlockName));
   6878 
   6879 var _glGetUniformBlockIndex = _emscripten_glGetUniformBlockIndex;
   6880 
   6881 /** @suppress {checkTypes} */ var jstoi_q = str => parseInt(str);
   6882 
   6883 /** @noinline */ var webglGetLeftBracePos = name => name.slice(-1) == "]" && name.lastIndexOf("[");
   6884 
   6885 var webglPrepareUniformLocationsBeforeFirstUse = program => {
   6886   var uniformLocsById = program.uniformLocsById, // Maps GLuint -> WebGLUniformLocation
   6887   uniformSizeAndIdsByName = program.uniformSizeAndIdsByName, // Maps name -> [uniform array length, GLuint]
   6888   i, j;
   6889   // On the first time invocation of glGetUniformLocation on this shader program:
   6890   // initialize cache data structures and discover which uniforms are arrays.
   6891   if (!uniformLocsById) {
   6892     // maps GLint integer locations to WebGLUniformLocations
   6893     program.uniformLocsById = uniformLocsById = {};
   6894     // maps integer locations back to uniform name strings, so that we can lazily fetch uniform array locations
   6895     program.uniformArrayNamesById = {};
   6896     var numActiveUniforms = GLctx.getProgramParameter(program, 35718);
   6897     for (i = 0; i < numActiveUniforms; ++i) {
   6898       var u = GLctx.getActiveUniform(program, i);
   6899       var nm = u.name;
   6900       var sz = u.size;
   6901       var lb = webglGetLeftBracePos(nm);
   6902       var arrayName = lb > 0 ? nm.slice(0, lb) : nm;
   6903       // Assign a new location.
   6904       var id = program.uniformIdCounter;
   6905       program.uniformIdCounter += sz;
   6906       // Eagerly get the location of the uniformArray[0] base element.
   6907       // The remaining indices >0 will be left for lazy evaluation to
   6908       // improve performance. Those may never be needed to fetch, if the
   6909       // application fills arrays always in full starting from the first
   6910       // element of the array.
   6911       uniformSizeAndIdsByName[arrayName] = [ sz, id ];
   6912       // Store placeholder integers in place that highlight that these
   6913       // >0 index locations are array indices pending population.
   6914       for (j = 0; j < sz; ++j) {
   6915         uniformLocsById[id] = j;
   6916         program.uniformArrayNamesById[id++] = arrayName;
   6917       }
   6918     }
   6919   }
   6920 };
   6921 
   6922 var _emscripten_glGetUniformLocation = (program, name) => {
   6923   name = UTF8ToString(name);
   6924   if (program = GL.programs[program]) {
   6925     webglPrepareUniformLocationsBeforeFirstUse(program);
   6926     var uniformLocsById = program.uniformLocsById;
   6927     // Maps GLuint -> WebGLUniformLocation
   6928     var arrayIndex = 0;
   6929     var uniformBaseName = name;
   6930     // Invariant: when populating integer IDs for uniform locations, we must
   6931     // maintain the precondition that arrays reside in contiguous addresses,
   6932     // i.e. for a 'vec4 colors[10];', colors[4] must be at location
   6933     // colors[0]+4.  However, user might call glGetUniformLocation(program,
   6934     // "colors") for an array, so we cannot discover based on the user input
   6935     // arguments whether the uniform we are dealing with is an array. The only
   6936     // way to discover which uniforms are arrays is to enumerate over all the
   6937     // active uniforms in the program.
   6938     var leftBrace = webglGetLeftBracePos(name);
   6939     // If user passed an array accessor "[index]", parse the array index off the accessor.
   6940     if (leftBrace > 0) {
   6941       arrayIndex = jstoi_q(name.slice(leftBrace + 1)) >>> 0;
   6942       // "index]", coerce parseInt(']') with >>>0 to treat "foo[]" as "foo[0]" and foo[-1] as unsigned out-of-bounds.
   6943       uniformBaseName = name.slice(0, leftBrace);
   6944     }
   6945     // Have we cached the location of this uniform before?
   6946     // A pair [array length, GLint of the uniform location]
   6947     var sizeAndId = program.uniformSizeAndIdsByName[uniformBaseName];
   6948     // If a uniform with this name exists, and if its index is within the
   6949     // array limits (if it's even an array), query the WebGLlocation, or
   6950     // return an existing cached location.
   6951     if (sizeAndId && arrayIndex < sizeAndId[0]) {
   6952       arrayIndex += sizeAndId[1];
   6953       // Add the base location of the uniform to the array index offset.
   6954       if ((uniformLocsById[arrayIndex] = uniformLocsById[arrayIndex] || GLctx.getUniformLocation(program, name))) {
   6955         return arrayIndex;
   6956       }
   6957     }
   6958   } else {
   6959     // N.b. we are currently unable to distinguish between GL program IDs that
   6960     // never existed vs GL program IDs that have been deleted, so report
   6961     // GL_INVALID_VALUE in both cases.
   6962     GL.recordError(1281);
   6963   }
   6964   return -1;
   6965 };
   6966 
   6967 var _glGetUniformLocation = _emscripten_glGetUniformLocation;
   6968 
   6969 var _emscripten_glLineWidth = x0 => GLctx.lineWidth(x0);
   6970 
   6971 var _glLineWidth = _emscripten_glLineWidth;

   6973 var _emscripten_glLinkProgram = program => {
   6974   program = GL.programs[program];
   6975   GLctx.linkProgram(program);
   6976   // Invalidate earlier computed uniform->ID mappings, those have now become stale
   6977   program.uniformLocsById = 0;
   6978   // Mark as null-like so that glGetUniformLocation() knows to populate this again.
   6979   program.uniformSizeAndIdsByName = {};
   6980 };
   6981 
   6982 var _glLinkProgram = _emscripten_glLinkProgram;
   6983 
   6984 var _emscripten_glPixelStorei = (pname, param) => {
   6985   if (pname == 3317) {
   6986     GL.unpackAlignment = param;
   6987   } else if (pname == 3314) {
   6988     GL.unpackRowLength = param;
   6989   }
   6990   GLctx.pixelStorei(pname, param);
   6991 };
   6992 
   6993 var _glPixelStorei = _emscripten_glPixelStorei;
   6994 
   6995 var computeUnpackAlignedImageSize = (width, height, sizePerPixel) => {
   6996   function roundedToNextMultipleOf(x, y) {
   6997     return (x + y - 1) & -y;
   6998   }
   6999   var plainRowSize = (GL.unpackRowLength || width) * sizePerPixel;
   7000   var alignedRowSize = roundedToNextMultipleOf(plainRowSize, GL.unpackAlignment);
   7001   return height * alignedRowSize;
   7002 };
   7003 
   7004 var colorChannelsInGlTextureFormat = format => {
   7005   // Micro-optimizations for size: map format to size by subtracting smallest
   7006   // enum value (0x1902) from all values first.  Also omit the most common
   7007   // size value (1) from the list, which is assumed by formats not on the
   7008   // list.
   7009   var colorChannels = {
   7010     // 0x1902 /* GL_DEPTH_COMPONENT */ - 0x1902: 1,
   7011     // 0x1906 /* GL_ALPHA */ - 0x1902: 1,
   7012     5: 3,
   7013     6: 4,
   7014     // 0x1909 /* GL_LUMINANCE */ - 0x1902: 1,
   7015     8: 2,
   7016     29502: 3,
   7017     29504: 4,
   7018     // 0x1903 /* GL_RED */ - 0x1902: 1,
   7019     26917: 2,
   7020     26918: 2,
   7021     // 0x8D94 /* GL_RED_INTEGER */ - 0x1902: 1,
   7022     29846: 3,
   7023     29847: 4
   7024   };
   7025   return colorChannels[format - 6402] || 1;
   7026 };
   7027 
   7028 var heapObjectForWebGLType = type => {
   7029   // Micro-optimization for size: Subtract lowest GL enum number (0x1400/* GL_BYTE */) from type to compare
   7030   // smaller values for the heap, for shorter generated code size.
   7031   // Also the type HEAPU16 is not tested for explicitly, but any unrecognized type will return out HEAPU16.
   7032   // (since most types are HEAPU16)
   7033   type -= 5120;
   7034   if (type == 0) return HEAP8;
   7035   if (type == 1) return HEAPU8;
   7036   if (type == 2) return HEAP16;
   7037   if (type == 4) return HEAP32;
   7038   if (type == 6) return HEAPF32;
   7039   if (type == 5 || type == 28922 || type == 28520 || type == 30779 || type == 30782) return HEAPU32;
   7040   return HEAPU16;
   7041 };
   7042 
   7043 var toTypedArrayIndex = (pointer, heap) => pointer >>> (31 - Math.clz32(heap.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT));
   7044 
   7045 var emscriptenWebGLGetTexPixelData = (type, format, width, height, pixels, internalFormat) => {
   7046   var heap = heapObjectForWebGLType(type);
   7047   var sizePerPixel = colorChannelsInGlTextureFormat(format) * heap.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT;
   7048   var bytes = computeUnpackAlignedImageSize(width, height, sizePerPixel);
   7049   return heap.subarray(toTypedArrayIndex(pixels, heap), toTypedArrayIndex(pixels + bytes, heap));
   7050 };
   7051 
   7052 var _emscripten_glReadPixels = (x, y, width, height, format, type, pixels) => {
   7053   if (GL.currentContext.version >= 2) {
   7054     if (GLctx.currentPixelPackBufferBinding) {
   7055       GLctx.readPixels(x, y, width, height, format, type, pixels);
   7056       return;
   7057     }
   7058     var heap = heapObjectForWebGLType(type);
   7059     var target = toTypedArrayIndex(pixels, heap);
   7060     GLctx.readPixels(x, y, width, height, format, type, heap, target);
   7061     return;
   7062   }
   7063   var pixelData = emscriptenWebGLGetTexPixelData(type, format, width, height, pixels, format);
   7064   if (!pixelData) {
   7065     GL.recordError(1280);
   7066     return;
   7067   }
   7068   GLctx.readPixels(x, y, width, height, format, type, pixelData);
   7069 };
   7070 
   7071 var _glReadPixels = _emscripten_glReadPixels;
   7072

   7080 var _emscripten_glTexImage2D = (target, level, internalFormat, width, height, border, format, type, pixels) => {
   7081   if (GL.currentContext.version >= 2) {
   7082     if (GLctx.currentPixelUnpackBufferBinding) {
   7083       GLctx.texImage2D(target, level, internalFormat, width, height, border, format, type, pixels);
   7084       return;
   7085     }
   7086     if (pixels) {
   7087       var heap = heapObjectForWebGLType(type);
   7088       var index = toTypedArrayIndex(pixels, heap);
   7089       GLctx.texImage2D(target, level, internalFormat, width, height, border, format, type, heap, index);
   7090       return;
   7091     }
   7092   }
   7093   var pixelData = pixels ? emscriptenWebGLGetTexPixelData(type, format, width, height, pixels, internalFormat) : null;
   7094   GLctx.texImage2D(target, level, internalFormat, width, height, border, format, type, pixelData);
   7095 };

   7153 var webglGetUniformLocation = location => {
   7154   var p = GLctx.currentProgram;
   7155   if (p) {
   7156     var webglLoc = p.uniformLocsById[location];
   7157     // p.uniformLocsById[location] stores either an integer, or a
   7158     // WebGLUniformLocation.
   7159     // If an integer, we have not yet bound the location, so do it now. The
   7160     // integer value specifies the array index we should bind to.
   7161     if (typeof webglLoc == "number") {
   7162       p.uniformLocsById[location] = webglLoc = GLctx.getUniformLocation(p, p.uniformArrayNamesById[location] + (webglLoc > 0 ? `[${webglLoc}]` : ""));
   7163     }
   7164     // Else an already cached WebGLUniformLocation, return it.
   7165     return webglLoc;
   7166   } else {
   7167     GL.recordError(1282);
   7168   }
   7169 };
   7170 
   7171 var _emscripten_glUniform1f = (location, v0) => {
   7172   GLctx.uniform1f(webglGetUniformLocation(location), v0);
   7173 };

   7175 var _glUniform1f = _emscripten_glUniform1f;
   7176 
   7177 var miniTempWebGLFloatBuffers = [];
   7178 
   7179 var _emscripten_glUniform1fv = (location, count, value) => {
   7180   if (GL.currentContext.version >= 2) {
   7181     count && GLctx.uniform1fv(webglGetUniformLocation(location), HEAPF32, ((value) >> 2), count);
   7182     return;
   7183   }
   7184   if (count <= 288) {
   7185     // avoid allocation when uploading few enough uniforms
   7186     var view = miniTempWebGLFloatBuffers[count];
   7187     for (var i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
   7188       view[i] = HEAPF32[(((value) + (4 * i)) >> 2)];
   7189     }
   7190   } else {
   7191     var view = HEAPF32.subarray((((value) >> 2)), ((value + count * 4) >> 2));
   7192   }
   7193   GLctx.uniform1fv(webglGetUniformLocation(location), view);
   7194 };
   7195 
   7196 var _glUniform1fv = _emscripten_glUniform1fv;

   7210 var _emscripten_glUniform2fv = (location, count, value) => {
   7211   if (GL.currentContext.version >= 2) {
   7212     count && GLctx.uniform2fv(webglGetUniformLocation(location), HEAPF32, ((value) >> 2), count * 2);
   7213     return;
   7214   }
   7215   if (count <= 144) {
   7216     // avoid allocation when uploading few enough uniforms
   7217     count *= 2;
   7218     var view = miniTempWebGLFloatBuffers[count];
   7219     for (var i = 0; i < count; i += 2) {
   7220       view[i] = HEAPF32[(((value) + (4 * i)) >> 2)];
   7221       view[i + 1] = HEAPF32[(((value) + (4 * i + 4)) >> 2)];
   7222     }
   7223   } else {
   7224     var view = HEAPF32.subarray((((value) >> 2)), ((value + count * 8) >> 2));
   7225   }
   7226   GLctx.uniform2fv(webglGetUniformLocation(location), view);
   7227 };

   7243 var _emscripten_glUniform4fv = (location, count, value) => {
   7244   if (GL.currentContext.version >= 2) {
   7245     count && GLctx.uniform4fv(webglGetUniformLocation(location), HEAPF32, ((value) >> 2), count * 4);
   7246     return;
   7247   }
   7248   if (count <= 72) {
   7249     // avoid allocation when uploading few enough uniforms
   7250     var view = miniTempWebGLFloatBuffers[4 * count];
   7251     // hoist the heap out of the loop for size and for pthreads+growth.
   7252     var heap = HEAPF32;
   7253     value = ((value) >> 2);
   7254     count *= 4;
   7255     for (var i = 0; i < count; i += 4) {
   7256       var dst = value + i;
   7257       view[i] = heap[dst];
   7258       view[i + 1] = heap[dst + 1];
   7259       view[i + 2] = heap[dst + 2];
   7260       view[i + 3] = heap[dst + 3];
   7261     }
   7262   } else {
   7263     var view = HEAPF32.subarray((((value) >> 2)), ((value + count * 16) >> 2));
   7264   }
   7265   GLctx.uniform4fv(webglGetUniformLocation(location), view);
   7266 };
   7267 
   7268 var _glUniform4fv = _emscripten_glUniform4fv;
   7269 
   7270 var miniTempWebGLIntBuffers = [];
   7271 
   7272 var _emscripten_glUniform4iv = (location, count, value) => {

   7275     return;

   7280     var view = miniTempWebGLIntBuffers[count];
   7281     for (var i = 0; i < count; i += 4) {
   7282       view[i] = HEAP32[(((value) + (4 * i)) >> 2)];
   7283       view[i + 1] = HEAP32[(((value) + (4 * i + 4)) >> 2)];
   7284       view[i + 2] = HEAP32[(((value) + (4 * i + 8)) >> 2)];
   7285       view[i + 3] = HEAP32[(((value) + (4 * i + 12)) >> 2)];

   7288     var view = HEAP32.subarray((((value) >> 2)), ((value + count * 16) >> 2));

   7337       view[i + 2] = HEAPF32[(((value) + (4 * i + 8)) >> 2)];
   7338       view[i + 3] = HEAPF32[(((value) + (4 * i + 12)) >> 2)];
   7339       view[i + 4] = HEAPF32[(((value) + (4 * i + 16)) >> 2)];

   7350 
   7351 var _glUniformMatrix3fv = _emscripten_glUniformMatrix3fv;

   7353 var _emscripten_glUniformMatrix4fv = (location, count, transpose, value) => {
   7354   if (GL.currentContext.version >= 2) {
   7355     count && GLctx.uniformMatrix4fv(webglGetUniformLocation(location), !!transpose, HEAPF32, ((value) >> 2), count * 16);
   7356     return;
   7357   }
   7358   if (count <= 18) {
   7359     // avoid allocation when uploading few enough uniforms
   7360     var view = miniTempWebGLFloatBuffers[16 * count];
   7361     // hoist the heap out of the loop for size and for pthreads+growth.
   7362     var heap = HEAPF32;
   7363     value = ((value) >> 2);
   7364     count *= 16;
   7365     for (var i = 0; i < count; i += 16) {
   7366       var dst = value + i;
   7367       view[i] = heap[dst];
   7368       view[i + 1] = heap[dst + 1];
   7369       view[i + 2] = heap[dst + 2];
   7370       view[i + 3] = heap[dst + 3];
   7371       view[i + 4] = heap[dst + 4];
   7372       view[i + 5] = heap[dst + 5];
   7373       view[i + 6] = heap[dst + 6];
   7374       view[i + 7] = heap[dst + 7];
   7375       view[i + 8] = heap[dst + 8];
   7376       view[i + 9] = heap[dst + 9];
   7377       view[i + 10] = heap[dst + 10];
   7378       view[i + 11] = heap[dst + 11];
   7379       view[i + 12] = heap[dst + 12];
   7380       view[i + 13] = heap[dst + 13];
   7381       view[i + 14] = heap[dst + 14];
   7382       view[i + 15] = heap[dst + 15];
   7383     }
   7384   } else {
   7385     var view = HEAPF32.subarray((((value) >> 2)), ((value + count * 64) >> 2));
   7386   }
   7387   GLctx.uniformMatrix4fv(webglGetUniformLocation(location), !!transpose, view);
   7388 };
   7389 
   7390 var _glUniformMatrix4fv = _emscripten_glUniformMatrix4fv;
   7391 
   7392 var _emscripten_glUseProgram = program => {
   7393   program = GL.programs[program];
   7394   GLctx.useProgram(program);
   7395   // Record the currently active program so that we can access the uniform
   7396   // mapping table of that program.
   7397   GLctx.currentProgram = program;
   7398 };
   7399 
   7400 var _glUseProgram = _emscripten_glUseProgram;
   7401 
   7402 var _emscripten_glVertexAttribPointer = (index, size, type, normalized, stride, ptr) => {
   7403   var cb = GL.currentContext.clientBuffers[index];
   7404   if (!GLctx.currentArrayBufferBinding) {
   7405     cb.size = size;
   7406     cb.type = type;
   7407     cb.normalized = normalized;
   7408     cb.stride = stride;
   7409     cb.ptr = ptr;
   7410     cb.clientside = true;
   7411     cb.vertexAttribPointerAdaptor = function(index, size, type, normalized, stride, ptr) {
   7412       this.vertexAttribPointer(index, size, type, normalized, stride, ptr);
   7413     };
   7414     return;
   7415   }
   7416   cb.clientside = false;
   7417   GLctx.vertexAttribPointer(index, size, type, !!normalized, stride, ptr);
   7418 };
   7419 
   7420 var _glVertexAttribPointer = _emscripten_glVertexAttribPointer;

   7426 function _mediapipe_find_canvas_event_target(canvasSelector) {
   7427   let target = findCanvasEventTarget(canvasSelector);
   7428   // WebGPU-on-worker uses this function to try to grab the canvas, but
   7429   // doesn't have a DOM element to find. So as a quick patch, if the default
   7430   // behavior is unsuccessful here then we try a webgpu canvas property
   7431   // which is set by the user directly on the Module, much like how our old
   7432   // pipeline used the Module.canvas property. See b/265271517 for details.
   7433   if (Module && !target) {
   7434     target = Module.canvasWebGpu;
   7435   }
   7436   return Emval.toHandle(target);
   7437 }

   7439 function _mediapipe_webgl_tex_image_drawable(drawableHandle) {
   7440   const drawable = Emval.toValue(drawableHandle);
   7441   GLctx.texImage2D(GLctx.TEXTURE_2D, 0, GLctx.RGBA, GLctx.RGBA, GLctx.UNSIGNED_BYTE, drawable);
   7442 }

   7444 var _random_get = (buffer, size) => randomFill(HEAPU8.subarray(buffer, buffer + size));

   7446 var _wgpuCommandEncoderBeginComputePass = (encoderPtr, descriptor) => {
   7447   var desc;
   7448   if (descriptor) {
   7449     desc = {
   7450       "label": WebGPU.makeStringFromOptionalStringView(descriptor + 4),
   7451       "timestampWrites": WebGPU.makePassTimestampWrites(HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (12)) >> 2)])
   7452     };
   7453   }
   7454   var commandEncoder = WebGPU.getJsObject(encoderPtr);
   7455   var ptr = _emwgpuCreateComputePassEncoder(0);
   7456   WebGPU.Internals.jsObjectInsert(ptr, commandEncoder.beginComputePass(desc));
   7457   return ptr;
   7458 };

   7460 var _wgpuCommandEncoderBeginRenderPass = (encoderPtr, descriptor) => {
   7461   function makeColorAttachment(caPtr) {
   7462     var viewPtr = HEAPU32[(((caPtr) + (4)) >> 2)];
   7463     if (viewPtr === 0) {
   7464       // Null `view` means no attachment in this slot.
   7465       return undefined;
   7466     }
   7467     var depthSlice = HEAPU32[(((caPtr) + (8)) >> 2)];
   7468     if (depthSlice == 4294967295) depthSlice = undefined;
   7469     return {
   7470       "view": WebGPU.getJsObject(viewPtr),
   7471       "depthSlice": depthSlice,
   7472       "resolveTarget": WebGPU.getJsObject(HEAPU32[(((caPtr) + (12)) >> 2)]),
   7473       "clearValue": WebGPU.makeColor(caPtr + 24),
   7474       "loadOp": WebGPU.LoadOp[HEAP32[(((caPtr) + (16)) >> 2)]],
   7475       "storeOp": WebGPU.StoreOp[HEAP32[(((caPtr) + (20)) >> 2)]]
   7476     };
   7477   }
   7478   function makeColorAttachments(count, caPtr) {
   7479     var attachments = [];
   7480     for (var i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
   7481       attachments.push(makeColorAttachment(caPtr + 56 * i));
   7482     }
   7483     return attachments;
   7484   }
   7485   function makeDepthStencilAttachment(dsaPtr) {
   7486     if (dsaPtr === 0) return undefined;
   7487     return {
   7488       "view": WebGPU.getJsObject(HEAPU32[(((dsaPtr) + (4)) >> 2)]),
   7489       "depthClearValue": HEAPF32[(((dsaPtr) + (16)) >> 2)],
   7490       "depthLoadOp": WebGPU.LoadOp[HEAP32[(((dsaPtr) + (8)) >> 2)]],
   7491       "depthStoreOp": WebGPU.StoreOp[HEAP32[(((dsaPtr) + (12)) >> 2)]],
   7492       "depthReadOnly": !!(HEAPU32[(((dsaPtr) + (20)) >> 2)]),
   7493       "stencilClearValue": HEAPU32[(((dsaPtr) + (32)) >> 2)],
   7494       "stencilLoadOp": WebGPU.LoadOp[HEAP32[(((dsaPtr) + (24)) >> 2)]],
   7495       "stencilStoreOp": WebGPU.StoreOp[HEAP32[(((dsaPtr) + (28)) >> 2)]],
   7496       "stencilReadOnly": !!(HEAPU32[(((dsaPtr) + (36)) >> 2)])
   7497     };
   7498   }
   7499   function makeRenderPassDescriptor(descriptor) {
   7500     var nextInChainPtr = HEAPU32[((descriptor) >> 2)];
   7501     var maxDrawCount = undefined;
   7502     if (nextInChainPtr !== 0) {
   7503       var sType = HEAP32[(((nextInChainPtr) + (4)) >> 2)];
   7504       var renderPassMaxDrawCount = nextInChainPtr;
   7505       // Note: The user could have passed a really huge value here, which is technically valid in
   7506       // C but will not be allowed by WebGPU in JS because of [EnforceRange]. We intentionally
   7507       // ignore that case because it's not useful - apps can just pick a smaller maxDrawCount.
   7508       maxDrawCount = readI53FromI64((renderPassMaxDrawCount) + (8));
   7509     }
   7510     var desc = {
   7511       "label": WebGPU.makeStringFromOptionalStringView(descriptor + 4),
   7512       "colorAttachments": makeColorAttachments(HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (12)) >> 2)], HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (16)) >> 2)]),
   7513       "depthStencilAttachment": makeDepthStencilAttachment(HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (20)) >> 2)]),
   7514       "occlusionQuerySet": WebGPU.getJsObject(HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (24)) >> 2)]),
   7515       "timestampWrites": WebGPU.makePassTimestampWrites(HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (28)) >> 2)]),
   7516       "maxDrawCount": maxDrawCount
   7517     };
   7518     return desc;
   7519   }
   7520   var desc = makeRenderPassDescriptor(descriptor);
   7521   var commandEncoder = WebGPU.getJsObject(encoderPtr);
   7522   var ptr = _emwgpuCreateRenderPassEncoder(0);
   7523   WebGPU.Internals.jsObjectInsert(ptr, commandEncoder.beginRenderPass(desc));
   7524   return ptr;
   7525 };

   7586 var _wgpuDeviceCreateBindGroup = (devicePtr, descriptor) => {
   7587   function makeEntry(entryPtr) {
   7588     var bufferPtr = HEAPU32[(((entryPtr) + (8)) >> 2)];
   7589     var samplerPtr = HEAPU32[(((entryPtr) + (32)) >> 2)];
   7590     var textureViewPtr = HEAPU32[(((entryPtr) + (36)) >> 2)];
   7591     var externalTexturePtr = 0;
   7592     WebGPU.iterateExtensions(entryPtr, {
   7593       14: ptr => {
   7594         externalTexturePtr = HEAPU32[(((ptr) + (8)) >> 2)];
   7595       }
   7596     });
   7597     var resource;
   7598     if (bufferPtr) {
   7599       // Note the sentinel UINT64_MAX will be read as -1.
   7600       var size = readI53FromI64((entryPtr) + (24));
   7601       if (size == -1) size = undefined;
   7602       resource = {
   7603         "buffer": WebGPU.getJsObject(bufferPtr),
   7604         "offset": readI53FromI64((entryPtr) + (16)),
   7605         "size": size
   7606       };
   7607     } else {
   7608       resource = WebGPU.getJsObject(samplerPtr || textureViewPtr || externalTexturePtr);
   7609     }
   7610     return {
   7611       "binding": HEAPU32[(((entryPtr) + (4)) >> 2)],
   7612       "resource": resource
   7613     };
   7614   }
   7615   function makeEntries(count, entriesPtrs) {
   7616     var entries = [];
   7617     for (var i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
   7618       entries.push(makeEntry(entriesPtrs + 40 * i));
   7619     }
   7620     return entries;
   7621   }
   7622   var desc = {
   7623     "label": WebGPU.makeStringFromOptionalStringView(descriptor + 4),
   7624     "layout": WebGPU.getJsObject(HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (12)) >> 2)]),
   7625     "entries": makeEntries(HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (16)) >> 2)], HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (20)) >> 2)])
   7626   };
   7627   var device = WebGPU.getJsObject(devicePtr);
   7628   var ptr = _emwgpuCreateBindGroup(0);
   7629   WebGPU.Internals.jsObjectInsert(ptr, device.createBindGroup(desc));
   7630   return ptr;
   7631 };
   7632 
   7633 var _wgpuDeviceCreateBindGroupLayout = (devicePtr, descriptor) => {
   7634   function makeBufferEntry(substructPtr) {
   7635     var typeInt = HEAPU32[(((substructPtr) + (4)) >> 2)];
   7636     if (!typeInt) return undefined;
   7637     return {
   7638       "type": WebGPU.BufferBindingType[typeInt],
   7639       "hasDynamicOffset": !!(HEAPU32[(((substructPtr) + (8)) >> 2)]),
   7640       "minBindingSize": readI53FromI64((substructPtr) + (16))
   7641     };
   7642   }
   7643   function makeSamplerEntry(substructPtr) {
   7644     var typeInt = HEAPU32[(((substructPtr) + (4)) >> 2)];
   7645     if (!typeInt) return undefined;
   7646     return {
   7647       "type": WebGPU.SamplerBindingType[typeInt]
   7648     };
   7649   }
   7650   function makeTextureEntry(substructPtr) {
   7651     var sampleTypeInt = HEAPU32[(((substructPtr) + (4)) >> 2)];
   7652     if (!sampleTypeInt) return undefined;
   7653     return {
   7654       "sampleType": WebGPU.TextureSampleType[sampleTypeInt],
   7655       "viewDimension": WebGPU.TextureViewDimension[HEAP32[(((substructPtr) + (8)) >> 2)]],
   7656       "multisampled": !!(HEAPU32[(((substructPtr) + (12)) >> 2)])
   7657     };
   7658   }
   7659   function makeStorageTextureEntry(substructPtr) {
   7660     var accessInt = HEAPU32[(((substructPtr) + (4)) >> 2)];
   7661     if (!accessInt) return undefined;
   7662     return {
   7663       "access": WebGPU.StorageTextureAccess[accessInt],
   7664       "format": WebGPU.TextureFormat[HEAP32[(((substructPtr) + (8)) >> 2)]],
   7665       "viewDimension": WebGPU.TextureViewDimension[HEAP32[(((substructPtr) + (12)) >> 2)]]
   7666     };
   7667   }
   7668   function makeEntry(entryPtr) {
   7669     var entry = {
   7670       "binding": HEAPU32[(((entryPtr) + (4)) >> 2)],
   7671       "visibility": HEAPU32[(((entryPtr) + (8)) >> 2)],
   7672       "buffer": makeBufferEntry(entryPtr + 24),
   7673       "sampler": makeSamplerEntry(entryPtr + 48),
   7674       "texture": makeTextureEntry(entryPtr + 56),
   7675       "storageTexture": makeStorageTextureEntry(entryPtr + 72)
   7676     };
   7677     WebGPU.iterateExtensions(entryPtr, {
   7678       13: ptr => {
   7679         entry["externalTexture"] = {};
   7680       }
   7681     });
   7682     return entry;
   7683   }
   7684   function makeEntries(count, entriesPtrs) {
   7685     var entries = [];
   7686     for (var i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
   7687       entries.push(makeEntry(entriesPtrs + 88 * i));
   7688     }
   7689     return entries;
   7690   }
   7691   var desc = {
   7692     "label": WebGPU.makeStringFromOptionalStringView(descriptor + 4),
   7693     "entries": makeEntries(HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (12)) >> 2)], HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (16)) >> 2)])
   7694   };
   7695   var device = WebGPU.getJsObject(devicePtr);
   7696   var ptr = _emwgpuCreateBindGroupLayout(0);
   7697   WebGPU.Internals.jsObjectInsert(ptr, device.createBindGroupLayout(desc));
   7698   return ptr;
   7699 };
   7700 
   7701 var _wgpuDeviceCreateCommandEncoder = (devicePtr, descriptor) => {

   7703   if (descriptor) {
   7704     desc = {

   7706     };
   7707   }
   7708   var device = WebGPU.getJsObject(devicePtr);

   7747 var _wgpuDeviceCreateSampler = (devicePtr, descriptor) => {

   7751       "label": WebGPU.makeStringFromOptionalStringView(descriptor + 4),
   7752       "addressModeU": WebGPU.AddressMode[HEAP32[(((descriptor) + (12)) >> 2)]],
   7753       "addressModeV": WebGPU.AddressMode[HEAP32[(((descriptor) + (16)) >> 2)]],
   7754       "addressModeW": WebGPU.AddressMode[HEAP32[(((descriptor) + (20)) >> 2)]],

   7756       "minFilter": WebGPU.FilterMode[HEAP32[(((descriptor) + (28)) >> 2)]],

   7760       "compare": WebGPU.CompareFunction[HEAP32[(((descriptor) + (44)) >> 2)]],

   7770 var _wgpuDeviceCreateTexture = (devicePtr, descriptor) => {
   7771   var nextInChainPtr = HEAPU32[((descriptor) >> 2)];
   7772   var textureBindingViewDimension;
   7773   if (nextInChainPtr !== 0) {
   7774     var sType = HEAP32[(((nextInChainPtr) + (4)) >> 2)];
   7775     var textureBindingViewDimensionDescriptor = nextInChainPtr;
   7776     textureBindingViewDimension = WebGPU.TextureViewDimension[HEAP32[(((textureBindingViewDimensionDescriptor) + (8)) >> 2)]];
   7777   }
   7778   var desc = {
   7779     "label": WebGPU.makeStringFromOptionalStringView(descriptor + 4),
   7780     "size": WebGPU.makeExtent3D(descriptor + 28),
   7781     "mipLevelCount": HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (44)) >> 2)],
   7782     "sampleCount": HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (48)) >> 2)],
   7783     "dimension": WebGPU.TextureDimension[HEAP32[(((descriptor) + (24)) >> 2)]],
   7784     "format": WebGPU.TextureFormat[HEAP32[(((descriptor) + (40)) >> 2)]],
   7785     "usage": HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (16)) >> 2)],
   7786     "textureBindingViewDimension": textureBindingViewDimension
   7787   };
   7788   var viewFormatCount = HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (52)) >> 2)];
   7789   if (viewFormatCount) {
   7790     var viewFormatsPtr = HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (56)) >> 2)];
   7791     // viewFormatsPtr pointer to an array of TextureFormat which is an enum of size uint32_t
   7792     desc["viewFormats"] = Array.from(HEAP32.subarray((((viewFormatsPtr) >> 2)), ((viewFormatsPtr + viewFormatCount * 4) >> 2)), format => WebGPU.TextureFormat[format]);
   7793   }
   7794   var device = WebGPU.getJsObject(devicePtr);
   7795   var ptr = _emwgpuCreateTexture(0);
   7796   WebGPU.Internals.jsObjectInsert(ptr, device.createTexture(desc));
   7797   return ptr;
   7798 };
   7799 
   7800 var _wgpuQueueSubmit = (queuePtr, commandCount, commands) => {
   7801   var queue = WebGPU.getJsObject(queuePtr);
   7802   var cmds = Array.from(HEAP32.subarray((((commands) >> 2)), ((commands + commandCount * 4) >> 2)), id => WebGPU.getJsObject(id));
   7803   queue.submit(cmds);
   7804 };
   7805 
   7806 function _wgpuQueueWriteBuffer(queuePtr, bufferPtr, bufferOffset_low, bufferOffset_high, data, size) {
   7807   var bufferOffset = convertI32PairToI53Checked(bufferOffset_low, bufferOffset_high);
   7808   var queue = WebGPU.getJsObject(queuePtr);
   7809   var buffer = WebGPU.getJsObject(bufferPtr);
   7810   // There is a size limitation for ArrayBufferView. Work around by passing in a subarray
   7811   // instead of the whole heap. crbug.com/1201109
   7812   var subarray = HEAPU8.subarray(data, data + size);
   7813   queue.writeBuffer(buffer, bufferOffset, subarray, 0, size);
   7814 }
   7815 
   7816 var _wgpuQueueWriteTexture = (queuePtr, destinationPtr, data, dataSize, dataLayoutPtr, writeSizePtr) => {
   7817   var queue = WebGPU.getJsObject(queuePtr);
   7818   var destination = WebGPU.makeTexelCopyTextureInfo(destinationPtr);
   7819   var dataLayout = WebGPU.makeTexelCopyBufferLayout(dataLayoutPtr);
   7820   var writeSize = WebGPU.makeExtent3D(writeSizePtr);
   7821   // This subarray isn't strictly necessary, but helps work around an issue
   7822   // where Chromium makes a copy of the entire heap. crbug.com/1134457
   7823   var subarray = HEAPU8.subarray(data, data + dataSize);
   7824   queue.writeTexture(destination, subarray, dataLayout, writeSize);
   7825 };

   7827 var _wgpuRenderPassEncoderDraw = (passPtr, vertexCount, instanceCount, firstVertex, firstInstance) => {
   7828   firstVertex >>>= 0;
   7829   firstInstance >>>= 0;
   7830   var pass = WebGPU.getJsObject(passPtr);
   7831   pass.draw(vertexCount, instanceCount, firstVertex, firstInstance);
   7832 };
   7833 
   7834 var _wgpuRenderPassEncoderEnd = encoderPtr => {

   7836   encoder.end();
   7837 };
   7838 
   7839 var _wgpuRenderPassEncoderSetBindGroup = (passPtr, groupIndex, groupPtr, dynamicOffsetCount, dynamicOffsetsPtr) => {
   7840   var pass = WebGPU.getJsObject(passPtr);
   7841   var group = WebGPU.getJsObject(groupPtr);
   7842   if (dynamicOffsetCount == 0) {
   7843     pass.setBindGroup(groupIndex, group);
   7844   } else {
   7845     pass.setBindGroup(groupIndex, group, HEAPU32, ((dynamicOffsetsPtr) >> 2), dynamicOffsetCount);
   7846   }
   7847 };
   7848 
   7849 var _wgpuRenderPassEncoderSetPipeline = (passPtr, pipelinePtr) => {
   7850   var pass = WebGPU.getJsObject(passPtr);
   7851   var pipeline = WebGPU.getJsObject(pipelinePtr);
   7852   pass.setPipeline(pipeline);
   7853 };

   7855 var _wgpuRenderPipelineGetBindGroupLayout = (pipelinePtr, groupIndex) => {
   7856   var pipeline = WebGPU.getJsObject(pipelinePtr);
   7857   var ptr = _emwgpuCreateBindGroupLayout(0);
   7858   WebGPU.Internals.jsObjectInsert(ptr, pipeline.getBindGroupLayout(groupIndex));
   7859   return ptr;
   7860 };
   7861 
   7862 var _wgpuTextureCreateView = (texturePtr, descriptor) => {
   7863   var desc;
   7864   if (descriptor) {
   7865     var swizzle;
   7866     var nextInChainPtr = HEAPU32[((descriptor) >> 2)];
   7867     if (nextInChainPtr !== 0) {
   7868       var sType = HEAP32[(((nextInChainPtr) + (4)) >> 2)];
   7869       var swizzleDescriptor = nextInChainPtr;
   7870       var swizzlePtr = swizzleDescriptor + 8;
   7871       var r = WebGPU.ComponentSwizzle[HEAP32[((swizzlePtr) >> 2)]] || "r";
   7872       var g = WebGPU.ComponentSwizzle[HEAP32[(((swizzlePtr) + (4)) >> 2)]] || "g";
   7873       var b = WebGPU.ComponentSwizzle[HEAP32[(((swizzlePtr) + (8)) >> 2)]] || "b";
   7874       var a = WebGPU.ComponentSwizzle[HEAP32[(((swizzlePtr) + (12)) >> 2)]] || "a";
   7875       swizzle = `${r}${g}${b}${a}`;
   7876     }
   7877     var mipLevelCount = HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (24)) >> 2)];
   7878     var arrayLayerCount = HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (32)) >> 2)];
   7879     desc = {
   7880       "label": WebGPU.makeStringFromOptionalStringView(descriptor + 4),
   7881       "format": WebGPU.TextureFormat[HEAP32[(((descriptor) + (12)) >> 2)]],
   7882       "dimension": WebGPU.TextureViewDimension[HEAP32[(((descriptor) + (16)) >> 2)]],
   7883       "baseMipLevel": HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (20)) >> 2)],
   7884       "mipLevelCount": mipLevelCount === 4294967295 ? undefined : mipLevelCount,
   7885       "baseArrayLayer": HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (28)) >> 2)],
   7886       "arrayLayerCount": arrayLayerCount === 4294967295 ? undefined : arrayLayerCount,
   7887       "aspect": WebGPU.TextureAspect[HEAP32[(((descriptor) + (36)) >> 2)]],
   7888       "usage": HEAPU32[(((descriptor) + (40)) >> 2)],
   7889       "swizzle": swizzle
   7890     };
   7891   }
   7892   var texture = WebGPU.getJsObject(texturePtr);
   7893   var ptr = _emwgpuCreateTextureView(0);
   7894   WebGPU.Internals.jsObjectInsert(ptr, texture.createView(desc));
   7895   return ptr;
   7896 };

   7908 var getCFunc = ident => {

   7914 var writeArrayToMemory = (array, buffer) => {
   7915   HEAP8.set(array, buffer);
   7916 };
   7917 
   7918 /**
   7919    * @param {string|null=} returnType
   7920    * @param {Array=} argTypes
   7921    * @param {Array=} args
   7922    * @param {Object=} opts
   7923    */ var ccall = (ident, returnType, argTypes, args, opts) => {
   7924   // For fast lookup of conversion functions
   7925   var toC = {
   7926     "string": str => {
   7927       var ret = 0;
   7928       if (str !== null && str !== undefined && str !== 0) {
   7929         // null string
   7930         ret = stringToUTF8OnStack(str);
   7931       }
   7932       return ret;
   7933     },
   7934     "array": arr => {
   7935       var ret = stackAlloc(arr.length);
   7936       writeArrayToMemory(arr, ret);
   7937       return ret;
   7938     }
   7939   };
   7940   function convertReturnValue(ret) {
   7941     if (returnType === "string") {
   7942       return UTF8ToString(ret);
   7943     }
   7944     if (returnType === "boolean") return Boolean(ret);
   7945     return ret;
   7946   }
   7947   var func = getCFunc(ident);
   7948   var cArgs = [];
   7949   var stack = 0;
   7950   if (args) {
   7951     for (var i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
   7952       var converter = toC[argTypes[i]];
   7953       if (converter) {
   7954         if (stack === 0) stack = stackSave();
   7955         cArgs[i] = converter(args[i]);
   7956       } else {
   7957         cArgs[i] = args[i];
   7958       }
   7959     }
   7960   }
   7961   var ret = func(...cArgs);
   7962   function onDone(ret) {
   7963     if (stack !== 0) stackRestore(stack);
   7964     return convertReturnValue(ret);
   7965   }
   7966   ret = onDone(ret);
   7967   return ret;
   7968 };

   8113 function BeginGlQueryTiming(calc_name, num_repetitions) {
   8114   const gl = Module.canvas.getContext("webgl2");
   8115   const query = gl.createQuery();
   8116   Module.WEBGL_SHADER_CALC_METRICS = Module.WEBGL_SHADER_CALC_METRICS || {};
   8117   Module.WEBGL_SHADER_CALC_METRICS[UTF8ToString(calc_name)] = {
   8118     query,
   8119     repetitions: num_repetitions
   8120   };
   8121   Module.WEBGL_QUERY_TIMER_EXT = Module.WEBGL_QUERY_TIMER_EXT || gl.getExtension("EXT_disjoint_timer_query_webgl2");
   8122   gl.beginQuery(Module.WEBGL_QUERY_TIMER_EXT.TIME_ELAPSED_EXT, query);
   8123 }
   8124

   8126   const gl = Module.canvas.getContext("webgl2");
   8127   gl.endQuery(Module.WEBGL_QUERY_TIMER_EXT.TIME_ELAPSED_EXT, Module.WEBGL_SHADER_CALC_METRICS[UTF8ToString(calc_name)].query);
   8128 }

   8312 function __asyncjs__mediapipe_map_buffer_jspi(buffer_handle, data) {
   8313   return Asyncify.handleAsync(async () => {
   8314     const buffer = WebGPU.getJsObject(buffer_handle);
   8315     if ("mapSync" in buffer) {
   8316       buffer.mapSync(GPUMapMode.READ);
   8317     } else {
   8318       await buffer.mapAsync(GPUMapMode.READ);
   8319     }
   8320     const mapped = buffer.getMappedRange();
   8321     HEAPU8.set(new Uint8Array(mapped), data);
   8322     buffer.unmap();
   8323   });
   8324 }

   8345 function custom_emscripten_dbgn(str, len) {
   8346   if (typeof (dbg) !== "undefined") {
   8347     dbg(UTF8ToString(str, len));
   8348   } else {
   8349     if (typeof (custom_dbg) === "undefined") {
   8350       function custom_dbg(text) {
   8351         console.warn.apply(console, arguments);
   8352       }
   8353     }
   8354     custom_dbg(UTF8ToString(str, len));
   8355   }
   8356 }

   8758 function run() {
   8759   if (runDependencies > 0) {
   8760     dependenciesFulfilled = run;
   8761     return;
   8762   }
   8763   preRun();
   8764   // a preRun added a dependency, run will be called later
   8765   if (runDependencies > 0) {
   8766     dependenciesFulfilled = run;
   8767     return;
   8768   }
   8769   function doRun() {
   8770     // run may have just been called through dependencies being fulfilled just in this very frame,
   8771     // or while the async setStatus time below was happening
   8772     Module["calledRun"] = true;
   8773     if (ABORT) return;
   8774     initRuntime();
   8775     readyPromiseResolve?.(Module);
   8776     Module["onRuntimeInitialized"]?.();
   8777     postRun();
   8778   }
   8779   if (Module["setStatus"]) {
   8780     Module["setStatus"]("Running...");
   8781     setTimeout(() => {
   8782       setTimeout(() => Module["setStatus"](""), 1);
   8783       doRun();
   8784     }, 1);
   8785   } else {
   8786     doRun();
   8787   }
   8788 }
